# Chakkanipāta (AN 6)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 6 (Chakkanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 3
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 227]*

bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^227-1]. Evaṃ kho Doṇa brāhmaṇo devasamo hoti.

5. Kathañ ca Doṇa brāhmaṇo mariyādo hoti? Idha Doṇa brāhmaṇo ubhato sujāto hoti mātito ca pitito ca saṃsuddhagahaṇiko[^227-2] yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhitto anupakkuṭṭho[^227-3] jātivādena. So aṭṭhacattāḷīsavassāni[^227-4] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^227-5] carati[^227-6] mante adhīyamāno, aṭṭhacattāḷīsavassani[^227-4] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^227-5] caritvā mante adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyesati dhammen'eva no adhammena. Tattha ca[^227-7] Doṇa ko dhammo? Neva kasiyā na vaṇijjāya[^227-8] na gorakkhena na issatthena na rājaporisena na sippaññatarena, kevalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimaññamāno. So ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ nīyādetvā[^227-9] dāraṃ pariyesati dhammen'eva no adhammena. Tattha ca Doṇa ko dhammo? Neva kayena na vikkayena, brāhmaṇiṃ yeva udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ[^227-10]. So brāhmaṇiṃ yeva gacchati, na khattiyiṃ na vessiṃ na suddiṃ na caṇḍāliṃ na nesādiṃ na veṇiṃ na rathakāriṃ na pukkusiṃ gacchati, na gabbhiniṃ gacchati, na pāyamānaṃ gacchati, na anutuniṃ gacchati. Kasmā ca Doṇa brāhmaṇo na gabbhiniṃ gacchati? Sace Doṇa brāhmaṇo gabbhiniṃ gacchati . . . pe[^227-11] . . . pajatthā'va[^227-12] brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hoti. So methunaṃ[^227-13] uppādetvā tam eva puttassādaṃ nikāmayamāno kuṭumbiṃ ajjhāvasati, na agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati. Yāva porāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādā[^227-14], tattha tiṭṭhati taṃ na vītikkamati. Yāva porāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādā[^227-14], tattha[^227-15] brāhmaṇo ṭhito taṃ na vītikkamatī ti kho[^227-16] Doṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇo[^227-17] mariyādo ti vuccati. Evaṃ kho Doṇa brāhmaṇo mariyādo hoti.

6. Kathañ ca Doṇa brāhmaṇo sambhinnamariyādo hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 228]*

Idha Doṇa brāhmaṇo ubhato sujāto hoti . . . pe[^228-1] . . . anupakkuṭṭho[^228-2] jātivādena. So aṭṭhacattāḷīsavassāni[^228-3] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^228-4] carati mante adhīyamāno, aṭṭhacattāḷīsavassāni[^228-3] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^228-4] caritvā mante adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyesati dhammen'eva no adhammena. Tattha ca[^228-5] Doṇa ko dhammo? Neva kasiyā na vaṇijjāya[^228-6] na gorakkhena na issatthena[^228-7] na rājaporisena na sippaññatarena, kevalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimaññamāno. So ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ nīyādetvā[^228-8] dāraṃ pariyesati dhammena pi adhammena[^228-9] pi[^228-9] kayena pi vikkayena pi brāhmaṇim[^228-10] pi[^228-10] udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ[^228-11]. So brāhmaṇim pi gacchati, khattiyim pi gacchati, vessim pi gacchati, suddim pi gacchati, caṇḍālim pi gacchati, nesādim[^228-12] pi gacchati, veṇim pi gacchati, rathakārim pi gacchati, pukkusim pi gacchati, gabbhinim pi gacchati, pāyamānam pi gacchati, utunim pi gacchati, anutunim pi gacchati. Tassa[^228-13] sā hoti brāhmaṇī kāmatthā pi davatthā pi ratatthā[^228-14] pi, pajatthā pi brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hoti. Yāva porāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādā[^228-15], tattha na tiṭṭhati taṃ vītikkamati. Yāva porāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādā[^228-15], tattha brāhmaṇo na ṭhito[^228-16] taṃ vītikkamatī ti kho[^228-17] Doṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇo sambhinnamariyādo ti vuccati. Evaṃ kho Doṇa brāhmaṇo sambhinnamariyādo hoti.

7. Kathañ ca Doṇa brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇacaṇḍālo hoti? Idha Doṇa brāhmaṇo ubhato sujāto hoti mātito ca pitito ca saṃsuddhagahaṇiko[^228-18] yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhitto anupakkuṭṭho[^228-2] jātivādena. So aṭṭhacattāḷīsavas-


<!-- PTS Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 229]*

sāni[^229-1] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^229-2] carati mante adhīyamāno, aṭṭhacattāḷīsavassāni[^229-1] komārabrahmacariyaṃ[^229-2] caritvā mante adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyesati dhammena pi adhammena pi kasiyā pi vaṇijjāya pi gorakkhena pi issatthena pi rājaporisena pi sippaññatarena pi kevalaṃ pi bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimaññamāno. So ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ nīyādetvā[^229-3] dāraṃ pariyesati dhammena pi adhammena pi kayena pi vikkayena pi udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ[^229-4]. So brāhmaṇim pi gacchati, khattiyim pi gacchati, vessim pi gacchati, suddim pi gacchati, caṇḍālim pi gacchati, nesādim pi gacchati, veṇim pi gacchati, rathakārim pi gacchati, pukkusim pi gacchati, gabbhinim pi gacchati, pāyamānam pi gacchati, utunim pi gacchati, anutunim pi gacchati. Tassa sā hoti brāhmaṇī kāmatthā pi davatthā pi ratatthā[^229-5] pi[^229-5], pajatthā[^229-6] pi[^229-6] brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hoti. So sabbakammehi jīvitaṃ kappeti. Tam enaṃ brāhmaṇā evam āhaṃsu ‘kasmā bhavaṃ brāhmaṇo paṭijānamāno sabbakammehi jīvitaṃ kappetī'; ti? So evam āha ‘seyyathā pi bho aggi[^229-7] sucim pi dahati asucim pi dahati, na ca tena aggi[^229-7] upalippati[^229-8], evam eva kho bho sabbakammehi ce pi brāhmaṇo jīvitaṃ kappeti, na ca tena brāhmaṇo upalippatī[^229-9]'; ti. Sabbakammehi jīvitaṃ kappetī ti kho Doṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇacaṇḍālo ti vuccati. Evaṃ kho Doṇa brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇacaṇḍālo hoti.

8. Ye kho te Doṇa brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā[^229-10] isayo[^229-11] mantānaṃ kattāro mantānaṃ pavattāro yesañ c'; idaṃ etarahi brāhmaṇā porāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ tad anugāyanti tad anubhāsanti bhāsitam anubhāsanti vācitam anuvācenti, seyyathīdaṃ Aṭṭhako Vāmako Vāmadevo Vessāmitto Yamadaggi[^229-12] Aṅgīraso[^229-13] Bhāradvājo Vā-


<!-- PTS Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 230]*

seṭṭho Kassapo Bhagu, tyassu 'me[^230-1] pañca brāhmaṇe paññāpenti: brahmasamaṃ devasamaṃ mariyādaṃ sambhinnamariyādaṃ brāhmaṇacaṇḍālaṃ yeva pañcamam. Tesaṃ tvaṃ Doṇa katamo ti? ‘Evaṃ sante mayaṃ[^230-2] bho Gotama brāhmaṇacaṇḍālam pi[^230-3] na pūrema. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . . pe[^230-4] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. CXCIII.

1. Atha kho Saṅgāravo[^230-5] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Saṅgāravo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Ko nu kho bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo, yen'; ekadā[^230-6] dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā[^230-7]? Ko pana bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo, yen'; ekadā[^230-8] dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā ti?

3. Yasmiṃ brāhmaṇa samaye[^230-9] kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na[^230-9] ppajānāti, attattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajjānāti na passati, parattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajjānāti na passati, ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajjānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto saṃsaṭṭho lākhāya[^230-10] vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā[^230-11] vā mañjiṭṭhāya[^230-12] vā, tattha cakkhumā puriso sa-


<!-- PTS Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 231]*

kaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathā bhūtaṃ na ppajāneyya na passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa[^231-1] yasmiṃ samaye kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ[^231-2] yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattham pi . . . pe[^231-3] . . . parattham pi . . . pe[^231-4] . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā.

4. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena[^231-5] cetasā viharati vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca vyāpādassa[^231-6] nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattham[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-8] . . . parattham[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-9] . . . ubhayattham[^231-10] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā[^231-11] pi[^231-11] brāhmaṇa udapatto agginā santatto ukkaṭṭhito[^231-12] ussadakajāto[^231-13], tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ na ppajāneyya na[^231-11] passeyya[^231-11], evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca[^231-14] vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attatthaṃ[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-8] . . . parattham[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-9] . . . ubhayattham[^231-10] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā.

5. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca[^231-11] thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattham[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-8] . . . parattham[^231-7] pi[^231-7] . . . pe[^231-9] . . . ubhayattham[^231-10] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhū-

##### *[PTS Page 232]*

taṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto sevālapaṇakapariyonaddho, tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ na ppajāneyya na[^232-1] passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca[^232-1] thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti[^232-2], attattham[^232-3] pi[^232-3] . . . pe[^232-4] . . . parattham[^232-3] pi[^232-3] . . . pe[^232-5] . . . ubhayattham[^232-6] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā.

6. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti[^232-7], attattham[^232-3] pi[^232-3] . . . pe[^232-4] . . . parattham[^232-3] pi[^232-3] . . . pe[^232-5] . . . ubhayattham[^232-6] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto vāterito calito bhanto ūmijāto[^232-8], tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ na ppajāneyya[^232-9] na passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattham[^232-10] pi[^232-10] . . . pe[^232-4] . . . parattham[^232-10] pi[^232-10] . . . pe[^232-5] . . . ubhayattham[^232-11] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti[^232-12] na passati, dīgharat-


<!-- PTS Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 233]*

taṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā.

7. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca[^233-1] vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattam[^233-2] pi[^233-2] . . . pe[^233-3] . . . parattham[^233-4] pi[^233-4] . . . pe[^233-5] . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti[^233-6] na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto āvilo luḷito kalalībhūto[^233-7] andhakāre nikkhitto, tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ na ppajāneyya[^233-8] na passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca[^233-1] vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, attattham[^233-2] pi[^233-2] . . . pe[^233-9] . . . parattham[^233-4] pi[^233-4] . . . pe[^233-5] . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti[^233-6] na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā.

8. Yasmiñ ca kho[^233-10] brāhmaṇa samaye na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati na kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^233-11], attattham[^233-12] pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^233-11] passati, parattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^233-11] passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto asaṃsaṭṭho lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā[^233-13] vā[^233-13]


<!-- PTS Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 234]*

mañjiṭṭhāya[^234-1] vā, tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ pajāneyya[^234-2] passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na[^234-3] kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^234-4] na kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attattham pi[^234-5] . . . parattham[^234-5] . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā.

9. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^234-6] na vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^234-7], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto agginā asantatto anukkaṭṭhito[^234-8] anussadakajāto[^234-9], tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ pajāṇeyya[^234-10] passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^234-4] na vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^234-7], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā.

10. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^234-11] na thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^234-7], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pa-

##### *[PTS Page 235]*

geva sajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto na sevālapaṇakapariyonaddho, tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ pajāneyya[^235-1] passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena[^235-2] cetasā viharati[^235-3] na thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^235-4], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā.

11. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^235-5] na uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^235-4], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto na vāterito na calito na bhanto na ūmijāto[^235-6], tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ pajāneyya passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati[^235-7] na uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^235-8], attattham pi . . . parattham pi . . . ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā.

12. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati na vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca[^235-9] vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pa-


<!-- PTS Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 236]*

jānāti[^236-1], attattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, parattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa udapatto accho vippasanno anāvilo āloke nikkhitto, tattha cakkhumā puriso sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno yathābhūtaṃ pajāneyya[^236-2] passeyya, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yasmiṃ samaye na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati na vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^236-3] attattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, parattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, ubhayattham pi tasmiṃ samaye yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yen'; ekadā[^236-4] dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatā pi mantā na ppaṭibhanti, pageva asajjhāyakatā. Ayaṃ pana brāhmaṇa hetu ayaṃ paccayo, yen'; ekadā[^236-4] dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatā pi mantā paṭibhanti, pageva sajjhāyakatā ti[^236-5].

13. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . . pe[^236-6] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. CXCIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Tena kho pana samayena Kāraṇapālī[^236-7] brāhmaṇo Licchavīnaṃ kammantaṃ kāreti[^236-8]. Addasā kho Kāraṇapālī brāhmaṇo Piṅgiyāniṃ[^236-9] brāhmaṇaṃ dūrato


<!-- PTS Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 237]*

'va āgacchantaṃ, disvā[^237-1] Piṅgiyāniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ etad avoca: ‘Handa kuto nu bhavaṃ Piṅgiyānī āgacchati divādivassā' ti? ‘Ito 'haṃ[^237-2] bho āgacchāmi samaṇassa Gotamassa santikā'; ti[^237-3]. ‘Taṃ[^237-4] kiṃ maññati[^237-5] bhavaṃ Piṅgiyānī samaṇassa Gotamassa paññāveyyattiyaṃ, paṇḍito maññe'; ti? ‘Ko cāhaṃ bho, ko ca samaṇassa Gotamassa paññāveyyattiyaṃ jānissāmi[^237-6]? So pi nūn'assa tādiso 'va, yo samaṇassa Gotamassa paññāveyyattiyaṃ jāneyyā[^237-6]'; ti. ‘Uḷārāya khalu bhavaṃ Piṅgiyānī samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ pasaṃsāya pasaṃsatī'; ti. ‘Ko cāhaṃ[^237-7] bho, ko ca samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ pasaṃsissāmi? Pasatthapasattho[^237-8] 'va[^237-9] so bhavaṃ Gotamo seṭṭho devamanussānan'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana bhavaṃ Piṅgiyānī atthavasaṃ[^237-10] sampassamāno samaṇe Gotame[^237-11] evaṃ abhippasanno'; ti? ‘Seyyathā pi bho puriso aggarasaparititto na aññesaṃ hīnānaṃ rasānaṃ piheti, evam eva kho bho yato yato[^237-12] tassa bhoto[^237-13] Gotamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti[^237-14] yadi suttaso yadi geyyaso yadi veyyākaraṇaso yadi abbhutadhammaso, tato tato[^237-4] na aññesaṃ puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇappavādānaṃ piheti. Seyyathā pi bho puriso jighacchādubbalyapareto[^237-15] madhupiṇḍikaṃ adhigaccheyya, so yato yato sāyetha, labhat'eva[^237-16] sādu rasaṃ asecanakaṃ[^237-17], evam eva kho bho yato yato tassa bhoto[^237-18] Gotamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti[^237-14] yadi suttaso yadi geyyaso yadi veyyākaraṇaso yadi abbhutadhammaso, tato tato labhat'eva[^237-16] attamanataṃ[^237-19] labhati[^237-20] cetaso pasādaṃ. Seyyathā pi bho puriso candanaghaṭikaṃ adhigaccheyya haricandanassa vā lohitacandanassa[^237-21] vā[^237-21], so yato yato ghāyetha[^237-22] yadi mūlato yadi majjhato yadi aggato, adhigac-


<!-- PTS Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 238]*

chat'eva surabhigandhaṃ asecanakaṃ[^238-1], evam eva kho bho yato yato tassa bhoto Gotamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti yadi suttaso yadi geyyaso yadi veyyākaraṇaso yadi abbhutadhammaso, tato tato adhigacchati pāmujjaṃ adhigacchati somanassaṃ. Seyyathā pi bho puriso ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno, tassa kusalo bhisakko ṭhānaso ābādhaṃ nīhareyya, evam eva kho bho yato yato tassa bhoto Gotamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti yadi suttaso yadi geyyaso yadi veyyākaraṇaso yadi abbhutadhammaso, tato tato sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā abbhatthaṃ[^238-2] gacchanti. Seyyathā pi bho pokkharaṇī acchodakā[^238-3] sātodakā[^238-3] sītodakā[^238-3] setodakā[^238-4] supatiṭṭhā ramaṇīyā[^238-5], atha puriso āgaccheyya ghammābhitatto ghammapareto kilanto tasito pipāsito, so taṃ pokkharaṇiṃ ogāhetvā nhātvā[^238-6] ca pivitvā ca sabbadarathakilamathapariḷāhaṃ paṭippassambheyya, evam eva kho bho yato yato tassa bhoto Gotamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti yadi suttaso yadi geyyaso yadi veyyākaraṇaso yadi abbhutadhammaso, tato tato sabbadarathakilamathapariḷāhā paṭippassambhantī'; ti.

2. Evaṃ vutte Karaṇapālī brāhmaṇo uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇajānumaṇḍalaṃ paṭhaviyaṃ[^238-7] nihantvā yena Bhagavā ten'; añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānesi:

<p align="center">Namo tassa Bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


<p align="center">Namo tassa Bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


<p align="center">Namo tassa Bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa[^238-8].</p>


‘Abhikkantaṃ bho Piṅgiyāni, abhikkantaṃ bho Piṅgiyāni. Seyyathā pi bho Piṅgiyāni nikkujjitaṃ vā ukkujjeyya, paṭicchannaṃ[^238-9] vā vivareyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkheyya,


<!-- PTS Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 239]*

andhakāre vā telappajjotaṃ dhāreyya <<cakkhumanto rūpāni dakkhantī>> ti, evam eva bhotā Piṅgiyaninā anekapariyāyena dhammo pakāsito. Esāhaṃ[^239-1] bho[^239-2] Piṅgiyāni taṃ bhavantaṃ[^239-3] Gotamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ ca bhikkhusaṅghañ ca, upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Piṅgiyānī dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. CXCV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Tena kho pana samayena pañcamattāni Licchavisatāni Bhagavantaṃ payirupāsanti. App ekacce Licchavi nīlā honti nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṃkārā, app ekacce Licchavī pītā honti pītavaṇṇā pītavatthā pītālaṃkārā, app ekacce Licchavī lohitakā honti lohitakavaṇṇā lohitakavatthā lohitakālaṃkārā, app ekacce Licchavī odātā honti odātavaṇṇā odātavatthā odātālaṃkārā. Tyassu[^239-4] 'daṃ Bhagavā atirocati vaṇṇena c'eva yasasā ca. Atha kho Piṅgiyānī[^239-5] brāhmaṇo uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā ten'; añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: ‘Paṭibhāti maṃ Bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ Sugatā'; ti. ‘Paṭibhātu taṃ[^239-6] Piṅgiyānī'; ti Bhagavā avoca. Atha kho Piṅgiyānī brāhmaṇo Bhagavato sammukhā sāruppāya[^239-7] gāthāya abhitthavi: Padumaṃ[^239-8] yathā kokanadaṃ[^239-9] sugandhaṃ pāto siyā phullam avītagandhaṃ[^239-10] Aṅgīrasaṃ passa virocamānaṃ tapantam ādiccam iv' antalikkhe ti. Atha kho te Licchavī pañcahi uttarāsaṅgasatehi Piṅgiyāniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ acchādesuṃ. Atha kho Piṅgiyānī brāhmaṇo tehi pañcahi uttarāsaṅgasatehi Bhagavantaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 240]*

acchādesi. Atha kho Bhagavā te[^240-1] Licchavī etad avoca:--

2. Pañcannaṃ Licchavī ratanānaṃ pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Katamesaṃ pañcannaṃ.

3. Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Tathāgatappaveditassa dhammavinayassa desetā[^240-2] puggalo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Tathāgatappaveditassa dhammavinayassa[^240-3] desitassa viññātā puggalo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Tathāgatappaveditassa dhammavinayassa desitassa[^240-4] viññātā[^240-5] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno puggalo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Kataññukatavedī puggalo dullabho lokasmiṃ. Imesaṃ kho Licchavī pañcannaṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmin ti. CXCVI.

1. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato pañca mahāsupinā pāturahesuṃ. Katame pañca?

2. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ mahāpaṭhavī[^240-6] mahāsayanaṃ[^240-6] ahosi, Himavā pabbatarājā bimbohanaṃ ahosi, puratthime samudde vāmo hattho ohito ahosi, pacchime samudde dakkhiṇo hattho ohito ahosi, dakkhiṇe[^240-7] samudde[^240-7] ubho pādā ohitā ahesuṃ. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ paṭhamo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato tiriyā nāma tiṇajāti nābhiyā uggantvā[^240-8] nabhaṃ āhacca[^240-9] ṭhitā[^240-9] ahosi. Tathāgatassa


<!-- PTS Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 241]*

bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ dutiyo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato setā[^241-1] kimī kaṇhasīsā pādehi ussakkitvā[^241-2] yāva[^241-1] jānumaṇḍalā paṭicchādesum. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ tatiyo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato cattāro sakuṇā nānāvaṇṇā catūhi disāhi[^241-3] āgantvā pādamūle[^241-4] nipatitvā[^241-4] sabbasetā sampajjiṃsu. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ catuttho mahāsupino pāturahosi.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato arahaṃ sammāsambuddho pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddho bodhisatto 'va samāno mahato mīḷhapabbatassa uparūpari caṅkamati alippamāno[^241-5] mīḷhena. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ pañcamo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

7. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato ayaṃ mahāpaṭhavī mahāsayanaṃ ahosi, Himavā pabbatarājā bimbohanaṃ ahosi, puratthime samudde vāmo hattho ohito ahosi, pacchime samudde dakkhiṇo hattho ohito ahosi, dakkhiṇe samudde ubho padā ohitā ahesuṃ, Tathāgatena bhikkhave arahatā sammāsambuddhena anuttarā sammāsambodhi abhisambuddhā[^241-4]. Tassa[^241-4] abhisambodhāya[^241-6] ayaṃ paṭhamo mahāsupino pāturahosi.


<!-- PTS Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 242]*

8. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato tiriyā nāma tiṇajāti nābhiyā uggantvā nabhaṃ āhacca ṭhitā ahosi, Tathāgatena bhikkhave arahatā sammāsambuddhena ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo abhisambujjhitvā yāva devamanussehi suppakāsito. Tassa abhisambodhāya[^242-1] ayaṃ dutiyo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

9. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato setā kimī kaṇhasīsā pādehi ussakkitvā yāva jānumaṇḍalā paṭicchādesuṃ, bahū bhikkhave gihī odātavasanā Tathāgataṃ pāṇupetaṃ[^242-2] saraṇaṃ gatā. Tassa abhisambodhāya ayaṃ tatiyo mahāsupino pāturahosi.

10. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'eva sato cattāro sakuṇā nānāvaṇṇā catūhi disāhi āgantvā pādamūle nipatitvā sabbasetā sampajjiṃsu, cattāro 'me bhikkhave vaṇṇā khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā, te Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ sacchikaronti. Tassa abhisambodhāya ayaṃ catuttho mahāsupino pāturahosi.

11. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato arahaṃ sammāsambuddho pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddho bodhisatto 'va samāno mahato mīḷhapabbatassa uparūpari caṅkamati alippamāno[^242-3] mīḷhena, lābhī bhikkhave Tathāgato cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ, tattha[^242-4] ca[^242-5] Tathāgato agadhito[^242-6] amucchito anajjhopanno[^242-7] ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati. Tassa abhisambodhāya ayaṃ pañcamo mahasupino pāturahosi. Tathāgatassa bhikkhave arahato sammāsambuddhassa pubb'eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass' eva sato ime pañca mahāsupinā pāturahesun ti.


<!-- PTS Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 243]*

CXCVII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave vassassa antarāyā, yaṃ nemittā[^243-1] na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu[^243-2] na kkhamati[^243-3]. Katame pañca?

2. Upari bhikkhave ākāse tejodhātu pakuppati, tena uppannā meghā paṭivigacchanti[^243-4]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo vassassa antarāyo, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamati.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave upari ākāse vāyodhātu pakuppati, tena uppannā meghā paṭivigacchanti[^243-4]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo vassassa antarāyo, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Rāhu asurindo[^243-5] pāṇinā udakaṃ paṭicchitvā[^243-6] mahāsamudde chaḍḍeti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo vassassa antarāyo, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamati.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave vassavalāhakā devā[^243-7] pamattā honti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho vassassa antarāyo, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamati.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave manussā adhammikā honti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo vassassa antarāyo, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca vassassa antarāyā, yaṃ nemittā na jānanti, yattha nemittānaṃ cakkhu na kkhamatī ti. CXCVIII.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgatā vācā subhāsitā hoti no[^243-8] dubbhāsitā, anavajjā ca ananuvajjā ca[^243-9] viññūnaṃ. Katamehi pañcahi?


<!-- PTS Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 244]*

2. Kālena ca bhāsitā hoti, saccā ca bhāsitā hoti, saṇhā ca bhāsitā hoti, atthasaṃhitā ca bhāsitā hoti, mettacittena ca bhāsitā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi aṅgehi[^244-1] samannāgatā vācā subhāsitā hoti no[^244-2] dubbhāsitā, anavajjā ca ananuvajjā ca[^244-3] viññūnan ti. CXCIX.

1. Yasmiṃ[^244-4] bhikkhave samaye[^244-5] sīlavanto pabbajitā kulaṃ upasaṅkamanti, tattha manussā pañcahi ṭhānehi bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavanti. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye sīlavante pabbajite kulaṃ upasaṅkamante manussā[^244-6] disvā cittāni pasādenti[^244-7], saggasaṃvattanikaṃ bhikkhave taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paṭipadaṃ[^244-3] paṭipannaṃ hoti.

3. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye sīlavante pabbajite kulaṃ upasaṅkamante manussā paccuṭṭhenti abhivādenti āsanaṃ denti, uccākulīnasaṃvattanikaṃ[^244-8] bhikkhave taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paṭipadaṃ paṭipannaṃ hoti.

4. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye sīlavante pabbajite kulaṃ upasaṅkamante manussā maccheramalaṃ paṭivinodenti[^244-9], mahesakkhasaṃvattanikaṃ bhikkhave taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paṭipadaṃ paṭipannaṃ hoti.

5. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye sīlavante pabbajite kulaṃ upasaṅkamante manussā yathāsattiṃ[^244-10] yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajanti, mahābhogasaṃvattanikaṃ bhikkhave taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paṭipadaṃ paṭipannaṃ hoti.

6. Yasmiṃ[^244-11] bhikkhave samaye sīlavante pabbajite kulaṃ upasaṅkamante manussā paripucchanti paripañhanti dhammaṃ suṇanti mahāpaññāsaṃvattanikaṃ bhikkhave taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paṭipadaṃ paṭipannaṃ hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 245]*

Yasmiṃ[^245-1] bhikkhave samaye[^245-2] sīlavanto pabbajitā kulaṃ upasaṅkamanti, tattha manussā imehi pañcahi ṭhānehi bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavantī ti. CC.

1. Pañc'; imā bhikkhave nissaraṇīyā[^245-3] dhātuyo. Katamā pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmaṃ[^245-4] manasikaroto kāmesu cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati, nekkhammaṃ kho pan'assa manasikaroto nekkhamme cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ sukataṃ[^245-5] subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ[^245-6] kāmehi, ye ca kāmapaccayā uppajjanti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, mutto so tehi, na so taṃ vedanaṃ vediyati. Idam akkhātaṃ kāmānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno vyāpādaṃ manasikaroto vyāpāde cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati, avyāpādaṃ kho pan'assa manasikaroto avyāpāde cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ sukataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ vyāpādena, ye ca vyāpādapaccayā uppajjanti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, mutto so tehi, na so taṃ vedanaṃ vediyati. Idam akkhātaṃ vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno vihesaṃ manasikaroto vihesāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati, avihesaṃ kho pan'assa manasikaroto avihesāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ sukataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ vihesāya, ye ca vihesāpaccayā uppajjanti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, mutto so tehi, na so taṃ vedanaṃ vediyati. Idam akkhātaṃ vihesāya nissaraṇaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 246]*

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno rūpaṃ[^246-1] manasikaroto rūpe[^246-2] cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati, arūpaṃ kho pan'assa manasikaroto arūpe cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ sukataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ rūpehi, ye ca rūpapaccayā uppajjanti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, mutto so tehi, na so taṃ vedanaṃ vediyati. Idam akkhātaṃ rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno sakkāyaṃ manasikaroto sakkāye cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati, sakkāyanirodhaṃ kho pan'assa manasikaroto sakkāyanirodhe cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ sukataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ sakkāyena, ye ca sakkāyapaccayā uppajjanti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, mutto so tehi, na so taṃ vedanaṃ vediyati. Idam akkhātaṃ sakkāyassa nissaraṇaṃ. Tassa kāmanandi[^246-3] pi nānuseti, vyāpādanandi[^246-3] pi nānuseti, vihesānandi[^246-4] pi[^246-4] nānuseti[^246-4], rūpanandi[^246-3] pi nānuseti, sakkāyanandi[^246-3] pi nānuseti, so[^246-5] kāmanandiyā pi ananusayā vyāpādanandiyā pi ananusayā vihesānandiyā pi ananusayā rūpanandiyā pi ananusayā sakkāyanandiyā pi ananusayā ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu[^246-6] niranusayo[^246-7], acchejji[^246-8] taṇhaṃ, vivaṭṭayi[^246-9] saṃyojanaṃ, sammāmānābhisamayā antam akāsi dukkhassa. Imā kho bhikkhave pañca nissaraṇīyā[^246-10] dhātuyo ti. Brāhmaṇavaggo[^246-11] vīsatimo[^246-12].


<!-- PTS Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 247]*

Uddānaṃ[^247-1]: Soṇo Doṇo Saṅgāravo Kāraṇapālī[^247-2] ca Piṅgiyānī Supinā[^247-3] ca vassā vācā[^247-4] kulaṃ nissaraṇiyena cā ti. PAÑCAMA-PAṆṆĀSAKO. CCI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kimbilāyaṃ[^247-5] viharati Veḷuvane. Atha kho āyasmā Kimbilo[^247-6] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Kimbilo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti? Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo Satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhamme agāravā viharanti appatissā, saṅghe agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, aññamaññaṃ agāravā viharanti appatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu ayaṃ paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti.

3. Ko pana[^247-7] bhante hetu ko paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti? Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo Satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhamme sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghe sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, aññamaññaṃ sagāravā viharanti sappatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu ayaṃ paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 248]*

CCII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā dhammasavane[^248-1]. Katame pañca?

2. Assutaṃ[^248-2] suṇāti, sutaṃ pariyodapeti, kaṅkhaṃ vihanati[^248-3], diṭṭhiṃ ujuṃ karoti, cittam assa pasīdati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā dhammasavane[^248-1] ti. CCIII.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhaddo[^248-4] assājāniyo[^248-5] rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo[^248-6], rañño aṅgan tveva[^248-7] saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Ajjavena[^248-8], javena[^248-9], maddavena[^248-10], khantiyā, soraccena. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhaddo[^248-11] assājāniyo rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo, rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati.

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi pañcahi?

4. Ajjavena[^248-8], javena[^248-9], maddavena, khantiyā, soraccena. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. CCIV.

1. Pañc'; imāni bhikkhave balāni. Katamāni pañca?

2. Saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, ottappabalaṃ, viriyabalaṃ, paññābalaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave pañca balānī ti. CCV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave cetokhilā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati


<!-- PTS Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 249]*

nādhimuccati na sampasīdati[^249-1]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu[^249-2] Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya[^249-3] padhānāya. Yassa[^249-4] cittaṃ na[^249-2] namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamo cetokhilo.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhamme kaṅkhati[^249-5] . . .[^249-6] saṅghe kaṅkhati[^249-5] . . .[^249-6] sikkhāya kaṅkhati[^249-5] . . .[^249-6] sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto khilajāto. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto khilajāto, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañcamo cetokhilo. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca cetokhilā ti. CCVI.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave cetaso vinibandhā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu[^249-7] avītarāgo[^249-8] hoti avigatacchando avigatapemo avigatapipāso avigatapariḷāho avigatataṇho. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu[^249-9] kāmesu[^249-7] avītarāgo[^249-10] hoti avigatacchando avigatapemo avigatapipāso avigatapariḷāho avigatataṇho, tassa cittaṃ na manati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamo cetaso vinibandho.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye avītarāgo hoti . . .[^249-11] rūpe avītarāgo hoti . . . yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^249-12] middhasukhaṃ[^249-13] anuyutto viharati . . . aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 250]*

paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañcamo cetaso vinibandho. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca cetaso vinibandhā ti. CCVII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā yāguyā. Katame pañca?

2. Khudaṃ[^250-1] paṭihanati, pipāsaṃ paṭivineti[^250-2], vātaṃ anulometi, vatthiṃ sodheti, āmāvasesaṃ pāceti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā yāguyā ti. CCVIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādane. Katame pañca?

2. Acakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ duggandhaṃ hoti, rasaharaṇiyo na visujjhanti, pittaṃ semhaṃ bhattaṃ pariyonaddhanti[^250-3], bhattaṃ[^250-4] assa na cchādeti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādane.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādane. Katame pañca?

4. Cakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ na duggandhaṃ hoti, rasaharaṇiyo visujjhanti, pittaṃ semhaṃ bhattaṃ na pariyonaddhanti[^250-3], bhattaṃ assa chādeti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādane ti.


<!-- PTS Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 251]*

CCIX.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā āyatakena[^251-1] gītassarena dhammaṃ bhaṇantassa. Katame pañca?

2. Attanā[^251-2] pi tasmiṃ sare sārajjati, pare pi tasmiṃ sare sārajjanti, gahapatikā pi ujjhāyanti ‘yath'eva[^251-3] mayaṃ gāyāma, evam ev'ime[^251-4] samaṇā Sakyaputtiyā gāyantī'; ti, sarakuttim pi nikāmayamānassa samādhissa bhaṅgo hoti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpajjati[^251-5]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā āyatakena gītassarena dhammaṃ bhaṇantassā ti. CCX.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ okkamayato[^251-6]. Katame pañca?

2. Dukkhaṃ supati, dukkhaṃ paṭibujjhati, pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, devatā na rakkhanti, asuci muccati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ okkamayato.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitasatissa[^251-7] sampajānassa niddaṃ okkamayato. Katame pañca?

4. Sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, devatā rakkhanti, asuci na muccati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitasatissa[^251-7] sampajānassa niddaṃ okkamayato ti. Kimbilavaggo[^251-8] ekavīsatimo[^251-9]. Uddānaṃ[^251-10]: Kimbilo[^251-11] dhammasavanaṃ[^251-12] ājānī ca balaṃ khilaṃ Vinibandhaṃ yāgu kaṭṭhaṃ gītaṃ muṭṭhassatinā[^251-13] cā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 252]*

CCXI.

1. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu akkosakaparibhāsako ariyūpavādī brahmacārīnaṃ, tassa pañca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā. Katame pañca?

2. Pārājiko vā hoti chinnaparipantho[^252-1], aññataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpajjati, bāḷhaṃ vā rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^252-2]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu akkosakaparibhāsako ariyūpavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, tassa ime pañca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā ti. CCXII.

1. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārako kalahakārako vivādakārako bhassakārako saṅghe adhikaraṇakārako, tassa[^252-3] pañca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā. Katame pañca?

2. Anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati, adhigataṃ parihāyati, pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^252-2]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārako kalahakārako vivādakārako bhassakārako saṅghe adhikaraṇakārako, tassa ime pañca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā ti. CCXIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave dussīlo sīlavipanno pamādādhikaraṇaṃ[^252-4] mahatiṃ bhogajāniṃ nigacchati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo ādīnavo dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave dussīlassa sīlavipannassa pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo ādīnavo dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā.


<!-- PTS Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 253]*

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave dussīlo sīlavipanno yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, avisārado upasaṅkamati maṅkubhūto. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo ādīnavo dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave dussīlo sīlavipanno sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho ādīnavo dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave dussīlo sīlavipanno kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^253-1]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo ādīnavo dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā[^253-2].

7. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā sīlavato sīlasampadāya. Katame pañca?

8. Idha bhikkhave sīlavā sīlasampanno appamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati[^253-3]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo ānisaṃso sīlavato sīlasampadāya.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sīlavato sīlasampannassa kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo ānisaṃso sīlavato sīlasampadāya.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sīlavā sīlasampanno yañ ñad eva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visārado upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūto. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo ānisaṃso sīlavato sīlasampadāya.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sīlavā sīlasampanno asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho ānisaṃso sīlavato sīlasampadāya.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave sīlavā sīlasampanno kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^253-1]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo ānisaṃso sīlavato sīlasampadāya.


<!-- PTS Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 254]*

Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā sīlavato sīlasampadāyā ti. CCXIV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā bahubhāṇismiṃ puggale. Katame pañca?

2. Musā bhaṇati, pisunaṃ[^254-1] bhaṇati, pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphappalāpaṃ bhaṇati[^254-2], kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^254-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā bahubhāṇismiṃ puggale[^254-4].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā mantabhāṇismiṃ puggale. Katame pañca?

4. Na musā bhaṇati, na pisunaṃ[^254-5] bhaṇati, na pharusaṃ bhaṇati, na samphappalāpaṃ bhaṇati[^254-6], kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^254-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā mantabhāṇismiṃ puggale ti. CCXV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā akkhantiyā[^254-7]. Katame pañca?

2. Bahuno janassa appiyo hoti amanāpo, verabahulo ca hoti, vajjabahulo ca, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^254-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā akkhantiyā[^254-8].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā khantiyā. Katame pañca?

4. Bahuno janassa piyo hoti manāpo, na verabahulo hoti, na vajjabahulo, asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^254-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā khantiyā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 255]*

CCXVI.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā akkhantiyā. Katame pañca?

2. Bahuno janassa appiyo hoti amanāpo[^255-1], luddho[^255-2] ca hoti, vippaṭisārī ca, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^255-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā akkhantiyā.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā khantiyā. Katame pañca?

4. Bahuno janassa piyo hoti manāpo, aluddho[^255-4] ca hoti, avippaṭisārī ca, asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^255-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā khantiyā ti. CCXVII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā apāsādike. Katame pañca?

2. Attā pi attānaṃ upavadati, anuvicca viññū garahanti, pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^255-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā apāsādike[^255-5].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā pāsādike. Katame pañca?

4. Attā pi attānaṃ na upavadati[^255-6], anuvicca viññū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^255-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā pāsādike ti. CCXVIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā apāsādike. Katame pañca?

##### *[PTS Page 256]*

2. Appasannā na ppasīdanti, pasannānañ ca[^256-1] ekaccānaṃ aññathattaṃ hoti, Satthu sāsanaṃ akataṃ[^256-2] hoti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpajjati[^256-3], cittam assa na ppasīdati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā apāsādike[^256-4].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā pāsādike. Katame pañca?

4. Appasannā pasīdanti, pasannānañ ca[^256-1] bhīyobhāvo hoti, Satthu sāsanaṃ kataṃ hoti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpajjati[^256-5], cittam assa pasīdati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā pāsādike ti. CCIX.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā aggismiṃ. Katame pañca?

2. Acakkhusso, dubbaṇṇakaraṇo, dubbalakaraṇo[^256-6], saṅgaṇikāpavaḍḍhano[^256-7], tiracchānakathāpavattaniko[^256-8] hoti[^256-9]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā aggismin ti. CCXX.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā Madhurāyaṃ[^256-10]. Katame pañca?

2. Visamā, bahurajā[^256-11], caṇḍā[^256-12] sunakhā, vāḷā[^256-13] yakkhā, dullabhapiṇḍā. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā Madhurāyan[^256-14] ti. Akkosakavaggo[^256-15] dvāvīsatimo[^256-16]. Uddānaṃ[^256-17]:

Akkosabhaṇḍanasīlaṃ bahubhāṇī dve akhantiyo[^257-1] Apāsādikā[^257-2] dve vuttā aggismiṃ[^257-3] Madhurenā cā ti. CCXXI.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharato. Katame pañca?

2. Assutaṃ[^257-4] na suṇāti, sutaṃ na pariyodapeti, suten' ekaccena avisārado[^257-5] hoti, gāḷhaṃ[^257-6] rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, na ca mittavā hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharato.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā samavatthacāre. Katame pañca?

4. Assutaṃ suṇāti, sutaṃ pariyodapeti, suten'; ekaccena visārado hoti, na gāḷhaṃ rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, mittavā ca hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā samavatthacāre ti. CCXXII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharato. Katame pañca?

2. Anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati, adhigatā[^257-7] parihāyati, adhigaten'; ekaccena avisārado hoti, gāḷhaṃ rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, na ca mittavā hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharato.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā samavatthacāre. Katame pañca?

4. Anadhigataṃ adhigacchati, adhigatā[^257-8] na parihāyati, adhigaten'; ekaccena visārado hoti, na gāḷhaṃ rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, mittavā ca hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā samavatthacāre ti.

CCXXIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā atinivāse[^258-1]. Katame pañca?

2. Bahubhaṇḍo hoti bahubhaṇḍasannicayo, bahubhesajjo hoti bahubhesajjasannicayo, bahukicco hoti bahukaraṇīyo avyatto[^258-2] kiṃkaraṇīyesu, saṃsaṭṭho viharati sagahaṭṭhapabbajitehi[^258-3] anulomikena gihisaṃsaggena, tamhā ca āvāsā pakkamanto sāpekkho pakkamati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā atinivāse.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā samavatthavāse[^258-4]. Katame pañca?

4. Na bahubhaṇḍo hoti na bahubhaṇḍasannicayo, na bahubhesajjo hoti na bahubhesajjasannicayo, na bahukicco hoti na bahukaraṇīyo vyatto kiṃkaraṇīyesu, asaṃsaṭṭho viharati[^258-5] sagahaṭṭhapabbajitehi ananulomikena gihisaṃsaggena, tamhā ca āvāsā pakkamanto anapekkho pakkamati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā samavatthavāse[^258-4] ti. CCXXIV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā atinivāse[^258-6]. Katame pañca?

2. Āvāsamaccharī hoti, kulamaccharī[^258-7] hoti[^258-7], lābhamaccharī hoti, vaṇṇamaccharī hoti, dhammamaccharī hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā atinivāse[^258-6].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā samavatthanivāse. Katame pañca?

4. Na āvāsamaccharī hoti, na kulamaccharī hoti, na lābhamaccharī hoti, na vaṇṇamaccharī hoti, na dhammamaccharī hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā samavatthanivāse ti. CCXXV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā kulupake. Katame pañca?

2. Anāmantacāre[^259-1] āpajjati, rahonisajjāya āpajjati, paṭicchanne āsane āpajjati, mātugāmassa uttariṃ[^259-2] chappañca[^259-3] vācāhi dhammaṃ desento āpajjati, kāmasaṅkappabahulo viharati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā kulupake ti. CCXXVI.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā kulupakassa bhikkhuno ativelaṃ kulesu saṃsaṭṭhassa viharato. Katame pañca?

2. Mātugāmassa abhiṇhadassanaṃ, dassane sati saṃsaggo, saṃsagge sati vissāso, vissāse sati otāro, otiṇṇacittass'etaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^259-4]: anabhirato vā brahmacariyaṃ carissati[^259-5] aññataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpajjissati sikkhaṃ vā paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissati. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā kulupakassa bhikkhuno ativelaṃ kulesu saṃsaṭṭhassa viharato ti. CCXXVII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā bhogesu. Katame pañca?

2. Aggisādhāraṇā bhogā, udakasādhāraṇā bhogā, rājasādhāraṇā bhogā, corasādhāraṇā bhogā, appiyehi dāyādehi sādhāraṇā bhogā. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā bhogesu.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā bhogesu. Katame pañca?

4. Bhoge nissāya attānaṃ sukheti pīṇeti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, mātāpitaro sukheti pīṇeti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, puttadāradāsakammakaraporise sukheti pīṇeti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, mittāmacce sukheti pīṇeti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, samaṇabrāhmaṇesu[^259-6] uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpeti[^259-7] sovaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā bhogesū ti.

CCXXVIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā ussūrabhatte kule. Katame pañca?

2. Ye te atithī pāhunā, te na kālena paṭipūjenti. Yā tā balipaṭiggāhikā devatā, tā na kālena paṭipūjenti. Ye te samaṇabrāhmaṇā ekabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhojanā, te na kālena paṭipūjenti. Dāsakammakaraporisā[^260-1] vimukhā kammaṃ karonti. Tāvatakam yeva[^260-2] asamayena bhuttaṃ anojavantaṃ hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā ussūrabhatte kule.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā samayabhatte kule. Katame pañca?

4. Ye te atithī pāhunā, te kālena paṭipujenti. Yā tā balipaṭiggāhikā devatā, tā kālena paṭipūjenti. Ye te samaṇabrāhmaṇā ekabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhojanā, te kālena paṭipūjenti. 1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā kaṇhasappe. Katame pañca?

5. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave mātugāmassa ghoravisatā: yebhuyyena bhikkhave mātugāmo tibbarāgo. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave mātugāmassa dujjivhatā: yebhuyyena bhikkhave mātugāmo pisunavāco[^261-1]. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave mātugāmassa mittadubbhitā: yebhuyyena bhikkhave mātugāmo aticārinī. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā mātugāme ti. Dīghacārikavaggo[^261-2] tevīsatimo[^261-3]. Uddānaṃ[^261-4]: Dīghacārikā dve[^261-5] vuttā[^261-6] atinivāsamacchare[^261-7] Dve[^261-8] ca[^261-9] kulupakā[^261-10] bhogo[^261-11] bhattaṃ sappāpare duve[^261-12] ti. CCXXXI.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu abhāvanīyo hoti. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Na ākappasampanno hoti na vattasampanno, na[^261-13] bahussuto hoti na sutadharo, na[^261-14] paṭisallekhitā[^261-15] hoti na paṭisallānārāmo[^261-16], na kalyāṇavāco hoti na kalyāṇavākkaraṇo, duppañño hoti jaḷo eḷamūgo. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu abhāvanīyo hoti.

##### *[PTS Page 262]*

4. Ākappasampanno hoti vattasampanno[^262-1], bahussuto hoti sutadharo, paṭisallekhitā[^262-2] hoti paṭisallānārāmo[^262-3], kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo, paññavā hoti ajaḷo aneḷamūgo. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu bhāvanīyo hotī ti. CCXXXII.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ[^262-4] savyañjanaṃ[^262-5] kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^262-6] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo, poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā. Catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ[^262-7] ābhicetasikānaṃ[^262-8] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Āsavānaṃ[^262-9] khayā[^262-10] anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo cā ti. CCXXXIII.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu āvāsaṃ sobheti. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Sīlavā hoti . . .[^263-1] samādāya[^263-2] sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto hoti . . .[^263-1] diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo, poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya[^263-3] atthassa viññāpaniyā. Paṭibalo hoti upasaṅkamante[^263-4] dhammiyā kathāya sandassetuṃ samādapetuṃ samuttejetuṃ sampahaṃsetuṃ. Catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicetasikānaṃ[^263-5] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu āvāsaṃ sobhetī ti. CCXXXIV.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu āvāsassa bahūpakāro hoti. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^263-6] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto hoti . . . pe[^263-7] . . . diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Khaṇḍaphullaṃ paṭisaṅkharoti. Mahā kho pana bhikkhusaṅgho abhikkanto[^263-8] nānāverajjakā[^263-9] bhikkhū gihīnaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āroceti ‘mahā kho āvuso bhikkhusaṅgho abhikkanto nānāverajjakā bhikkhū, karotha puññāni, samayo puññāni kātun'; ti. Catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu āvāsassa bahūpakāro hotī ti. CCXXXV.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu gihīnaṃ[^263-10] anukampati. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Adhisīle[^263-11] samādapeti. Dhammadassane niveseti. Gilāṇake upasaṅkamitvā satiṃ uppādeti ‘arahaggataṃ

##### *[PTS Page 264]*

āyasmanto satiṃ upaṭṭhāpethā[^264-1]'; ti. Mahā kho pana bhikkhusaṅgho[^264-2] abhikkanto[^264-3] nānāverajjakā[^264-4] bhikkhū gihīnaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āroceti ‘mahā kho āvuso bhikkhusaṅgho[^264-5] abhikkanto nānāverajjakā bhikkhū, karotha puññāni, samayo puññāni kātun'; ti. Yaṃ kho pan'assa bhojanaṃ denti lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, taṃ[^264-6] attanā paribhuñjati, saddhādeyyaṃ[^264-7] na vinipāteti. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu gihīnaṃ[^264-8] anukampatī ti. CCXXXVI.

Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti.

4. Anuvicca pariyogāhetvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvicca pariyogāhetvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhā-

<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 266]*

sati, na āvāsamaccharī hoti, na kulamaccharī hoti, na[^266-1] lābhamaccharī[^266-1] hoti. Imehi kho[^266-2] bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCXXXIX.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Āvāsamaccharī hoti, kulamaccharī[^266-3] hoti[^266-3], lābhamaccharī hoti, vaṇṇamaccharī[^266-4] hoti, saddhādeyyaṃ vinipāteti. Imehi kho[^266-2] bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi pañcahi?

4. Na āvāsamaccharī hoti, na kulamaccharī hoti, na lābhamaccharī hoti, na vaṇṇamaccharī[^266-4] hoti, saddhādeyyaṃ na vinipāteti. Imehi kho[^266-2] bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCXL.

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Āvāsamaccharī hoti, kulamaccharī hoti, lābhamaccharī hoti, vaṇṇamaccharī hoti, dhammamaccharī hoti. Imehi kho[^266-2] bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi pañcahi?

4. Na āvāsamaccharī hoti, na kulamaccharī hoti, na lā-


<!-- PTS Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 267]*

bhamaccharī hoti, na vaṇṇamaccharī hoti, na dhammamaccharī hoti. Imehi kho[^267-1] bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato āvāsiko bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. Āvāsikavaggo[^267-2] catuvīsatimo[^267-3]. Uddānaṃ[^267-4]: Āvāsiko piyo[^267-5] ca[^267-6] sobhanā[^267-7] bahūpakāro[^267-8] anukampako ca Yathābhataṃ[^267-9] avaṇṇaṃ[^267-10] ca[^267-6] catukkamacchariyena[^267-11] cā ti. CCXLI.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā duccarite[^267-12]. Katame pañca?

2. Attā[^267-13] pi[^267-13] attānaṃ upavadati, anuvicca viññū garahanti, pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^267-14]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā duccarite[^267-12].

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā sucarite. Katame pañca?

4. Attā pi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvicca viññū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^267-14]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā sucarite ti. CCXLII-CCXLIV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā kāyaduccarite . . .[^267-15] kāyasucarite . . .[^267-15] vacīduccarite . . .[^267-15] vacīsucarite . . .[^267-15] manoduccarite . . .[^267-16] manosucarite. Katame pañca?


<!-- PTS Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 268]*

2. Attā pi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvicca viññū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, asammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^268-1]. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā manosucarite ti. CCXLV.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā duccarite. Katame pañca?

2. Attā pi attānaṃ upavadati, anuvicca viññū garahanti, pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, saddhammā vuṭṭhāti, asaddhamme patiṭṭhāti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā duccarite.

3. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā sucarite. Katame pañca?

4. Attā pi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvicca viññū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, asaddhammā vuṭṭhāti, saddhamme patiṭṭhāti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā sucarite ti. CCXLVI-CCXLVIII.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā kāyaduccarite . . .[^268-2] kāyasucarite . . .[^268-2] vacīduccarite . . .[^268-2] vacīsucarite . . .[^268-2] manoduccarite . . .[^268-2] manosucarite. Katame pañca?

2. Attā pi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvicca viññū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchati, asaddhammā vuṭṭhāti, saddhamme patiṭṭhāti. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ānisaṃsā manosucarite ti. CCXLIX.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā sīvathikāya. Katame pañca?

2. Asuci, duggandhā, sappaṭibhayā, vāḷānaṃ[^268-3] amanussānaṃ āvāso[^268-4], bahuno janassa ārodanā. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā sīvathikāya.

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave pañc'ime ādīnavā sīvathikūpame puggale. Katame pañca?


<!-- PTS Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 269]*

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo asucinā kāyakammena samannāgato hoti, asucinā vacīkammena samannāgato hoti, asucinā manokammena samannāgato hoti: idam assa asucitāya vadāmi. Seyyathā pi sā bhikkhave sīvathikā asuci, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi.

5. Tassa asucinā kāyakammena samannāgatassa asucinā vacīkammena samannāgatassa asucinā manokammena samannāgatassa pāpako kittisaddo abbhuggacchati: idam assa duggandhatāya vadāmi. Seyyathā pi sā bhikkhave sīvathikā duggandhā, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi.

6. Tam enaṃ asucinā kāyakammena samannāgataṃ asucinā vacīkammena samannāgataṃ asucinā manokammena samannāgataṃ pesalā sabrahmacārī[^269-1] ārakā parivajjenti[^269-2]: idam assa sappaṭibhayasmiṃ vadāmi. Seyyathā pi sā bhikkhave sīvathikā sappaṭibhayā, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi.

7. So asucinā kāyakammena samannāgato asucinā vacīkammena samannāgato asucinā manokammena samannāgato sabhāgehi puggalehi saddhiṃ saṃvasati: idam assa vāḷāvasathasmiṃ[^269-3] vadāmi. Seyyathā pi sā bhikkhave sīvathikā vāḷānaṃ amanussānaṃ āvāso[^269-4], tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi.

8. Tam enaṃ asucinā kāyakammena samannāgataṃ[^269-5] asucinā vacīkammena samannāgataṃ asucinā manokammena samannāgataṃ pesalā sabrahmacārī[^269-1] disvā khīyadhammaṃ[^269-6] āpajjanti ‘aho vata no dukkhaṃ, ye[^269-7] mayaṃ evarūpehi puggalehi saddhiṃ saṃvasāmā'; ti: idam assa ārodanāya vadāmi. Seyyathā pi sā bhikkhave sīvathikā bahuno janassa ārodanā, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā sīvathikūpame puggale ti.


<!-- PTS Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 270]*

CCL.

1. Pañc'ime bhikkhave ādīnavā puggalappasāde. Katame pañca?

2. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave puggale puggalo abhippasanno hoti, so tathārūpaṃ[^270-1] āpattiṃ āpanno hoti, yathārūpāya āpattiyā saṅgho ukkhipati. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yo kho myāyaṃ puggalo piyo manāpo, so saṅghena ukkhitto'; ti, bhikkhūsu appasādabahulo hoti, bhikkhūsu appasādabahulo samāno aññe bhikkhū na bhajati, aññe bhikkhū abhajanto saddhammaṃ na suṇāti, saddhammaṃ asuṇanto saddhammā parihāyati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo ādīnavo puggalappasāde.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave yasmiṃ puggale puggalo abhippasanno hoti, so tathārūpaṃ[^270-2] āpattiṃ āpanno hoti, yathārūpāya āpattiyā saṅgho ante nisīdāpeti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yo kho myāyaṃ puggalo piyo manāpo, so saṅghena ante nisīdāpito'; ti, bhikkhūsu appasādabahulo hoti, bhikkhūsu appasādabahulo samāno aññe bhikkhū na bhajati, aññe bhikkhū abhajanto[^270-3] saddhammaṃ na suṇāti, saddhammaṃ asuṇanto saddhammā parihāyati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo ādīnavo puggalappasāde.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave yasmiṃ puggale puggalo abhippasanno hoti, so disāpakkanto hoti . . . pe[^270-4] . . . so vibbhanto hoti . . . pe[^270-5] . . . so kālakato hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yo kho myāyaṃ puggalo piyo manāpo, so kālakato' ti, aññe bhikkhū na bhajati, aññe bhikkhū abhajanto saddhammaṃ na suṇāti, saddhammaṃ asuṇanto saddhammā parihāyati. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo ādīnavo puggalappasāde. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca ādīnavā puggalappasāde ti. Duccaritavaggo[^270-6] pañcavīsatimo[^270-7].


<!-- PTS Page 271 -->

##### *[PTS Page 271]*

Uddānaṃ[^271-1]: Duccaritaṃ kāyaduccaritaṃ vacīduccaritaṃ manoduccaritaṃ. Catūhi pare dve sīvathikā puggalappasādena cā ti[^271-2]. [CHAṬṬHA-PAṆṆĀSAKO[^271-3].]

1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā upasampādetabbaṃ. Katamehi pañcahi?

2. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā upasampādetabban ti.

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu asekhena sīlakkhandhena samannāgato[^271-6] hoti[^271-7], asekhena samādhikkhandhena . . . asekhena paññākkhandhena[^271-8] . . . asekhena vimuttikkhandena . . . asekhena vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhena samannāgato[^271-9] hoti[^271-9]. 1. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi pañcahi?

Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, abrahmacariyā paṭivirato hoti, musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti.

##### *[PTS Page 279]*

CHAKKA-NIPĀTA.

<p align="center">Namo Tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


PAṬHAMA-PAṆṆĀSAKO. I.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^279-1] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo[^279-2] dakkhiṇeyyo[^279-2] añjalikaraṇīyo[^279-2] anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā neva sumano hoti na dummano, upekkhako[^279-3] viharati sato sampajāno. Sotena saddaṃ sutvā . . . ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā . . . jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā . . . kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā . . . manasā dhammaṃ viññāya[^279-4] neva sumano hoti na dummano, upekkhako[^279-3] viharati sato sampajāno. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo[^279-5] dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. Idam[^279-6] avoca Bhagavā. Attamanā te bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinandun ti.


<!-- PTS Page 280 -->
<a id="page-280"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 280]*

II.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^280-1] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anekavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ paccanubhoti, eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti, bahudhā[^280-2] pi hutvā eko hoti, āvibhāvaṃ tirobhāvaṃ tirokuḍḍaṃ tiropākāraṃ tiropabbataṃ asajjamāno gacchati seyyathā pi ākāse, paṭhaviyā pi ummujjanimujjaṃ karoti seyyathā pi udake, udake pi abhijjamāne[^280-3] gacchati seyyathā pi paṭhaviyaṃ[^280-4], ākāse pi pallaṅkena kamati seyyathā pi pakkhī sakuṇo, ime pi candimasuriye evaṃmahiddhike evaṃmahānubhāve pāṇinā parāmasati parimajjati, yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena 'va saṃvatteti.

3. Dibbāya sotadhātuyā visuddhāya atikkantamānusikāya ubho sadde suṇāti[^280-5] dibbe ca mānuse ca ye dūre santike ca[^280-6].

4. Parasattānaṃ parapuggallānaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittan ti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . sadosaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vītadosaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . samohaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vītamohaṃ vā cittam . . . saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ . . . amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ . . . sa-uttaraṃ[^280-7] vā cittaṃ . . . anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ . . . samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittan ti pajānāti.

5. Anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo[^280-8] tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo pañca pi jātiyo dasa[^280-9] pi[^280-9] jātiyo[^280-9] vīsatim[^280-10] pi jātiyo tiṃsatim[^280-11] pi jātiyo cattāḷīsam pi jātiyo paññāsam pi jā-

tiyo jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam[^281-1] pi[^281-1] aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke[^281-1] pi[^281-1] vivaṭṭakappe[^281-1] aneke[^281-2] pi[^281-2] saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe[^281-2] amutrāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto[^281-3], so tato cuto idhūpapanno[^281-4] ti. Iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesam anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati.

6. Dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^281-5] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate, yathākammūpage satte pajānāti ‘ime vata bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīduccaritena samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapannā[^281-5], ime vā[^281-6] pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena samannāgatā vacīsucaritena samannāgatā manosucaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapannā[^281-5]'; ti. Iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^281-5] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate, yathākammūpage satte pajānāti. Āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^281-7] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. III.

2. Saddhābalena viriyabalena satibalena samādhibalena paññābalena āsavānaṃ[^282-4] khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. 1. Chahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhadro assājāniyo[^282-6] rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo[^282-7], rañño aṅgan tveva[^282-8] saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave rañño bhadro assājāniyo khamo hoti rūpānaṃ, khamo saddānaṃ, khamo gandhānaṃ, khamo rasānaṃ, khamo phoṭṭhabbānaṃ, vaṇṇasampanno ca hoti. Evam eva kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^282-9] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khamo hoti rūpānaṃ, khamo saddānaṃ, khamo gandhānaṃ, khamo rasānaṃ, khamo phoṭṭhabbānaṃ, khamo dhammānaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^283-1] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. VI.

1. Chahi bhikkhave aṅgehi[^283-2] samannāgato rañño bhadro assājāniyo[^283-3] rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo, rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave rañño bhadro assājāniyo khamo hoti rūpānaṃ, khamo saddānaṃ, khamo gandhānaṃ, khamo rasānaṃ, khamo phoṭṭhabbānaṃ, balasampanno ca hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhadro assājāniyo[^283-3] rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo, rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Evam eva kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^283-4] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khamo hoti rūpānaṃ, khamo saddānaṃ[^283-5], khamo gandhānaṃ, khamo rasānaṃ, khamo phoṭṭhabbānaṃ, khamo dhammānaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^283-4] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettam lokassā ti. VII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave aṅgehi[^283-6] samannāgato rañño bhadro assājāniyo[^283-7] rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo, rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Katamehi chahi?
