Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhadro assājāniyo rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo, rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Evam eva kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^284-1] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khamo hoti rūpānaṃ . . . pe[^284-2] . . . khamo dhammānaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^284-1] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. VIII.

1. Cha yimāni[^284-3] bhikkhave anuttariyāni. Katamāni cha?

2. Dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ[^284-4], anussatānuttariyaṃ[^284-5]. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha anuttariyānī[^284-6] ti. IX.

1. Cha yimāni[^284-3] bhikkhave anussatiṭṭhānāni[^284-7]. Katamāni cha?

2. Buddhānussati, dhammānussati, saṅghānussati, sīlānussati, cāgānussati, devatānussati. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha anussatiṭṭhānānī[^284-7] ti. X.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme. Atha kho Mahānāmo Sakko yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo Sakko Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: Yo so bhante ariyasāvako āgataphalo viññātasāsano, so katamena vihārena bahulaṃ viharatī ti? Yo so Mahānāma ariyasāvako


<!-- PTS Page 285 -->
<a id="page-285"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 285]*

āgataphalo viññātasāsano, so iminā vihārena bahulaṃ viharati[^285-1]:--

2. Idha Mahānāma ariyasāvako Tathāgataṃ anussarati ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Yasmiṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako Tathāgataṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti Tathāgataṃ ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati[^285-2] dhammavedaṃ[^285-2], labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ[^285-3], pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati[^285-4], sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno[^285-5] viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho viharati, dhammasotaṃ[^285-6] samāpanno[^285-6] buddhānussatiṃ bhāveti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ Mahānāma ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati ‘svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo sandiṭṭhiko akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^285-7] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī' ti. Yasmiṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti dhammaṃ ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati dhammavedaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ[^285-3], pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati[^285-4], sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho


<!-- PTS Page 286 -->
<a id="page-286"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 286]*

viharati, dhammasotaṃ samāpanno dhammānussatiṃ bhāveti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ Mahānāma ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati ‘supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, ujupaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, ñāyapaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, samīcipaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, yad idaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, esa Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā'; ti. Yasmiṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti saṅghaṃ ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati dhammavedaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ[^286-1], pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati[^286-2], sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho viharati, dhammasotaṃ samāpanno saṅghānussatiṃ bhāveti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ Mahānāma ariyasāvako attano sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni[^286-3] viññūpasaṭṭhāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni. Yasmiṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako attano sīlaṃ[^286-4] anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti sīlaṃ ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati dhammavedaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ[^286-5], pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati, sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati.


<!-- PTS Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 287]*

Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho viharati, dhammasotaṃ samāpanno sīlānussatiṃ bhāveti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ Mahānāma ariyasāvako attano cāgaṃ anussarati ‘lābhā vata me suladdhaṃ vata me, yo 'haṃ maccheramalapariyuṭṭhitāya pajāya vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasāmi muttacāgo payatapāṇi vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato'; ti. Yasmiṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako cāgaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti cāgaṃ ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati dhammavedaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati, sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho viharati, dhammasotaṃ samāpanno cāgānussatiṃ bhāveti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ Mahānāma ariyasāvako devatānussatiṃ bhāveti ‘santi devā Catummahārājikā, santi devā Tāvatiṃsā, santi devā Yāmā, santi devā Tusitā, santi devā Nimmānaratino, santi devā Paranimmitavasavattino, santi devā Brahmakāyikā, santi devā Taduttari[^287-1]; yathārūpāya saddhāya samannāgatā tā[^287-2] devatā ito cutā tattha[^287-3] upapannā[^287-4], mayham pi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvijjati; yathārūpena sīlena samannāgatā tā devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^287-4], mayham pi tathārūpaṃ sīlaṃ saṃvijjati; yathārūpena sutena samannāgatā tā devatā tato cutā tattha upapannā[^287-4], mayham pi tathārūpaṃ sutaṃ saṃvijjati; yathārūpena cāgena samannāgatā tā devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^287-4], mayham pi tathārūpo cāgo saṃvijjati; yathārūpāya paññāya samannāgatā tā devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^287-4], mayham pi tathārūpā paññā saṃvijjatī'; ti. Yas-


<!-- PTS Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 288]*

miṃ Mahānāma samaye ariyasāvako attano ca tāsañ ca devatānaṃ saddhañ ca sīlañ ca sutañ ca cāgañ ca paññañ ca anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti devatā[^288-1] ārabbha. Ujugatacitto kho pana Mahānāma ariyasāvako labhati atthavedaṃ, labhati dhammavedam, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmujjaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati, sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Ayaṃ vuccati Mahānāma ariyasāvako: visamagatāya pajāya sampanno viharati, savyāpajjhāya pajāya avyāpajjho viharati, dhammasotaṃ samāpanno devatānussatiṃ bhāveti. Yo so Mahānāma ariyasāvako āgataphalo viññātasāsano, so iminā vihārena bahulaṃ viharatī ti. Āhuneyyavaggo[^288-2] paṭhamo[^288-2]. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^288-3]: Dve āhuneyyā indriya-balāni tayo[^288-4] ājāniyā Anuttariya[^288-5]-anussatā[^288-6] Mahānāmena te dasā ti. XI.

1. Cha yime[^288-7] bhikkhave dhammā sārāṇīyā. Katame cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ kāyakammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti sabrahmacārīsu āvi c'eva raho ca: ayaṃ pi dhammo sārāṇīyo.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ vacīkammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti sabrahmacārīsu āvi c'eva raho ca: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ manokammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti sabrahmacārīsu āvi c'eva raho ca: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo.


<!-- PTS Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 289]*

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu[^289-1], ye te lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamaso pattapariyāpannamattam pi, tathārūpehi lābhehi appaṭivibhattabhogī hoti, sīlavantehi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhogī: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu, yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni[^289-2] viññūpasaṭṭhāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpehi sīlehi sīlasāmaññagato[^289-3] viharati sabrahmacārīhi[^289-4] āvi c'eva raho ca: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu, yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā nīyānikā nīyāti takkarassa sammādukkhakkhayāya, tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmaññagato[^289-3] viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi c'eva raho ca: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā sārāṇīyā ti. XII.

1. Cha yime[^289-5] bhikkhave dhammā sārāṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ kāyakammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti sabrahmacārīsu āvi c'eva raho ca. Ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ vacīkammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti . . .pe[^289-6] . . . mettaṃ manokammaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti sabrahmacārīsu āvi c'eva raho ca. Ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu[^289-7], ye te lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamaso pattapariyāpannamattam pi, tathārūpehi lābhehi appaṭivibhattabhogī hoti sīla-


<!-- PTS Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 290]*

vantehi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhogī. Ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu[^290-1], yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni[^290-2] viññūpasaṭṭhāni[^290-3] aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpehi sīlehi sīlasāmaññagato viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi c'eva raho ca. Ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu, yāyaṃ diṭṭhi[^290-4] ariyā nīyānikā[^290-5] nīyāti[^290-5] takkarassa sammādukkhakkhayāya, tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmaññagato viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi c'eva raho ca. Ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā sārāṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattantī ti. XIII.

1. Cha yimā[^290-6] bhikkhave nissāraṇīyā dhātuyo. Katamā cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vadeyya ‘mettā hi kho me cetovimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā[^290-7] yānikatā[^290-8] vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana me vyāpādo cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā[^290-9] evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, aṭṭhānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ mettāya cetovimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya[^290-10] vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha


<!-- PTS Page 291 -->
<a id="page-291"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 291]*

ca pan'assa vyāpādo cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇaṃ h'etaṃ āvuso vyāpādassa, yad idaṃ mettā cetovimutti.

3. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vadeyya ‘karuṇā hi kho me cetovimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana me vihesā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, aṭṭhānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ karuṇāya cetovimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca pan'assa vihesā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇam[^291-1] h'etaṃ āvuso vihesāya, yad idaṃ karuṇā cetovimutti.

4. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vadeyya ‘muditā hi kho me cetovimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana me arati cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, atthānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ muditāya cetovimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca pan'assa arati cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇaṃ h'etaṃ āvuso aratiyā, yad idaṃ muditā cetovimutti.

5. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vaddeyya ‘upekkhā[^291-2] hi kho me cetovimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana me rāgo cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, aṭṭhānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ upekkhāya cetovimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamārad-


<!-- PTS Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 292]*

dhāya, atha ca pan'assa rāgo cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī' ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇaṃ h'etaṃ āvuso rāgassa, yad idaṃ upekkhā cetovimutti.

6. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vadeyya ‘animittā hi kho me cetovimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana me[^292-1] nimittānusāri viññāṇaṃ[^292-2] hotī'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, aṭṭhānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ animittāya cetovimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānikatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca pan'assa nimittānusāri viññāṇaṃ[^292-3] bhavissatī'; ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇaṃ h'etaṃ āvuso sabbanimittānaṃ, yad idaṃ animittā cetovimutti.

7. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ vadeyya ‘asmī ti kho me[^292-4] vigataṃ[^292-5], ayam aham asmī ti ca[^292-6] na samanupassāmi, atha ca pana me vicikicchākathaṃkathāsallaṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī[^292-7]'; ti. So <<mā h'evan>> ti 'ssa vacanīyo: ‘māyasmā evaṃ avaca, mā Bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu Bhagavato abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi Bhagavā evaṃ vadeyya, aṭṭhānam etaṃ āvuso anavakāso, yaṃ asmī ti vigate[^292-8], ayam aham asmī ti ca na samanupassato[^292-9], atha ca pan'assa vicikicchākathaṃkathāsallaṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Nissaraṇaṃ h'etaṃ āvuso vicikicchākathaṃkathāsallassa, yad idaṃ asmī ti mānasamugghāto. Imā kho bhikkhave cha nissāraṇīyā dhātuyo ti. XIV.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^292-10] ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyas-


<!-- PTS Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 293]*

mato Sāriputtassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Sāriputto etad avoca:--

2. Tathā tathā āvuso bhikkhu[^293-1] vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^293-2] vihāraṃ kappayato na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, na bhaddikā[^293-3] kālakiriyā. Kathañ[^293-4] cāvuso bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa vihāraṃ kappayato na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, na bhaddikā kālakiriyā?

3. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kammārāmo hoti kammarato kammārāmataṃ anuyutto, bhassārāmo hoti bhassarato bhassārāmataṃ anuyutto, niddārāmo hoti niddārato niddārāmataṃ anuyutto, saṅgaṇikārāmo hoti saṅgaṇikārato saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyutto, saṃsaggārāmo hoti saṃsaggarato saṃsaggārāmataṃ anuyutto, papañcārāmo hoti papañcarato papañcārāmataṃ anuyutto. Evaṃ kho āvuso bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^293-2] vihāraṃ kappayato na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, na bhaddikā kālakiriyā. Ayaṃ vuccat'; āvuso[^293-5] bhikkhu sakkāyābhirato, na pahāsi[^293-6] sakkāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya.

4. Tathā tathā āvuso[^293-7] bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^293-8] vihāraṃ kappayato bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā. Kathañ cāvuso bhikkhu[^293-1] tathā tathā[^293-9] vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^293-2] vihāraṃ kappayato bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā?

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu na[^293-9] kammārāmo hoti na kammarato na kammārāmataṃ anuyutto, na bhassārāmo hoti na bhassarato na bhassārāmataṃ anuyutto, na niddārāmo hoti na niddārato na niddārāmataṃ anuyutto, na saṅgaṇikārāmo hoti na saṅgaṇikārato na saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyutto, na saṃsaggārāmo hoti na saṃsaggarato na saṃsaggārāmataṃ anuyutto, na papañcārāmo hoti na papañcarato na papañcārāmataṃ anuyutto. Evaṃ kho āvuso bhikkhu


<!-- PTS Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 294]*

tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^294-1] vihāraṃ kappayato bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hoti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā. Ayaṃ vuccat'; āvuso bhikkhu nibbānābhirato, pahāsi sakkāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā ti. Yo papañcaṃ[^294-2] anuyutto papañcābhirato mago[^294-3] virādhayi[^294-4] so nibbānaṃ yogakkhemaṃ anuttaraṃ yo ca[^294-5] papañcaṃ hitvāna[^294-6] nippapañcapade[^294-7] rato ārādhayi[^294-4] so nibbānaṃ yogakkhemaṃ anuttaran ti[^294-5]. XV.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Tathā tathāvuso[^294-8] bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^294-1] vihāraṃ kappayato kālakiriyā anutappā hoti. Kathañ[^294-9] cāvuso[^294-10] bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^294-1] vihāraṃ kappayato kālakiriyā anutappā hoti?

2. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kammārāmo hoti kammarato kammārāmataṃ anuyutto, bhassārāmo hoti . . . niddārāmo hoti . . . saṅgaṇikārāmo hoti . . . saṃsaggārāmo hoti . . . papañcārāmo hoti papañcarato papañcārāmataṃ anuyutto. Evaṃ kho āvuso bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa[^294-11] vihāraṃ kappayato kālakiriyā anutappā hoti. Ayaṃ vuccat'; āvuso bhikkhu sakkāyābhirato, na pahāsi[^294-12] sakkāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya.

3. Tathā tathā āvuso bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa vihāraṃ kappayato kālakiriyā ananutappā hoti. Kathañ[^294-13] cāvuso[^294-10] bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa vihāraṃ kappayato[^294-14] kālakiriyā ananutappā hoti?

4. Idhāvuso[^294-15] bhikkhu na kammārāmo hoti na kammārato na kammarāmataṃ anuyutto, na bhassārāmo hoti . . .[^294-16]


<!-- PTS Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 295]*

na niddārāmo hoti . . . na saṅgaṇikārāmo hoti[^295-1] . . . na saṃsaggārāmo hoti . . .[^295-2] na papañcārāmo hoti na papañcarato na papañcārāmataṃ anuyutto. Evaṃ kho āvuso bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappeti, yathā yathā 'ssa vihāraṃ kappayato kālakiriyā ananutappā[^295-3] hoti. Ayaṃ vuccat'; āvuso bhikkhu nibbānābhirato[^295-4], pahāsi[^295-5] sakkāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā ti. Yo papañcaṃ[^295-6] anuyutto papañcābhirato mago[^295-7] virādhayi so nibbānaṃ yogakkhemaṃ anuttaraṃ yo ca papañcaṃ hitvāna nippapañcapade[^295-8] rato[^295-9] ārādhayi so nibbānaṃ yogakkhemaṃ anuttaran ti. XVI.

1. Ekam samayaṃ Bhagavā Bhaggesu[^295-10] viharati Suṃsumāragire[^295-11] Bhesakaḷāvane[^295-12] Migadāye. Tena kho pana samayena Nakulapitā gahapati ābādhiko[^295-13] hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno. Atha kho Nakulamātā gahapatānī Nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ etad avoca:--

2. Mā kho tvaṃ gahapati sāpekho[^295-14] kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā ca Bhagavatā[^295-15] sāpekhassa kālakiriyā. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā[^295-16] gahapatānī mam'; accayena na[^295-4] sakkoti[^295-17] dārake posetuṃ gharāvāsaṃ santharitun'[^295-18] ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Kusalā 'haṃ gahapati kappāsaṃ kantituṃ[^295-19] veṇiṃ olikhituṃ[^295-20], sakkom'[^295-21] ahaṃ gahapati tav'; accayena dārake posetuṃ gha-


<!-- PTS Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 296]*

rāvāsaṃ santharituṃ. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā ca Bhagavatā sāpekhassa kālakiriyā. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā gahapatānī mam'; accayena aññaṃ gharaṃ[^296-1] gamissatī'; ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Tvañ c'eva kho gahapati jānāsi, ahañ ca, yathā[^296-2] no[^296-3] soḷasavassāni gahaṭṭhakaṃ[^296-4] brahmacariyaṃ samāciṇṇaṃ[^296-5]. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā ca Bhagavatā sāpekhassa kālakiriyā. Siyā kho pana te[^296-6] gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā gahapatānī mam'; accayena na dassanakāmā bhavissati Bhagavato, na dassanakāmā bhikkhusaṅghassā'; ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Ahaṃ hi gahapati tav'; accayena[^296-7] dassanakāmatarā[^296-8] c'eva[^296-9] bhavissāmi[^296-10] Bhagavato dassanakāmatarā ca bhikkhusaṅghassa. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā ca Bhagavatā sāpekhassa kālakiriyā. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā[^296-11] gahapatānī mam'; accayena[^296-12] na[^296-13] sīlesu paripūrakārinī'; ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Yāvatā kho gahapati tassa Bhagavato sāvikā gihī[^296-6] odātavasanā sīlesu paripūrakāriniyo, ahan tāsaṃ aññatarā. Yassa kho pan'assa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, ayaṃ so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho Bhaggesu viharati Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye, taṃ Bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam


<!-- PTS Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā ca Bhagavatā sāpekhassa kālakiriyā. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā gahapatānī na[^297-1] lābhinī ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassā'; ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Yāvatā kho gahapati tassa Bhagavato sāvikā gihī odātavasanā lābhiniyo ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa, ahan tāsaṃ aññatarā. Yassa kho pan'assa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, ayaṃ so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho Bhaggesu viharati Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye, taṃ Bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā, garahitā[^297-2] ca[^297-2] Bhagavatā[^297-2] sāpekhassa[^297-2] kālakiriyā[^297-2]. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘Nakulamātā gahapatānī na[^297-3] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye ogādhappattā paṭigādhappattā[^297-4] assāsappattā[^297-5] tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vesārajjappattā aparappaccayā Satthu sāsane viharatī'; ti. Na kho pan'etaṃ gahapati evaṃ[^297-6] daṭṭhabbaṃ. Yāvatā kho gahapati tassa Bhagavato sāvikā gihī odātavasanā imasmiṃ dhammavinaye ogādhappattā paṭigādhappattā[^297-7] assāsappattā[^297-8] tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vesārajjappattā aparappaccayā Satthu sāsane viharantiyo, ahan tāsaṃ aññatarā. Yassa kho pan'assa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, ayaṃ so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho Bhaggesu viharati Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye, taṃ Bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ gahapati mā sāpekho kālam akāsi. Dukkhā gahapati sāpekhassa kālakiriyā[^297-9], garahitā ca Bhagavatā sāpekhassa kālakiriyā ti.

3. Atha kho Nakulapituno[^297-2] gahapatissa[^297-2] Nakulamā-


<!-- PTS Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 298]*

tarā[^298-1] gahapatāniyā iminā ovādena ovadiyamānassa so ābādho ṭhānaso paṭippassambhi, vuṭṭhahi ca Nakulapitā gahapati tamhā ābādhā. Tathā pahīno ca pana Nakulapituno gahapatissa so ābādho ahosi. Atha kho Nakulapitā gahapati gilānā vuṭṭhito[^298-2] aciravuṭṭhito[^298-3] gelaññā daṇḍam[^298-4] olubbha[^298-4] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

4. Lābhā te gahapati suladdhan te gahapati, yassa te Nakulamātā[^298-5] gahapatānī anukampikā atthakāmā ovādikā anusāsikā; yāvatā kho gahapati mama sāvikā gihī odātavasanā sīlesu paripūrakāriniyo, Nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ aññatarā; yāvatā kho gahapati mama sāvikā gihī odātavasanā labhiniyo ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa, Nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ aññatarā[^298-6]; yāvatā kho gahapati mama sāvikā gihī odātavasanā imasmiṃ dhammavinaye ogādhappattā paṭigādhappattā[^298-7] assāsappattā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vesārajjappattā aparappaccayā Satthu sāsane viharantiyo, Nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ aññatarā. Lābhā te gahapati suladdhan te gahapati, yassa te Nakulamātā gahapatānī anukampikā atthakāmā ovādikā anusāsikā ti. XVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ[^298-8] paṭisallānā[^298-9] vuṭṭhito yen'; upaṭṭhānasālā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Āyasmā pi kho Sāriputto sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yen'; upaṭṭhānasālā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhaga-


<!-- PTS Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 299]*

vantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Āyasmā pi kho Mahāmoggallāno, āyasmā pi kho Mahākassapo[^299-1], āyasmā pi kho Mahākaccāno, āyasmā pi kho Mahākoṭṭhito[^299-2], āyasmā pi kho Mahācundo, āyasmā pi kho Mahākappino, āyasmā pi kho Anuruddho, āyasmā pi kho Revato, āyasmā pi kho Ānando sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yen'; upaṭṭhānasālā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi[^299-3]. Atha kho Bhagavā bahu-deva[^299-4] rattiṃ nisajjāya vītināmetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi. Te pi kho[^299-5] āyasmanto acirapakkantassa Bhagavato uṭṭhāyāsanā[^299-6] yathāvihāraṃ agamaṃsu[^299-7]. Ye pana tattha bhikkhū navā[^299-8] acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, te yāva suriyass'; uggamanā kākacchamānā supiṃsu. Addasā[^299-9] kho Bhagavā dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena te bhikkhū yāva suriyass'; uggamanā kākacchamāne supante, disvā yen'; upaṭṭhānasālā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā te[^299-5] bhikkhū āmantesi:--

2. Kahan nu kho bhikkhave Sāriputto, kahaṃ[^299-10] Mahāmoggallāno[^299-10], kahaṃ[^299-10] Mahākassapo[^299-10], kahaṃ Mahākaccāno, kahaṃ Mahākoṭṭhito[^299-2], kahaṃ Mahācundo, kahaṃ Mahākappino, kahaṃ Anuruddho, kahaṃ Revato, kahaṃ Ānando, kahan nu[^299-11] kho te bhikkhave therā sāvakā gatā ti[^299-12]? ‘Te pi kho bhante āyasmanto acirapakkantassa Bhagavato uṭṭhāyāsanā[^299-13] yathāvihāraṃ agamaṃsū[^299-14]'; ti. Tena[^299-15] no[^299-16] tumhe bhikkhave therā, bhikkhū navā[^299-17] yāva suriyass'; uggamanā kākacchamānā supatha. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā: rājā[^299-18] khattiyo[^299-19] muddhāvasitto[^299-20] yāvadatthaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^300-1] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharanto yāvajīvaṃ rajjaṃ kārento[^300-2] janapadassa vā piyo manāpo ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.'; Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā pi kho etaṃ bhikkhave neva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ: rājā khattiyo muddhāvasitto yāvadatthaṃ seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharanto yāvajīvaṃ rajjaṃ kārento[^300-3] janapadassa vā piyo manāpo ti. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā: raṭṭhiko pettaniko[^300-4] senāya[^300-4] senāpatiko gāmagāmiko[^300-5] pūgagāmaṇiko[^300-6] yāvadaṭṭhaṃ seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharanto yāvajīvaṃ pūgagāmaṇikattaṃ[^300-7] kārento pūgassa[^300-8] vā piyo manāpo[^300-9] ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.'; Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā pi kho etaṃ bhikkhave neva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ: pūgagāmaṇiko yāvadattham seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharanto yāvajīvaṃ pūgagāmaṇikattaṃ[^300-7] kārento pūgassa vā piyo manāpo'; ti. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā: samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā yāvadatthaṃ seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutto indriyesu aguttadvāro[^300-10] bhojane amattaññū jāgariyaṃ ananuyutto avipassako kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ[^300-11] bodhapakkhikānaṃ[^300-12] dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyogaṃ[^300-13] ananuyutto[^300-14] āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanto ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante'; . Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā pi kho etaṃ bhikkhave neva diṭṭhaṃ na


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

sutaṃ: samaṇo[^301-1] vā brāhmaṇo[^301-1] vā yāvadatthaṃ seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^301-2] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto indriyesu aguttadvāro[^301-3] bhojane amattaññū jāgariyaṃ ananuyutto[^301-4] avipassako kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ[^301-5] bodhapakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyogaṃ[^301-6] ananuyutto āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanto ti. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

3. Indriyesu guttadvārā bhavissāma bhojane mattaññuno[^301-7] jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā[^301-8] vipassakā[^301-9] kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ[^301-10] bodhapakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyogam anuyuttā viharissāmā ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti. XVIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosalesu cārikaṃ carati[^301-11] mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ. Addasā[^301-12] kho Bhagavā[^301-13] addhānamaggapaṭipanno aññatarasmiṃ padese macchikaṃ[^301-14] macchabandhaṃ[^301-15] macche vadhitvā vadhitvā[^301-16] vikkiṇamāṇaṃ, disvā maggā ukkamma[^301-17] aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--

2. Passatha no tumhe bhikkhave amuṃ[^301-18] macchikaṃ macchabandhaṃ[^301-19] macche vadhitvā vadhitvā[^301-20] vikkiṇamānan ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.' Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā: macchiko macchabandho macche vadhitvā


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

vadhitvā[^302-1] vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.'; Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā[^302-2] pi[^302-2] kho[^302-2] etaṃ[^302-2] bhikkhave[^302-2] neva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ: macchiko macchabandho macche vadhitvā vadhitvā vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī va rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Te[^302-3] hi so bhikkhave macche vajjhe vadhāy'; upanīte[^302-4] pāpakena manasānupekkhati, tasmā so neva hatthiyāyī hoti na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhogabhogī, na mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā: goghātako gāvo[^302-5] vadhitvā vadhitvā vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante[^302-6].'; Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā pi kho etaṃ bhikkhave neva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ: goghātako gāvo[^302-5] vadhitvā vadhitvā vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Te[^302-7] hi so bhikkhave gāvo vajjhe vadhāy'; upanīte[^302-8] pāpakena manasānupekkhati, tasmā so neva hatthiyāyī hoti[^302-9] na[^302-10] assayāyī[^302-11] na[^302-10] rathayāyī[^302-11] na[^302-10] yānayāyī[^302-11] na[^302-10] bhogabhogī[^302-12], na mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati. Tam kim maññatha bhikkhave, api nu tumhehi diṭṭhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

vā sutaṃ vā: orabbhiko[^303-1] . . . pe[^303-2] . . . sūkariko[^303-3] . . .[^303-4] sākuṇiko . . .[^303-4] māgaviko mige[^303-5] vadhitvā vadhitvā vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.' Sādhu bhikkhave, mayā pi kho etaṃ[^303-6] bhikkhave neva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ: māgaviko mige[^303-5] vadhitvā vadhitvā vikkiṇamāno tena kammena tena ājīvena hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhogabhogī vā mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasanto ti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Te hi so bhikkhave mige[^303-5] vajjhe vadhāy'; upanīte[^303-7] pāpakena manasānupekkhati, tasmā so neva hatthiyāyī[^303-8] hoti[^303-9] na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhogabhogī, na[^303-10] mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati. Te hi[^303-11] so bhikkhave tiracchānagate pāṇe vajjhe vadhāy'; upanīte[^303-7] pāpakena manasānupekkhamāno[^303-12] neva hatthiyāyī bhavissati[^303-13] na assayāyī[^303-14] na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhogabhogī, na mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasissati[^303-15]. Ko pana vādo yaṃ manussabhūtaṃ vajjhaṃ vadhāy'; upanītaṃ[^303-7] pāpakena manasānupekkhati, taṃ hi 'ssa[^303-16] bhikkhave hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjatī ti. XIX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Nādike[^303-17] viharati Giñjakāvasathe[^303-18]. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhik-


<!-- PTS Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 304]*

khavo ti. Bhadante[^304-1] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Maraṇasati[^304-2] bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Bhāvetha no tumhe bhikkhave maraṇasatin ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ahaṃ[^304-3] kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu[^304-4] vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

4. Aññataro[^304-5] pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

5. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

6. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattāro pañca[^304-6] ālope saṃkhāditvā[^304-7] ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu

##### *[PTS Page 305]*

vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

7. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ ālopaṃ saṃkhāditvā[^305-1] ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

8. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi passasitvā vā assasāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā>> ti, evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

9. Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā te[^305-2] bhikkhū etad avoca:-- Yvāyaṃ[^305-3] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti, yo cāyaṃ[^305-4] bhikkhave bhikkhu[^305-5] evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti, yo cāyaṃ[^305-4] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti, yo cāyaṃ[^305-4] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattāro pañca ālope saṃkhāditvā[^305-6] ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti;


<!-- PTS Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 306]*

ime vuccanti bhikkhave bhikkhū: pamattā[^306-1] viharanti, dandhaṃ[^306-2] maraṇasatiṃ bhāventi āsavānaṃ khayāya. Yo ca khvāyaṃ[^306-3] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ ālopaṃ saṃkhāditvā[^306-4] ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti, yo cāyaṃ[^306-5] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi passasitvā vā assasāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahu vata me kataṃ assā'; ti; ime vuccanti bhikkhave bhikkhū: appamattā viharanti, tikkhaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāventi āsavānaṃ khayāya. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

10. Appamattā viharissāma[^306-6], tikkhaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāvessāma[^306-7] āsavānaṃ khayāyā ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti. XX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Nādike[^306-8] viharati Giñjakāvasathe. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--

2. Maraṇasati bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Kathaṃ bhāvitā ca bhikkhave maraṇasati, kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu divase nikkhante rattiyā paṭihitāya[^306-9] iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘bahukā kho me paccayā maraṇassa: ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, vicchiko vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mam'assa antarāyo; upakkhalitvā vā papateyyaṃ[^306-10], bhattaṃ vā[^306-11] me bhuttaṃ vyāpajjeyya, pittaṃ vā me[^306-12]


<!-- PTS Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 307]*

kuppeyya[^307-1], semhaṃ vā me[^307-2] kuppeyya[^307-1], satthakā[^307-3] vā me vātā kuppeyyuṃ[^307-4], tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mam'[^307-5] assa antarāyo'; ti. Tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā iti paṭisañcikkhitabbaṃ ‘atthi nu kho me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu[^307-6] rattiṃ kālaṃ[^307-7] karontassa antarāyāyā' ti. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti ‘atthi me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu[^307-6] rattiṃ kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto[^307-8] chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ādittacelo vā ādittasīso vā tass'eva[^307-9] celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ ca vāyāmañ ca ussāhañ ca ussoḷhiñ ca appaṭivāniñ ca satiñ[^307-2] ca[^307-2] sampajaññañ ca kareyya, evam eva kho bhikkhave tena bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Sace pana bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti ‘natthi me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye[^307-2] me assu[^307-10] rattiṃ kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā ten'eva pītipāmujjena[^307-11] vihātabbaṃ ahorattānusikkhinā kusalesu dhammesu.

4. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu rattiyā nikkhantāya divase paṭihite[^307-12] iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘bahukā kho me paccayā maraṇassa: ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, vicchiko vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya, tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mam'assa antarāyo; upakkhalitvā vā papateyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā me bhuttaṃ vyāpajjeyya, pittaṃ vā me kuppeyya, semhaṃ[^307-13] vā[^307-13] me[^307-13] kuppeyya[^307-13], satthakā vā me kuppeyyuṃ, tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mam'assa antarāyo'; ti. Tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā iti paṭisañcik-

##### *[PTS Page 308]*

khitabbaṃ[^308-1] ‘atthi nu kho me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu[^308-2] divā[^308-3] kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā' ti. Sace[^308-4] bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti ‘atthi me[^308-5] pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu[^308-6] divā[^308-3] kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesaṃ[^308-7] yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ[^308-8]. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ādittacelo vā ādittasīso vā tass'eva celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ ca vāyāmañ ca ussāhañ ca ussoḷhiñ ca appaṭivāniñ ca satiñ ca sampajaññañ ca kareyya[^308-9], evam eva kho bhikkhave tena bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Sace pana bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti ‘natthi me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu[^308-6] divā kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā ten'eva pītipāmujjena[^308-10] vihātabbaṃ ahorattānusikkhinā kusalesu dhammesu. Evaṃ bhāvitā[^308-11] kho bhikkhave maraṇasati, evaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti. Sārāṇīyavaggo[^308-12] dutiyo. Tass'; uddānaṃ: Dve sārānīyā[^308-13] mettaṃ[^308-14] bhaddakaṃ[^308-15] ananutappiyaṃ[^308-16] Nakula[^308-17] maccha[^308-18] dve ca[^308-19] honti maraṇasatinā[^308-20] cā[^308-21] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 309]*

XXI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Sāmagāmake pokkharaṇiyāyaṃ[^309-1]. Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ pokkharaṇiyaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘tayo 'me bhante dhammā bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame tayo? Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā. Ime kho bhante tayo dhammā bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattantī'; ti. Idam avoca sā devatā. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho sā devatā ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ pokkharaṇiyaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave sā devatā maṃ etad avoca ‘tayo 'me bhante dhammā bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame tayo? Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā,niddārāmatā. Ime kho bhante tayo dhammā bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattantī' ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave sā[^309-2] devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi. Tesaṃ vo[^309-3] bhikkhave alābhā tesaṃ dulladdhaṃ, ye vo[^309-2] devatā pi jānanti kusalehi dhammehi parihānāya saṃvattamāne[^309-4]. Apare pi[^309-5] bhikkhave tayo parihāniye dhamme desessāmi[^309-6], taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho[^309-7] te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 310 -->
<a id="page-310"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 310]*

3. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo parihāniyā dhammā? Saṅgaṇikārāmatā, dovacassatā, pāpamittatā. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo parihāniyā dhammā.

4. Ye hi[^310-1] keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ parihāyiṃsu kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva chahi dhammehi[^310-2] parihāyiṃsu kusalehi dhammehi. Ye hi[^310-1] keci bhikkhave anāgatam addhānaṃ parihāyissanti kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva[^310-3] chahi dhammehi parihāyissanti kusalehi dhammehi. Ye hi pi[^310-4] keci bhikkhave etarahi parihāyanti kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva chahi dhammehi parihāyanti kusalehi dhammehī ti. XXII.

1. Cha[^310-5] yime[^310-6] bhikkhave[^310-7] aparihāniye dhamme desessāmi, taṃ suṇātha . . .[^310-8]

2. Katame ca bhikkhave cha aparihāniyā dhammā? Na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, sovacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha aparihāniyā dhammā.

3. Ye[^310-1] hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ na parihāyiṃsu kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva chahi dhammehi na parihāyiṃsu kusalehi dhammehi. Ye hi[^310-1] keci bhikkhave anāgatam addhānaṃ na parihāyissanti kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva chahi dhammehi na parihāyissanti kusalehi dhammehi. Ye pi[^310-4] hi[^310-9] keci bhikkhave etarahi na parihāyanti kusalehi dhammehi, sabbe te ime heva chahi dhammehi na parihāyanti kusalehi dhammehī ti. XXIII.

1. Bhayan ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ[^310-10], dukkhan ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, rogo ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, gaṇḍo ti bhik-


<!-- PTS Page 311 -->
<a id="page-311"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 311]*

khave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, saṅgo ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, paṅko ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ.

2. Kasmā ca bhikkhave bhayan ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ? Kāmarāgarattāyaṃ bhikkhave chandarāgavinibbaddho[^311-1] diṭṭhadhammikā pi bhayā na parimuccati, samparāyikā pi bhayā na parimuccati. Tasmā bhayan ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ.

3. Kasmā ca bhikkhave dukkhan ti . . . rogo ti . . . gaṇḍo[^311-2] ti[^311-2] . . . saṅgo ti . . . paṅko ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ? Kāmarāgarattāyaṃ bhikkhave chandarāgavinibaddho[^311-3] diṭṭhadhammikā pi paṅkā na parimuccati, samparāyikā[^311-2] pi[^311-2] paṅkā[^311-2] na[^311-2] parimuccati[^311-2]. Tasmā paṅko ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanan ti. Bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ[^311-4] rogo gaṇḍaṃ[^311-5] saṅgo paṅko ca ubhayaṃ[^311-6]: ete kāmā pavuccanti, yattha satto puthujjano. Upādāne bhayaṃ disvā jātimaraṇasambhave anupādā vimuccanti jātimaraṇasaṃkhaye. Te khemappattā[^311-7] sukhino diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā sabbaverabhayātītā[^311-8] sabbadukkhaṃ upaccagun ti. XXIV.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu Himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ padāleyya, ko pana vādo chavāya avijjāya. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu samādhissa samāpattikusalo hoti, samādhissa[^311-9] ṭhitikusalo[^311-10] hoti, samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalo[^311-11] hoti, samādhissa kallitākusalo[^311-12] hoti, samādhissa gocarakusalo hoti, samādhissa abhinīhārakusalo[^311-13] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 312 -->
<a id="page-312"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 312]*

Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu Himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ padāleyya, ko pana vādo chavāya avijjāyā ti. XXV.

1. Cha yimani[^312-1] bhikkhave anussatiṭṭhānāni[^312-2]. Katamāni cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Tathāgataṃ anussarati ‘iti pi so Bhagavā . . . pe . . . Sattā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samayena ariyasāvako Thathāgataṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapuriyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujagatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'; etam kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā[^312-4] evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati ‘svākkhāto[^312-5] Bhagavatā[^312-6] dhammo . . . pe[^312-3] . . . paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na[^312-7] dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ[^312-7] cittaṃ[^312-7] hoti[^312-7], na[^312-7] mohapuriyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti[^312-8], nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'; etam kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati supaṭipanno[^312-9] Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho . . . pe[^312-10] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave


<!-- PTS Page 313 -->
<a id="page-313"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 313]*

samaye ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako attano sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni[^313-1] . . . pe[^313-2] . . . samādhisaṃvattanikāni[^313-3]. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako sīlaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako attano cāgaṃ anussarati ‘lābhā vata me suladdhaṃ vata me[^313-4] . . . pe[^313-5] . . . yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato'; ti[^313-6]. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako cāgaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na[^313-7] mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ[^313-7] cittaṃ[^313-7] hoti[^313-7], ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako devatā anussarati ‘santi devā Cātummahārājikā[^313-8], santi devā Tāvatiṃsā, santi devā Yāmā, santi devā Tusitā, santi devā Nimmānaratino, santi devā Paranimmitavasavattino, santi devā


<!-- PTS Page 314 -->
<a id="page-314"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 314]*

Brahmakāyikā, santi devā Tatuttari[^314-1]; yathārūpāya saddhāya samannāgatā tā[^314-2] devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^314-3], mayham pi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvijjati; yathārūpena sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya samannāgatā tā[^314-4] devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^314-3], mayham pi tathārūpā paññā saṃvijjatī'; ti. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye ariyasāvako attano ca tāsañ ca devatānaṃ saddhañ ca sīlañ ca sutañ ca cāgañ ca paññañ ca anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho bhikkhave pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idam pi kho bhikkhave ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visujjhanti. Imāni kho bhikkhave[^314-5] cha anussatiṭṭhānānī ti. XXVI.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Mahākaccāno bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^314-6] ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahākaccānassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahākaccāno etad avoca:--

2. Acchariyaṃ āvuso abbhutaṃ āvuso, yāvañ c'; idaṃ[^314-7] tena Bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena sambādhe okāsādhigamo anubuddho sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ[^314-8] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^314-9] ñāyassa[^314-10] adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ[^314-11] cha anussatiṭṭhānāni. Katamāni cha?

3. Idhāvuso ariyasāvako Tathāgataṃ anussarati ‘iti pi so Bhagavā . . . pe[^314-12] . . . Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye ariyasāvako Tathāgataṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na


<!-- PTS Page 315 -->
<a id="page-315"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 315]*

mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ[^315-1] muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa[^315-2] kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati ‘svākkhāto[^315-3] Bhagavatā dhammo . . . pe[^315-4] . . . paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye ariyasāvako dhammaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ[^315-5] muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa[^315-6] kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati ‘supaṭipanno[^315-7] Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho . . . pe[^315-8] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā'; ti. Yasmiṃ samaye ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ[^315-9] muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso


<!-- PTS Page 316 -->
<a id="page-316"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 316]*

ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso ariyasāvako attano sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni[^316-1] . . . pe[^316-2] . . . samādhisaṃvattanikāni. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye ariyasāvako sīlaṃ anussarati, nev' assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ[^316-3] muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso ariyasāvako attano cāgaṃ anussarati ‘lābhā vata me suladdhaṃ vata me[^316-4] . . . pe[^316-5] . . . yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato'; ti. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye ariyasāvako cāgaṃ anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na[^316-6] dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na[^316-6] mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti.

8. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso ariyasāvako devatā anussarati ‘santi devā Cātummahārājika[^316-7], santi devā . . . pe[^316-8] . . . Tatuttari[^316-9]; yathārūpāya saddhāya samannāgatā tā devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^316-10], mayham pi tathārūpā saddhā saṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 317]*

vijjati; yathārūpena sīlena . . . pe . . . sutena cāgena paññāya samannāgatā tā devatā ito cutā tattha upapannā[^317-1], mayham pi tathārūpā paññā saṃvijjati'; ti. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye ariyasāvako attano ca tāsañ ca devatānaṃ saddhañ ca sīlañ ca sutañ ca cāgañ ca paññañ ca anussarati, nev'assa tasmiṃ samaye rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na dosapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, na mohapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hoti, ujugatam ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gedhamhā, gedho ti kho āvuso pañcann'etaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Sa kho so āvuso ariyasāvako sabbaso ākāsasamena cetasā viharati vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Idam pi kho āvuso ārammaṇaṃ karitvā evam idh'; ekacce sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti. Acchariyaṃ āvuso abbhutaṃ āvuso, yāvañ c'; idaṃ tena Bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena sambādhe okāsādhigamo anubuddho sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ[^317-2] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^317-3] ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ imāni cha anussatiṭṭhānānī ti. XXVII.

1. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kati nu kho bhante samayā manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti?

2. Cha yime bhikkhu samayā manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Katame cha?

3. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa


<!-- PTS Page 318 -->
<a id="page-318"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 318]*

ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa[^318-1] manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu paṭhamo samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi vyāpādaparetena, uppannassa ca vyāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā vyāpādassa pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu vyāpādassa pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu dutiyo samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi thīnamiddhaparetena, uppannassa ca thīnamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā thīnamiddhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa[^318-1] manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu thīnamiddhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu tatiyo samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi uddhaccakukkuccaparetena, uppannassa ca


<!-- PTS Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 319]*

uddhaccakukkuccassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā uddhaccakukkuccassa pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu uddhaccakukkuccassa pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu catuttho samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi vicikicchāparetena, uppannāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā vicikicchāya pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu vicikicchāya pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu pañcamo samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikaroto anantarā āsavānaṃ khayo hoti, taṃ nimittaṃ na jānāti[^319-1] na passati, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikaroto anantarā āsavānaṃ khayo hoti, taṃ nimittaṃ na jānāmi[^319-2] na passāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu chaṭṭho samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Ime kho bhikkhu cha samayā manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya[^319-3] upasaṅkamitun ti[^319-4].


<!-- PTS Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 320]*

XXVIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā therā bhikkhū Bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti Isipatane Migadāye. Atha kho tesaṃ therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantānaṃ maṇḍalamāle[^320-1] sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘ko no kho āvuso samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti?

2. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘yasmiṃ āvuso samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto pāde pakkhāletvā nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā[^320-2] ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, so samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ etad avoca ‘na kho[^320-3] āvuso so[^320-4] samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto pāde pakkhāletvā nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, cārittakilamatho pi 'ssa, tasmiṃ samaye appaṭippassaddho hoti, bhattakilamatho[^320-5] pi 'ssa, tasmiṃ samaye appaṭippassaddho hoti. Tasmā so asamayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Yasmiṃ[^320-6] āvuso samaye[^320-7] manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu[^320-8] sāyaṇhasamayaṃ[^320-9] paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito vihārapacchāyāyaṃ nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā[^320-10] ujuṃ kāyaṃ[^320-11] paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, so samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti.

4. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ etad avoca ‘na kho āvuso so[^320-12] samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye


<!-- PTS Page 321 -->
<a id="page-321"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 321]*

manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito vihārapacchāyāyaṃ nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā[^321-1] ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, yad ev'assa[^321-2] divā samādhinimittaṃ manasikataṃ hoti, tad ev'assa tasmiṃ samaye samudācarati. Tasmā so asamayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Yasmiṃ[^321-3] āvuso samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, so samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti.

5. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ etad avoca ‘na kho āvuso so[^321-4] samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Yasmiṃ āvuso samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā, ojaṭṭhāyi 'ssa[^321-5] tasmiṃ samaye kāyo hoti, phāsu 'ssa[^321-6] hoti buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ. Tasmā so asamayo[^321-7] manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti.

6. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Mahākaccāno there[^321-8] bhikkhū etad avoca:-- Sammukhā me taṃ āvuso Bhagavato sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ: cha yime[^321-9] bhikkhu samayā manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Katame cha?

7. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharāmi kāmarāgaparetena, uppannassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāmi, sādhu


<!-- PTS Page 322 -->
<a id="page-322"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 322]*

vata me āyasmā kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ desetū' ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu paṭhamo samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati . . . pe[^322-1] . . . thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati . . . uddhaccakukkuccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati . . . vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā viharati . . .[^322-2] yaṃ[^322-3] nimittaṃ[^322-3] āgamma[^322-3] yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikaroto anantarā āsavānaṃ khayo hoti, taṃ[^322-3] nimittaṃ na jānāti na passati, tasmiṃ samaye manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā evam assa vacanīyo ‘ahaṃ kho āvuso yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikaroto anantarā āsavānaṃ khayo hoti, taṃ nimittaṃ na jānāmi na passāmi, sādhu vata me āyasmā āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ desetū'; ti, tassa manobhāvanīyo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ deseti. Ayaṃ bhikkhu chaṭṭho samayo manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ. Sammukhā me taṃ āvuso Bhagavato sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ: ime kho bhikkhu cha samayā manobhāvanīyassa bhikkhuno dassanāya upasaṅkamitun ti. XXIX.

1. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Udāyiṃ āmantesi ‘kati nu kho Udāyi anussatiṭṭhānānī'; ti? Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Udāyi tuṇhī ahosi. Dutiyam pi kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Udāyiṃ āmantesi ‘kati nu kho Udāyi anussatiṭṭhānānī'; ti? Dutiyam[^322-4] pi āyasmā Udāyi tuṇhī ahosi. Tatiyam pi kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Udāyiṃ āmantesi ‘kati nu kho Udāyi anussatiṭṭhānānī'; ti? Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Udāyi tuṇhī ahosi. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando āyasmantaṃ Udāyiṃ etad avoca ‘Satthā taṃ āvuso Udāyi āmantetī[^322-5]'; ti. ‘Suṇām'; ahaṃ[^322-6] āvuso Ānanda Bhagavato.


<!-- PTS Page 323 -->
<a id="page-323"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 323]*

Idha bhante bhikkhu anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^323-1] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati. Idaṃ[^323-2] bhante anussatiṭṭhānan'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi ‘aññāsiṃ[^323-3] kho[^323-3] ahaṃ[^323-3] Ānanda <<nevāyaṃ[^323-4] Udāyi moghapuriso adhicittam anuyutto viharatī>> ti. Kati nu kho Ānanda anussatiṭṭhānānānī'; ti? Pañca bhante anussatiṭṭhānāni. -- Katamāni pañca?

2. Idha bhante bhikkhu vivicc'eva kāmehi . . . pe[^323-5] . . . tatiyaṃ[^323-6] jhānaṃ[^323-6] upasampajja viharati. Idaṃ bhante anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhante bhikkhu ālokasaññaṃ manasikaroti, divāsaññaṃ adhiṭṭhāti: yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā. Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ[^323-7] cittaṃ bhāveti. Idaṃ bhante anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ ñāṇadassanapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhante bhikkhu imam eva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhokesamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa[^323-8] asucino paccavekkhati ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāye kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nahārū[^323-9] aṭṭhī aṭṭhimiñjaṃ[^323-10] vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilomakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ sedo medo assu vasā khelo[^323-11] siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttan'; ti. Idaṃ bhante anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ kāmarāgassa[^323-12] pahānāya[^323-12] saṃvattati.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhante bhikkhu seyyathā pi passeyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya[^323-13] chaḍḍitaṃ ekāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ


<!-- PTS Page 324 -->
<a id="page-324"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 324]*

vā tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajātaṃ, so imam eva kāyaṃ evaṃ[^324-1] upasaṃharati ‘ayam pi kho kāyo evaṃdhammo evaṃbhāvī evaṃ-anatīto'; ti; seyyathā[^324-2] pi[^324-2] vā[^324-3] pana passeyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ kākehi vā khajjamānaṃ kulalehi[^324-4] vā[^324-4] khajjamānaṃ[^324-4] gijjhehi vā khajjamānaṃ suvānehi[^324-5] vā khajjamānaṃ sigālehi[^324-6] vā khajjamānaṃ vividhehi vā pāṇakajātehi khajjamānaṃ, so imam eva kāyaṃ evaṃ upasaṃharati ‘ayam pi kho kāyo evaṃdhammo evaṃbhāvī evaṃ[^324-1]-anatīto'; ti; seyyathā pi[^324-7] vā pana passeyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ samaṃsalohitaṃ nahārusambandhaṃ[^324-8], aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ nimmaṃsalohitamakkhitaṃ[^324-9] nahārusambandhaṃ[^324-8], aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ[^324-10] apagatamaṃsalohitaṃ nahārusambandhaṃ[^324-8], aṭṭhikāni apagatasambandhāni disā vidisā vikkhittāni aññena[^324-4] hatthaṭṭhikaṃ[^324-4] aññena pādaṭṭhikaṃ aññena jaṅghaṭṭhikaṃ aññena ūruṭṭhikaṃ[^324-11] aññena kaṭiṭṭhikaṃ[^324-12] aññena piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ[^324-13] aññena sīsakaṭāhaṃ, so[^324-14] imam eva kāyaṃ evaṃ upasaṃharati ‘ayam pi kho kāyo evaṃdhammo evaṃbhāvī evaṃ[^324-15]- anatīto'; ti; seyyathā pi[^324-16] vā pana passeyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhikāni setāni saṅkhavaṇṇūpanibhāni[^324-17], aṭṭhikāni puñjakatāni[^324-18], aṭṭhikāni[^324-16] terovassikāni[^324-19] pūtīni[^324-20] cuṇṇakajātāni[^324-21], so imam eva kāyaṃ evaṃ upasaṃharati


<!-- PTS Page 325 -->
<a id="page-325"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 325]*

‘ayam pi kho kāyo evaṃdhammo evaṃbhāvī evaṃ[^325-1]-anatīto'; ti. Idaṃ bhante anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ asmimānasamugghātāya saṃvattati.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhante bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā[^325-2] . . . pe[^325-3] . . . catutthaṃ[^325-4] jhānaṃ[^325-4] upasampajja viharati. Idaṃ[^325-5] bhante anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ anekadhātupaṭivedhāya saṃvattati. Imāni kho bhante pañca anussatiṭṭhānānī ti.

7. Sādhu sādhu Ānanda, tena hi tvaṃ Ānanda idam pi chaṭṭhaṃ anussatiṭṭhānaṃ dhārehi[^325-6]: idh'; Ānanda bhikkhu sato 'va[^325-7] abhikkamati, sato 'va[^325-8] paṭikkamati, sato 'va[^325-8] tiṭṭhati, sato 'va[^325-8] nisīdati, sato 'va[^325-8] seyyaṃ kappeti, sato 'va kammaṃ[^325-9] adhiṭṭhāti. Idaṃ Ānanda anussatiṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ bhāvitaṃ evaṃ bahulīkataṃ satisampajaññāya[^325-10] saṃvattatī ti. XXX.

1. Cha yimāni[^325-11] bhikkhave anuttariyāni. Katamāni cha?

2. Dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave dassanānuttariyaṃ?

3. Idha bhikkhave ekacco hatthiratanam pi dassanāya gacchati, assaratanam pi dassanāya gacchati, maṇiratanam pi dassanāya gacchati, uccāvacaṃ[^325-12] vā pana[^325-8] dassanāya gacchati, samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipannaṃ dassanāya gacchati. Atth'etaṃ bhikkhave dassanaṃ? N'etaṃ natthī ti vadāmi, tañ ca kho etaṃ bhikkhave dassanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pothujjanikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na


<!-- PTS Page 326 -->
<a id="page-326"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 326]*

nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yo ca kho bhikkhave Tathāgataṃ vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā dassanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^326-1] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave dassanānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ[^326-2] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^326-3] ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgataṃ vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā dassanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^326-1] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dassanānuttariyaṃ. Iti dassanānuttariyaṃ. Savanānuttariyañ ca kathaṃ hoti?

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco bherisaddam[^326-4] pi savanāya gacchati, vīnāsaddam[^326-4] pi[^326-5] savanāya gacchati, gītasaddam[^326-4] pi savanāya gacchati, uccāvacaṃ[^326-6] vā pana savanāya gacchati, samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā micchādiṭṭhikassa micchāpaṭipannassa dhammasavanāya gacchati. Atth'etaṃ bhikkhave savanaṃ? N'etaṃ natthī ti vadāmi, tañ ca kho etaṃ bhikkhave savanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pothujjanikaṃ[^326-7] anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yo ca kho[^326-5] bhikkhave Tathāgatassa vā Tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammasavanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^326-1] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave savanānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgatassa vā Tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammasavanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^326-1] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave savanānuttariyaṃ. Iti dassanānuttariyaṃ[^326-8] {savanānuttariyaṃ}. Lābhānuttariyañ ca kathaṃ hoti?

5. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puttalābham pi labhati, dāralābham pi labhati, dhanalābham pi labhati, uccāvacaṃ vā


<!-- PTS Page 327 -->
<a id="page-327"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 327]*

pana lābhaṃ[^327-1] labhati, samaṇe vā brāhmaṇe vā micchādiṭṭhike micchāpaṭipanne saddhaṃ paṭilabhati[^327-2]. Atth'; eso bhikkhave lābho? N'eso natthī ti vadāmi, so ca kho eso bhikkhave lābho hīno gammo pothujjaniko[^327-3] anariyo anatthasaṃhito na nibbidāya[^327-4] na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yo ca kho bhikkhave Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāvake vā saddhaṃ paṭilabhati[^327-5] niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^327-6] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave lābhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāvake vā saddhaṃ paṭilabhati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^327-6] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave lābhānuttariyaṃ. Iti dassanānuttatiyaṃ savanānuttariyaṃ lābhānuttariyaṃ. Sikkhānuttariyañ ca kathaṃ hoti?

6. Idha bhikkhave ekacco hatthismim pi sikkhati, assasmim pi sikkhati, rathasmim pi sikkhati, dhanusmiṃ[^327-7] pi[^327-7] sikkhati[^327-7], tharusmim[^327-8] pi sikkhati, uccāvacaṃ vā pana sikkhati, samaṇassa vā[^327-9] brāhmaṇassa vā micchādiṭṭhikassa micchāpaṭipannassa[^327-10] sikkhati. Atth'; esā bhikkhave sikkhā? N'; esā natthī ti vadāmi, sā ca kho esā bhikkhave sikkhā hīnā gammā pothujjanikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yo ca kho bhikkhave Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye adhisīlam pi sikkhati adhicittam pi sikkhati adhipaññam pi sikkhati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^327-6] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave sikkhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa


<!-- PTS Page 328 -->
<a id="page-328"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 328]*

sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye adhisīlam pi sikkhati adhicittam pi sikkhati adhipaññam pi sikkhati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^328-1] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sikkhānuttariyaṃ. Iti dassanānuttariyaṃ savanānuttariyaṃ lābhānuttariyaṃ sikkhānuttariyaṃ. Pāricariyānuttariyañ ca kathaṃ hoti?

7. Idha bhikkhave ekacco khattiyam pi paricarati, brāhmaṇam pi paricarati, gahapatim[^328-2] pi paricarati, uccāvacaṃ vā pana paricarati, samaṇaṃ vā[^328-3] brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipannaṃ paricarati. Atth'; esā bhikkhave pāricariyā? N'; esā natthī ti vadāmi, sā ca kho esā bhikkhave pāricariyā hīnā gammā pothujjanikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā, na nibbidāya . . . pe[^328-4] . . . na nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yo ca kho bhikkhave Tathāgataṃ vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā paricarati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^328-1] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave pāricariyānaṃ sattānaṃ[^328-3] visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgataṃ vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā paricarati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^328-1] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave pāricariyānuttariyaṃ. Iti dassanānuttariyaṃ savanānuttariyaṃ lābhānuttariyaṃ sikkhānuttariyaṃ pāricariyānuttariyaṃ. Anussatānuttariyañ ca kathaṃ hoti?

8. Idha bhikkhave ekacco puttalābham pi anussarati, dāralābham pi anussarati, dhanalābham pi anussarati, uccāvacaṃ vā pana lābhaṃ[^328-5] anussarati, samaṇaṃ vā[^328-6] brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipannaṃ anussarati. Atth'; esā bhikkhave anussati? N'; esā natthī ti vadāmi, sā ca kho esā bhikkhave anussati hīnā gammā pothujjanikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na[^328-7] nirodhāya[^328-7] na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbanāya saṃvattati, yo ca kho bhikkhave Tathāgataṃ


<!-- PTS Page 329 -->
<a id="page-329"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 329]*

vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā anussarati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^329-1] abhippasanno: etad ānuttariyaṃ bhikkhave anussatīnaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yad idaṃ Tathāgataṃ vā Tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā anussarati niviṭṭhasaddho niviṭṭhapemo ekantagato[^329-2] abhippasanno. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave anussatānuttariyaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha anuttariyānī ti. Ye[^329-3] dassanavaraṃ[^329-3] laddhā savanañ[^329-4] ca anuttaraṃ[^329-5], lābhānuttariyaṃ laddhā, sikkhānuttariye[^329-6] ratā[^329-7], upaṭṭhitā pāricariye[^329-8], bhāvayanti anussatiṃ[^329-9] vivekapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ khemaṃ amatagāminiṃ[^329-10], appamāde[^329-11] pamuditā[^329-12] nipakā sīlasaṃvutā: te ve kālena paccenti[^329-13], yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatī ti. Anuttariyavaggo[^329-14] tatiyo. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^329-15]: Sāmako[^329-16] aparihāniyo[^329-17] bhayaṃ[^329-18] Himavānussati[^329-19] Kaccāno dve ca[^329-20] samayā[^329-21] Udāyi anuttariyenā ti[^329-22]. XXXI.

1. Cha yime[^329-23] bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa[^329-24] bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha?


<!-- PTS Page 330 -->
<a id="page-330"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 330]*

2. Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu aguttadvāratā, bhojane amattaññutā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā sekhassa[^330-1] bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti.

3. Cha yime[^330-2] bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa[^330-1] bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha?

4. Na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu guttadvāratā, bhojane mattaññutā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā sekhassa[^330-1] bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī ti. XXXII.

1. Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi[^330-3]. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘cha yime[^330-2] bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā[^330-4]. Ime kho bhante cha dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī'; ti. Idam avoca sā devatā. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho sā devatā ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi ‘imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave sā devatā maṃ etad avoca <<cha[^330-5] yime[^330-2] bhante dhammā bhikkhuno[^330-6] aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā.


<!-- PTS Page 331 -->
<a id="page-331"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 331]*

Ime kho bhante cha dhammā bhikkhuno[^331-1] aparihānāya saṃvattantī>> ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave sā devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhinaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyī'; ti. Satthugaru dhammagaru saṅghe ca tibbagāravo appamādagaru[^331-2] bhikkhu paṭisanthāragāravo abhabbo parihānāya nibbānass'eva santike ti. XXXIII.

1. Imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave sā devatā maṃ etad avoca ‘cha yime[^331-3] bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, hirigāravatā, ottappagāravatā. Ime kho bhante cha dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī'; ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave sā devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyī ti. Satthugaru dhammagaru saṅghe ca tibbagāravo hiri-ottappasampanno[^331-4] sappatisso[^331-5] sagāravo abhabbo[^331-6] parihānāya nibbānass'eva santike ti. XXXIV.
