1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho āyasmato Mahāmoggallānassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi[^331-7] ‘katamesānaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha[^331-8] avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhi-


<!-- PTS Page 332 -->
<a id="page-332"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 332]*

pārāyanā'; ti? Tena kho pana samayena Tisso nāma bhikkhu adhunā kālakato aññataraṃ Brahmalokaṃ upapanno[^332-1] hoti. Tatra[^332-2] pi naṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘Tisso Brahmā mahiddhiko mahānubhāvo'; ti.

2. Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^332-3] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva Jetavane antarahito tasmiṃ Brahmaloke pāturahosi. Addasā kho Tisso Brahmā āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvā[^332-4] āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ etad avoca ‘ehi kho mārisa Moggallāna, svāgataṃ[^332-5] mārisa Moggallāna, cirassaṃ kho mārisa Moggallāna imaṃ pariyāyam akāsi, yad idaṃ idhāgamanāya, nisīda mārisa Moggallāna, idam āsanaṃ paññattan'; ti. Nisīdi kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno paññatte āsane. Tisso pi Brahmā āyasmantaṃ Mahāmoggallānaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Tissaṃ Brahmānaṃ āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno etad avoca ‘katamesānaṃ kho Tissa devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti? ‘Cātummahārājikānaṃ[^332-6] kho marisa Moggallāna devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti. ‘Sabbesañ ñeva[^332-7] nu[^332-8] kho Tissa Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti? ‘Na kho mārisa Moggallāna sabbesaṃ Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā 'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti. Ye kho[^332-9] te mārisa Moggallāna Cātummahārājikā devā buddhe aveccappasādena asamannāgatā[^332-10] dhamme aveccappasādena asamannāgatā saṅghe[^332-11] aveccappasādena[^332-11] asamannāgatā[^332-11] ariyakantehi sīlehi asamannāgatā, tesaṃ na[^332-12] evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 333 -->
<a id="page-333"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 333]*

ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti. Ye ca[^333-1] kho te[^333-2] mārisa Moggallāna Cātummahārājikā devā buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā dhamme[^333-3] aveccappasādena[^333-3] samannāgatā[^333-3] saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgatā ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgatā, tesaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti. ‘Cātummahārājikānañ[^333-4] ñeva nu[^333-5] kho Tissa devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā 'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti, udāhu Tāvatiṃsānam pi devānaṃ . . .[^333-6] Yāmānam pi devānaṃ . . . Tusitānam pi devānaṃ . . . Nimmānaratīnam pi devānaṃ . . . Paranimmitavasavattīnam pi devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti? ‘Paranimmitavasavattīnam pi[^333-2] kho mārisa Moggallāna devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā 'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti. Sabbesañ ñeva nu kho Tissa Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti? ‘Na kho mārisa Moggallāna sabbesaṃ[^333-7] Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti. Ye kho te mārisa Moggallāna Paranimmitavasavattī[^333-8] devā buddhe aveccappasādena asamannāgatā dhamme aveccappasādena asamannāgatā saṅghe aveccappasādena asamannāgatā ariyakantehi sīlehi asamannāgatā, tesaṃ na[^333-9] devānaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti. Ye ca kho te mārisa Moggallāna Paranimmitavasavattī[^333-8] devā buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā dhamme aveccappasādena samannāgatā saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgatā ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgatā, tesaṃ evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti: sotāpannā'mha avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno Tissassa Brahmuno bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā, seyyathā pi nāma


<!-- PTS Page 334 -->
<a id="page-334"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 334]*

balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva Brahmaloke antarahito Jetavane pāturahosī ti. XXXV.

1. Cha yime[^334-1] bhikkhave dhammā vijjābhāgiyā. Katame cha?

2. Aniccasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā[^334-2], pahānasaññā, virāgasaññā, nirodhasaññā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā vijjābhāgiyā ti. XXXVI.

1. Cha yimāni[^334-3] bhikkhave vivādamūlāni. Katamāni cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kodhano hoti upanāhī. Yo so[^334-4] bhikkhave bhikkhu kodhano hoti upanāhī, so Satthari pi agāravo viharati appatisso[^334-5], dhamme pi agāravo viharati appatisso, saṅghe pi agāravo viharati appatisso, sikkhāya pi na paripūrakārī hoti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari[^334-6] agāravo viharati appatisso, dhamme agāravo viharati appatisso, saṅghe agāravo viharati appatisso, sikkhāya[^334-6] na paripūrakārī[^334-7], so saṅghe vivādaṃ janeti, so[^334-8] hoti vivādo bahujanāhitāya[^334-9] bahujanāsukhāya[^334-10] bahuno[^334-11] janassa anatthāya[^334-12] ahitāya dukkhāya devamanussānaṃ. Evarūpañ ce tumhe bhikkhave vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupasseyyātha, tatra tumhe bhikkhave tass'eva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyameyyātha. Evarūpañ ce tumhe bhikkhave vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupasseyyātha[^334-13], tatra tumhe bhikkhave tass'eva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassa-


<!-- PTS Page 335 -->
<a id="page-335"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 335]*

vāya[^335-1] paṭipajjeyyātha. Evam etassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hoti. Evam etassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavo[^335-2] hoti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu makkhī hoti paḷāsī . . .[^335-3] issukī hoti maccharī . . . saṭho[^335-4] hoti māyāvī . . . pāpiccho hoti micchādiṭṭhi . . . sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī[^335-5] hoti ādhānagāhī[^335-6] duppaṭinissaggī. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hoti ādhānagāhī duppaṭinissaggī, so Satthari pi agāravo viharati appatisso, dhamme pi agāravo viharati appatisso, saṅghe pi agāravo viharati appatisso, sikkhāya pi na paripūrakārī hoti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari[^335-7] agāravo viharati appatisso, dhamme[^335-8] . . . pe[^335-9] . . . saṅghe agāravo viharati appatisso, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, so saṅghe vivādaṃ janeti, so[^335-10] hoti vivādo bahujanāhitāya[^335-11] bahujanāsukhāya[^335-12] bahuno[^335-13] janassa anatthāya[^335-14] ahitāya dukkhāya devamanussānaṃ. Evarūpañ ce tumhe bhikkhave vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupasseyyātha, tatra tumhe bhikkhave tass'eva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa[^335-15] pahānāya vāyameyyātha. Evarūpañ ce tumhe bhikkhave vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupasseyyātha, tatra tumhe bhikkhave tass'eva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya[^335-1] paṭipajjeyyātha. Evam etassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hoti. Evam etassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavo[^335-16] hoti. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha vivādamūlānī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 336 -->
<a id="page-336"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 336]*

XXXVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa[^336-1] ārāme[^336-1]. Tena kho pana samayena Veḷukaṇḍakī[^336-2] Nandamātā upāsikā Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukhe bhikkhusaṅghe chaḷaṅgasamannāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpeti. Addasā kho Bhagavā dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena Veḷukaṇḍakiṃ Nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukhe bhikkhusaṅghe chaḷaṅgasamannāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpentiṃ, disvā[^336-3] bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Esā bhikkhave Veḷukaṇḍakī Nandamātā upāsikā Sāriputta-Moggallānapamukhe bhikkhusaṅghe chaḷaṅgasamannāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpeti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave chaḷaṅgasamannāgatā dakkhiṇā hoti?

2. Idha bhikkhave dāyakassa tīṇ'; aṅgāni honti, paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇ'; aṅgāni. Katamāni dāyakassa tīṇ'; aṅgāni? Idha bhikkhave dāyako pubb'eva dānā sumano hoti, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādeti, datvā attamano hoti. Imāni dāyakassa tīṇ'; aṅgāni. Katamāni paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇ'; aṅgāni? Idha bhikkhave paṭiggāhakā vītarāgā vā honti rāgavinayāya vā paṭipannā, vītadosā vā honti dosavinayāya vā paṭipannā, vītamohā[^336-4] vā honti mohavinayāya vā paṭipannā. Imāni paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇ'; aṅgāni. Iti dāyakassa tīṇ'; aṅgāni, paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇ'; aṅgāni. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave chaḷaṅgasamannāgatā dakkhiṇā hoti.

3. Evaṃ chaḷaṅgasamannāgatāya bhikkhave dakkhiṇāya na sukaraṃ puññassa pamāṇaṃ gahetuṃ[^336-5] ‘ettako puññābhisando kusalābhisando[^336-6] sukhassāhāro sovaggiko sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī'; ti. Atha kho asaṃkheyyo appameyyo, mahāpuññakkhandho tveva[^336-7] saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave mahāsamudde na sukaraṃ udakassa pamā-


<!-- PTS Page 337 -->
<a id="page-337"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 337]*

ṇaṃ gahetuṃ ‘ettakāni[^337-1] udakāḷhakānī'; ti vā[^337-2] ‘ettakāni[^337-3] udakāḷhakasatānī'; ti vā ‘ettakāni[^337-4] udakāḷhakasahassānī'; ti vā ‘ettakāni[^337-1] udakāḷhakasatasahassānī'; ti vā. Atha kho asaṃkheyyo appameyyo, mahā-udakakkhandho tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchati. Evam eva kho bhikkhave evaṃ chaḷaṅgasamannāgatāya dakkhiṇāya na sukaraṃ puññassa pamāṇaṃ gahetuṃ ‘ettako puññābhisando kusalābhisando[^337-5] sukhassāhāro sovaggiko sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī'; ti. Atha kho asaṃkheyyo appameyyo, mahāpuññakkhandho tveva saṃkhaṃ gacchatī ti. Pubb'eva dānā sumano, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādaye[^337-6], datvā attamano hoti: esā[^337-7] yaññassa[^337-8] sampadā. Vītarāgā[^337-9] vītadosā vītamohā anāsavā[^337-10]: khettaṃ yaññassa sampannaṃ saññatā brahmacārayo[^337-11]. Sayaṃ ācamayitvāna[^337-12] datvā sakehi pāṇibhi attano parato c'; eso[^337-13] yañño hoti mahapphalo. Evaṃ yajitvā medhāvī saddho muttena cetasā avyāpajjhaṃ sukhaṃ lokaṃ[^337-14] paṇḍito upapajjatī ti. XXXVIII.

1. Atha kho aññataro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā[^337-1] ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^337-15] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Ahaṃ hi bho Gotama evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhi: Natthi attakāro, natthi parakāro ti. ‘Māhaṃ brāhmaṇa evaṃvādiṃ[^337-16] evaṃdiṭṭhiṃ[^337-16] addasaṃ vā assosiṃ vā, kathaṃ hi


<!-- PTS Page 338 -->
<a id="page-338"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 338]*

nāma sayaṃ[^338-1] abhikkamanto[^338-1] sayaṃ paṭikkamanto evaṃ vakkhati: Natthi attakāro, natthi parakāro ti? Taṃ kiṃ maññasi brāhmaṇa, atthi ārabbhadhātū'; ti? Evaṃ bho. ‘Ārabbhadhātuyā sati ārabbhavanto sattā paññāyantī'; ti? Evaṃ bho. ‘Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa ārabbhadhātuyā sati ārabbhavanto sattā paññāyanti[^338-2], ayaṃ sattānaṃ attakāro, ayaṃ parakāro[^338-3]. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi brāhmaṇa, atthi nikkamadhātu[^338-4] . . . pe[^338-5] . . . atthi parakkamadhātu . . . atthi thāmadhātu . . .atthi ṭhitidhātu[^338-6] . . . atthi upakkamadhātū'; ti? Evaṃ bho. ‘Upakkamadhātuyā[^338-7] sati upakkamavanto sattā paññāyantī'; ti[^338-8]? Evaṃ bho. ‘Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa upakkamadhātuyā[^338-8] sati[^338-8] upakkamavanto sattā paññāyanti, ayaṃ sattānaṃ attakāro, ayaṃ parakāro[^338-9]. Māhaṃ brāhmaṇa evaṃvādiṃ evaṃditthiṃ addasaṃ vā assosiṃ vā, kathaṃ hi nāma sayaṃ abhikkamanto sayaṃ paṭikkamanto evaṃ vakkhati: Natthi attakāro, natthi parakāro'; ti? Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . .pe[^338-10] . . . ajjat-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. XXXIX.

1. Tīṇ 'imāni[^338-11] bhikkhave nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya. Katamāni tīṇi?

2. Lobho nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, doso nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, moho nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya. Na bhikkhave lobhā alobho samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave lobhā lobho 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave dosā adoso samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave dosā doso 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave mohā amoho samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave mohā moho 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave lobha-


<!-- PTS Page 339 -->
<a id="page-339"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 339]*

jena kammena dosajena kammena mohajena kammena devā paññāyanti, manussā paññāyanti, yā[^339-1] vā pan'; aññā pi kāci sugatiyo[^339-2], atha kho bhikkhave lobhajena kammena dosajena kammena mohajena kammena nirayo paññāyati, tiracchānayoni paññāyati, pettivisayo[^339-3] paññāyati, yā[^339-1] vā pan'; aññā pi kāci duggatiyo[^339-2]. Imāni[^339-4] kho bhikkhave tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya.

3. Tīṇ'imāni bhikkhave nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya. Katamāni tīṇi?

4. Alobho nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, adoso nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, amoho nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya. Na bhikkhave alobhā lobho samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave alobhā alobho 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave adosā doso samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave adosā adoso 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave amohā moho samudeti, atha kho bhikkhave amohā amoho 'va samudeti. Na bhikkhave alobhajena kammena adosajena kammena amohajena kammena nirayo paññāyati, tiracchānayoni paññāyati, pettivisayo[^339-5] paññāyati, yā[^339-1] vā pan'; aññā pi kāci duggatiyo[^339-2], atha kho bhikkhave alobhajena kammena adosajena kammena amohajena kammena devā paññāyanti, manussā paññāyanti, yā[^339-1] vā pan'; aññā pi kāci sugatiyo[^339-2]. Imāni kho bhikkhave tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāyā ti. XL.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kimbilāyaṃ[^339-6] viharati Veḷuvane. Atha kho āyasmā Kimbilo[^339-7] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Kimbilo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 340 -->
<a id="page-340"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 340]*

2. Ko nu kho bhante hetu, ko paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti?

3. Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo Satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhamme[^340-1] agāravā viharanti appatissā[^340-2], saṅghe agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, appamāde agāravā viharanti appatissā, paṭisanthāre[^340-3] agāravā viharanti appatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu, ayaṃ paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti.

4. Ko pana bhante hetu, ko panayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti?

5. Idha Kimbila Tathāgate parinibbute bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo Satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhamme sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghe sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, appamāde sagāravā viharanti sappatissā paṭisanthāre[^340-3] sagāravā viharanti sappatissā. Ayaṃ kho Kimbila hetu, ayaṃ paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti. XLI.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā[^340-4] Sāriputto Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya sambahulehi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ Gijjhakūṭā pabbatā orohanto[^340-5] addasa[^340-6] aññatarasmiṃ padese mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ, disvā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Passatha no tumhe[^340-7] āvuso amuṃ mahantaṃ dārukkhandhan ti? Evam āvuso ti.

2. Ākaṅkhamāno āvuso bhikkhu iddhimā cetovasippatto amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ paṭhavī tveva adhimucceyya. Taṃ


<!-- PTS Page 341 -->
<a id="page-341"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 341]*

kissa hetu? Atthi[^341-1] āvuso amusmiṃ[^341-2] dārukkhandhe paṭhavidhātu, yaṃ nissāya[^341-3] bhikkhu iddhimā cetovasippatto amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ paṭhavī tveva adhimucceyya.

3. Ākaṅkhamāno āvuso bhikkhu iddhimā cetovasippatto amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ āpo tveva adhimucceyya . . .pe[^341-4] . . . tejo tveva adhimucceyya . . . vāyo tveva adhimucceyya . . . subhan tveva adhimucceyya . . .asubhan tveva adhimucceyya. Taṃ kissa hetu? Atthi[^341-1] āvuso amusmiṃ[^341-5] dārukkhandhe asubhadhātu, yaṃ nissāya bhikkhu iddhimā cetovasippatto amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ asubhan tveva adhimucceyyā ti. XLII.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosalesu cārikaṃ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ yena Icchānaṅgalaṃ[^341-6] nāma Kosalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmo tad avasari. Tatra sudaṃ Bhagavā Icchānaṅgale viharati Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe. Assosuṃ kho Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā ‘samaṇo khalu bho Gotamo Sakyaputto Sakyakulā pabbajito Icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppatto Icchānaṅgale viharati Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe. Taṃ kho pana bhavantaṃ[^341-7] Gotamaṃ evaṃ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggato: iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno . . .pe[^341-8] . . . buddho Bhagavā ti. So imaṃ lokaṃ sadevakaṃ samārakaṃ . . .pe[^341-9] . . . arahataṃ dassanaṃ hotī'; ti. Atha kho Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tassā rattiyā accayena pahūtaṃ[^341-10] khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ ādāya yena Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bahidvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhaṃsu uccāsaddā mahāsaddā.

2. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Nāgito Bhagavato upaṭṭhāko hoti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Nāgitaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 342 -->
<a id="page-342"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 342]*

āmantesi ‘ke[^342-1] pana te Nāgita uccāsaddā mahāsaddā, kevaṭṭā[^342-2] maññe macche vilopentī'; ti[^342-3]? ‘Ete bhante Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā pahūtaṃ khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ ādāya bahidvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitā Bhagavantaṃ yeva[^342-4] uddissa bhikkhusaṅghañ cā'; ti. ‘Māhaṃ[^342-5] Nāgita yasena samāgamaṃ mā ca mayā yaso. Yo kho[^342-6] Nāgita na-yimassa nekkhammasukhassa[^342-7] pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa[^342-8] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ[^342-9] nekkhammasukhassa[^342-10] pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, so taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ[^342-8] lābhasakkārasilokasukhaṃ sādiyeyyā[^342-11]'; ti ‘Adhivāsetu dāni bhante Bhagavā, adhivāsetu sugato, adhivāsanakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato[^342-12]. Yena yen'eva dāni bhante Bhagavā gamissati, tanninnā 'va bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā negamā c'eva jānapadā[^342-13] ca. Seyyathā pi bhante thullaphusitake deve vassante yathāninnaṃ udakāni pavattanti, evam eva kho bhante yena yen'eva dāni Bhagavā gamissati, tanninnā 'va bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā negamā c'eva jānapadā[^342-13] ca. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi bhante Bhagavato sīlapaññāṇan'; ti[^342-14]. ‘Māhaṃ[^342-5] Nāgita yasena samāgamaṃ mā ca mayā yaso. Yo kho Nāgita na-y-imassa nekkhammasukhassa[^342-15] pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa[^342-16] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ[^342-9] nekkhammasukhassa[^342-15] pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī[^342-17] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, so taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasakkārasilokasukhaṃ sādiyeyyā[^342-18]'; ti.

3. Idhāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāraṃ


<!-- PTS Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 343]*

samāhitaṃ nisinnaṃ, tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti ‘idān' imaṃ āyasmantaṃ ārāmiko vā[^343-1] ghaṭṭessati[^343-2] samaṇuddeso vā[^343-3] taṃ[^343-4] tamhā[^343-4] samādhimhā[^343-5] cāvessatī[^343-6]'; ti. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno na attamano[^343-7] homi gāmantavihārena.

4. Idha panāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi araññakaṃ[^343-8] araññe[^343-9] pacalāyamānaṃ[^343-10] nisinnaṃ[^343-11], tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti ‘idāni ayam āyasmā imaṃ niddākilamathaṃ paṭivinodetvā araññasaññaṃ yeva manasikarissati ekattan[^343-12]'; ti. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno attamano homi araññavihārena.

5. Idha panāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi araññakaṃ araññe asamāhitaṃ nisinnaṃ, tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita[^343-13] evaṃ hoti ‘idāni ayam āyasmā asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samādahissati[^343-14], samāhitaṃ[^343-15] vā cittaṃ anurakkhissatī[^343-16]'; ti. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno attamano homi araññavihārena.

6. Idha[^343-17] panāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi araññakaṃ araññe samāhitaṃ nisinnaṃ, tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti ‘idāni ayam āyasmā avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimocessati, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ anurakkhissatī'; ti. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno attamano homi araññavihārena.

7. Idha[^343-18] panāhaṃ[^343-18] Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāraṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ, so taṃ lābhasakkārasilokaṃ nikāmayamāno riñcati[^343-19] paṭisallānaṃ, riñcati araññavanapat-


<!-- PTS Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 344]*

thāni[^344-1] pantāni senāsanāni, gāmanigamarājadhāniṃ[^344-2] osaritvā[^344-3] vāsaṃ kappeti. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno na attamano[^344-4] homi gāmantavihārena.

8. Idha panāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ passāmi araññakaṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ, so taṃ lābhasakkārasilokaṃ paṭipaṇāmetvā[^344-5] na riñcati paṭisallānaṃ, na riñcati araññavanapatthāni[^344-1] pantāni[^344-6] senāsanāni. Tenāhaṃ Nāgita tassa bhikkhuno attamano homi araññavihārena. Yasmāhaṃ[^344-7] Nāgita samaye addhānamaggapaṭipanno na kiñci[^344-8] passāmi purato vā pacchato vā, phāsu me Nāgita tasmiṃ samaye hoti, antamaso uccārapassāvakammāyā ti. Devatāvaggo[^344-9] catuttho. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^344-10]: Sekhā[^344-11] dve aparihāni[^344-12] Moggallānavijjābhāgiyā Vivādadānattakārī[^344-13] nidānaṃ[^344-14] Kimbila[^344-15] dārukkhandhena Nāgito ti. XLIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi. Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:-- Āyām'; Ānanda yena pubbārāmo Migāramātu pā-


<!-- PTS Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 345]*

sādo ten'; upasaṅkamissāma[^345-1] divāvihārāyā ti. Evaṃ bhante ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbārāmo Migāramātu pāsādo ten'; upasaṅkami. Atha kho Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:-- Āyām'; Ānanda yena pubbakoṭṭhako ten'; upasaṅkamissāma[^345-1] gattāni parisiñcitun ti. Evaṃ bhante ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbakoṭṭhako ten'; upasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Pubbakoṭṭhake gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamāno[^345-2].

2. Tena kho pana samayena rañño Pasenadi[^345-3]-Kosalassa Seto nāma nāgo mahāturiyatāḷitavāditena pubbakoṭṭhakā paccuttarati. Api 'ssu taṃ jano disvā evam āha ‘abhirūpo vata[^345-4] bho[^345-4] rañño nāgo, dassanīyo vata bho rañño nāgo, pāsādiko vata bho rañño nāgo, kāyupapanno[^345-5] vata bho rañño nāgo, nāgo[^345-6] vata bho nāgo'; ti. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Udāyi Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘hatthim eva nu kho bhante mahantaṃ[^345-7] brahantaṃ[^345-8] kāyupapannaṃ[^345-5] jano disvā evam āha: nāgo vata bho nāgo ti udāhu aññam pi kañci mahantaṃ[^345-9] brahantaṃ[^345-10] kāyupapannaṃ[^345-5] jano disvā evam āha: nāgo vata bho nāgo'; ti? ‘Hatthim pi kho Udāyi mahantaṃ[^345-7] brahantaṃ[^345-11] kāyupapannaṃ[^345-5] jano disvā evam āha: nāgo vata bho[^345-12] nāgo[^345-12] ti[^345-12]. Assam pi kho Udāyi[^345-13] . . . pe[^345-14] . . . goṇam pi kho Udāyi[^345-15] . . . pe[^345-16] . . . uragam[^345-17] pi kho Udāyi[^345-15] . . . pe[^345-16] . . . rukkham pi kho


<!-- PTS Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 346]*

Udāyi[^346-1] . . . pe[^346-2] . . . manussam pi kho Udāyi mahantaṃ brahantaṃ kāyupapannaṃ[^346-3] jano disvā evam āha: nāgo vata bho nāgo ti. Api c'; Udāyi yo sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā[^346-4] pajāya sadevamanussāya āguṃ na karoti kāyena vācāya manasā, tam ahaṃ nāgo ti brūmī'; ti. ‘Acchariyaṃ bhante abbhutaṃ bhante, yāva subhāsitañ c'; idaṃ bhante Bhagavatā <<api c'; Udāyi yo sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā[^346-5] pajāya sadevamanussāya āguṃ na karoti kāyena vācāya manasā, tam ahaṃ nāgo ti brūmī>> ti. Idañ ca panāhaṃ bhante Bhagavatā[^346-6] subhāsitaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumodāmi: Manussabhūtaṃ sambuddhaṃ attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ iriyamānaṃ brahmapathe[^346-7] cittassūpasame rataṃ, yaṃ manussā namassanti sabbadhammānapāraguṃ, devā pi naṃ[^346-8] namassanti: iti me arahato sutaṃ. Sabbasaññojanātītaṃ vanā nibbānam āgataṃ kāmehi nekkhammarataṃ[^346-9] muttaṃ selā va kañcanaṃ sabbe accarucī nāgo Himavā[^346-10] 'ññe[^346-10] siluccaye sabbesaṃ nāganāmānaṃ[^346-11] saccanāmo[^346-12] anuttaro. Nāgaṃ vo[^346-13] kittayissāmi, na hi āguṃ karoti so: soraccaṃ avihiṃsā ca pādā nāgassa te duve, tapo ca brahmacariyaṃ[^346-14] caraṇā nāgassa tyāpare, saddhāhattho mahānāgo upekkhāsetadantavā[^346-15], sati gīvā, siro paññā, vimaṃsā dhammacintanā, dhammakucchi[^346-16] samātapo[^346-17], viveko tassa vāladhi. So jhāyī assāsarato ajjhattaṃ[^346-18] susamāhito[^346-18], gacchaṃ samāhito nāgo, ṭhito nāgo samāhito, sayaṃ[^346-19] samāhito nāgo, nisinno pi samāhito,


<!-- PTS Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 347]*

sabbattha saṃvuto nāgo, esā nāgassa sampadā. Bhuñjati anavajjāni, sāvajjāni na bhuñjati, ghāsam[^347-1] acchādanaṃ laddhā, sannidhiṃ parivajjayaṃ[^347-2], saññojanaṃ anuṃ thūlaṃ sabbaṃ chetvāna bandhanaṃ, yena yen'eva gacchati, anapekkho[^347-3] 'va gacchati. Yathā pi udake jātaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ pavaḍḍhati[^347-4], na upalippati[^347-5] toyena sucigandhaṃ manoramaṃ, tath'eva loke sujāto[^347-6] buddho loke virajjati[^347-7], na upalippati[^347-5] lokena toyena padumaṃ yathā. Mahāgini[^347-8] pajjalito anāhārūpasammati saṅkhāresūpasantesu[^347-9] nibbuto ti pavuccati. Atth'; assāyaṃ viññāpanī[^347-10] upamā viññūhi desitā, viññissanti[^347-11] mahānāgā nāgaṃ nāgena desitaṃ. Vītarāgo vītadoso vītamoho anāsavo, sarīraṃ vijahaṃ nāgo parinibbāti[^347-12] 'nāsavo[^347-13] ti. XLIV.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Migasālāya[^347-14] upāsikāya nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Atha kho Migasālā upāsikā yenāyasmā Ānando ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho Migasālā upāsikā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ bhante Ānanda Bhagavatā dhammo desito aññeyyo[^347-15], yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubho samasamagatikā[^347-16] bhavissanti abhi-


<!-- PTS Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 348]*

samparāyaṃ[^348-1]? Pitā me bhante Purāṇo brahmacārī ahosi ārācārī[^348-2] virato methunā gāmadhammā, so kālakato Bhagavatā vyākato ‘sakadāgāmī satto[^348-3] Tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapanno'[^348-4] ti; petteyyo[^348-5] piyo[^348-6] me[^348-7] bhante Isidatto abrahmacārī ahosi sadārasantuṭṭho, so pi kālakato Bhagavatā vyākato ‘sakadāgāmī satto Tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapanno'; ti. Kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ bhante Ānanda Bhagavatā dhammo desito aññeyyo, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubho samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāyan ti? ‘Evaṃ kho pan'etaṃ bhagini Bhagavatā vyākatan'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Migasālāya upāsikāya nivesane piṇḍapātaṃ gahetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando paccābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: Idhāhaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Migasālāya[^348-8] upāsikāya nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami[^348-9], upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi[^348-10]. Atha kho bhante Migasālā[^348-8] upāsikā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho bhante Migasālā[^348-8] upāsikā maṃ etad avoca ‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ bhante Ānanda Bhagavatā dhammo desito aññeyyo, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubho samasamagatikā[^348-11] bhavissanti abhisamparāyaṃ? Pitā me bhante Purāṇo brahmacārī ahosi ārācārī[^348-12] virato methunā gāmadhammā, so kālakato Bhagavatā vyākato <<sakadāgāmī satto Tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapanno>> ti; petteyyo[^348-5] piyo[^348-13] me bhante Isidatto abrahmacārī ahosi sadārasantuṭṭho, so pi kālakato Bhagavatā vyākato <<sakadāgāmī


<!-- PTS Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 349]*

satto Tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapanno>> ti. Kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ bhante Ānanda Bhagavatā dhammo desito aññeyyo, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubho samasamagatikā[^349-1] bhavissanti abhisamparāyan'; ti? Evaṃ vutte ahaṃ bhante Migasālaṃ[^349-2] upāsikaṃ etad avocaṃ ‘evaṃ kho pan'etaṃ bhagini Bhagavatā vyākatan'; ti. Kā c'; Ānanda[^349-3] Migasālā upāsikā bālā avyattā ambakā[^349-4] ambakasaññā[^349-4] ke ca purisapuggalaparopariyañāṇe[^349-5]! Cha yime Ānanda puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Katame cha?

4. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco puggalo sorato hoti sukhasaṃvāso, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī ekattavāsena. Tassa savanena pi akataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi akataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi appaṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam[^349-6] pi vimuttiṃ na labhati. So[^349-7] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā hānāya pareti[^349-8] no[^349-8] visesāya, hānagāmī yeva[^349-9] hoti no visesagāmī[^349-10].

5. Idha pan'; Ānanda ekacco puggalo sorato hoti sukhasaṃvāso, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī ekattavāsena. Tassa savanena pi kataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi kataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi paṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam[^349-6] pi vimuttiṃ labhati. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā visesāya pareti no hānāya, visesagāmī[^349-10] yeva[^349-9] hoti no hānagāmī. Tatr'; Ānanda pamāṇikā pamiṇanti[^349-11] ‘imassa pi te 'va[^349-12] dhammā aparassa pi te 'va[^349-12] dhammā, kasmā tesaṃ eko hīno eko paṇīto'; ti? Taṃ hi tesaṃ Ānanda hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya. Tatr'; Ānanda yvāyaṃ puggalo sorato[^349-13] hoti sukhasaṃvāso, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī ekattavāsena. Tassa savanena pi kataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi kataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi paṭividdhaṃ[^349-14] hoti, sāmāyikam pi vimuttiṃ labhati. Ayaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 350]*

Ānanda puggalo amunā purimena puggalena abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro ca. Taṃ kissa hetu? Imaṃ[^350-1] Ānanda puggalaṃ dhammasoto nibbahati. Tadanantaraṃ[^350-2] ko jāneyya aññatra Tāthāgatena? Tasmā ti h'; Ānanda mā puggalesu pamāṇikā ahuvattha, mā puggalesu[^350-3] pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha. Khaññati[^350-4] h'; Ānanda puggalesu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhanto. Ahaṃ vā Ānanda puggalesu pamāṇaṃ gaṇheyyaṃ, yo vā pan'assa mādiso.

6. Idha pan'; Ānanda ekaccassa puggalassa kodhamāno adhigato[^350-5] hoti, samayena samayañ c'assa lobhadhammā uppajjanti, tassa[^350-6] savanena pi akataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi akataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi appaṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam pi vimuttiṃ na labhati. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā hānāya pareti no visesāya, hānagāmī yeva[^350-7] hoti no visesagāmī.

7. Idha[^350-8] pan'; Ānanda ekaccassa puggalassa kodhamāno adhigato hoti, samayena samayañ c'assa lobhadhammā uppajjanti. Tassa savanena pi kataṃ hoti . . . pe[^350-9] . . .

8. Idha pan'; Ānanda ekaccassa puggalassa kodhamāno adhigato hoti, samayena samayañ c'assa vacīsaṅkhārā uppajjanti. Tassa savanena pi akataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi akataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi appaṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam[^350-10] pi vimuttiṃ na labhati. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā hānāya pareti no visesāya, hānāgāmī yeva[^350-11] hoti no visesagāmī.

9. Idha pan'; Ānanda ekaccassa puggalassa kodhamāno adhigato hoti, samayena samayañ c'assa vacīsaṅkhārā uppajjanti. Tassa savanena pi kataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi kataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi paṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam[^350-12] pi vimuttiṃ labhati. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā vise-


<!-- PTS Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 351]*

sāya pareti no hānāya, visesagāmī yeva[^351-1] hoti no hānagāmī. Tatr'; Ānanda pamāṇikā pamiṇanti ‘imassa pi te 'va[^351-2] dhammā aparassa[^351-3] pi te 'va[^351-2] dhammā, kasmā tesaṃ eko hīno eko paṇīto'; ti? Taṃ[^351-4] hi[^351-4] tesaṃ Ānanda hoti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya. Tatr'; Ānanda yassa puggalassa kodhamāno adhigato hoti, samayena samayañ c'assa vacīsaṅkhārā uppajjanti. Tassa savanena pi kataṃ hoti, bāhusaccena pi kataṃ hoti, diṭṭhiyā pi paṭividdhaṃ hoti, sāmāyikam[^351-5] pi vimuttiṃ labhati. Ayaṃ Ānanda puggalo amunā purimena puggalena abhikantataro ca paṇītataro ca. Taṃ kissa hetu? Imaṃ h'; Ānanda[^351-6] puggalaṃ dhammasoto nibbahati. Tadanantaraṃ[^351-7] ko jāneyya aññatra Tathāgatena? Tasmā ti h'; Ānanda mā puggalesu pamāṇikā ahuvattha[^351-8], mā puggalesu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha. Khaññati[^351-9] h'; Ānanda[^351-10] puggalesu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhanto. Ahaṃ vā Ānanda puggalesu pamāṇaṃ gaṇheyyaṃ, yo vā pan'assa mādiso. Kā c'; Ānanda Migasālā upāsikā bālā avyattā ambakā[^351-11] ambakapaññā[^351-11] ke ca purisapuggalaparopariyañāṇe[^351-12]! Ime kho Ānanda cha puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Yathārūpena Ānanda sīlena Purāṇo samannāgato ahosi[^351-13], tathārūpena sīlena Isidatto samannāgato abhavissa. Nāy-idha Purāṇo Isidattassa gatim[^351-14] pi aññassa[^351-15]. Yathārūpāya ca Ānanda paññāya Isidatto samannāgato ahosi, tathārūpāya paññāya Purāṇo samannāgato abhavissa. Nay-idha Isidatto Purāṇassa gatim[^351-14] pi aññassa. Iti kho Ānanda ime puggalā ubho ekaṅgahīnā ti. XLV.

1. Dāliddiyaṃ[^351-16] bhikkhave dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yam pi bhikkhave daliddo[^351-17]


<!-- PTS Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 352]*

assako anāḷiko[^352-1] iṇaṃ ādiyati, iṇādānam[^352-2] pi bhikkhave dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yam pi bhikkhave daliddo[^352-3] assako anāḷiko iṇaṃ ādiyitvā vaḍḍhiṃ paṭisuṇāti, vaḍḍhi pi bhikkhave dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yam pi bhikkhave daliddo assako anāḷiko vaḍḍhiṃ paṭisuṇitvā kālābhataṃ[^352-4] vaḍḍhiṃ na deti, codenti pi naṃ, codanā pi bhikkhave dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yaṃ pi bhikkhave daliddo assako anāḷiko codiyamāno na deti, anucaranti pi naṃ, anucariyā pi bhikkhave dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yam pi bhikkhave daliddo assako anāḷiko anucariyamāno na deti, bandhanti pi naṃ, bandhanam pi bhikkhave dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Iti kho bhikkhave dāliddiyam[^352-5] pi dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino, iṇādānaṃ[^352-6] pi dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino, vaḍḍhi pi dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino, codanā pi dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino, anucariyā pi dukkhā lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino, bandhanam pi dukkhaṃ lokasmiṃ kāmabhogino. Evam eva kho bhikkhave yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalesu dhammesu, hiri natthi kusalesu dhammesu, ottappaṃ natthi kusalesu dhammesu, viriyaṃ natthi kusalesu dhammesu, paññā natthi kusalesu dhammesu. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyassa vinaye daliddo assako anāḷiko[^352-7].

2. Sa kho so bhikkhave daliddo assako anāḷiko[^352-7] saddhāya asati kusalesu dhammesu, hiriyā asati kusalesu dhammesu, ottappe asati kusalesu dhammesu, viriye asati kusalesu dhammesu, paññāya asati kusalesu dhammesu kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya duccaritaṃ carati, manasā duccaritaṃ carati. Idam assa iṇādānasmiṃ[^352-8] vadāmi. So tassa kāyaduccaritassa paṭicchādanahetu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati[^352-9], ‘mā maṃ jaññū[^352-10]'; ti icchati, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti


<!-- PTS Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 353]*

saṅkappeti[^353-1], ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti vācaṃ bhāsati, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti kāyena parakkamati[^353-2]. So tassa vacīduccaritassa paṭicchādanahetu . . . pe . . . So tassa manoduccaritassa paṭicchādanahetu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti icchati, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti saṅkappeti, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti vācaṃ bhāsati, ‘mā maṃ jaññū'; ti kāyena parakkamati. Idam assa vaḍḍhiyā vadāmi. Tam enaṃ pesalā sabrahmacārī evam āhaṃsu: Ayañ ca[^353-3] so āyasmā evaṃkārī evaṃsamācāro[^353-4] ti. Idam assa codanāya vadāmi. Tam enaṃ araññagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā suññāgāragataṃ vā vippaṭisārasahagatā pāpakā akusalavitakkā samudācaranti[^353-5]. Idam assa anucariyāya vadāmi.

3. Sa kho so bhikkhave daliddo assako anāḷiko[^353-6] kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā vācāya duccaritaṃ caritvā manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā nirayabandhane[^353-7] vā bajjhati tiracchānayonibandhane[^353-7] vā. Nāhaṃ bhikkhave aññaṃ ekabandhanaṃ[^353-8] pi[^353-9] samanupassāmi evaṃdāruṇaṃ evaṃkaṭukaṃ[^353-10] evaṃ-antarāyakaraṃ anuttarassa yogakkhemassa adhigamāya, yathayidaṃ[^353-11] bhikkhave nirayabandhanaṃ vā tiracchānayonibandhanaṃ vā ti. Dāliddiyaṃ[^353-12] dukkhaṃ loke iṇādānañ[^353-13] ca vuccati, daliddo iṇam ādāya bhuñjamāno[^353-14] vihaññati, tato anucaranti naṃ, bandhanam pi nigacchati, etaṃ hi bandhanaṃ dukkhaṃ kāmalābhābhijappinaṃ[^353-15]. Tath'eva[^353-16] ariyavinaye[^353-17] saddhā yassa na vijjati,


<!-- PTS Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 354]*

ahiriko anottāpi[^354-1] pāpakammavinicchayo[^354-2] kāyaduccaritaṃ katvā vacīduccaritāni ca manoduccaritaṃ katvā ‘mā maṃ jaññū[^354-3]'; ti icchati. So saṃsappati[^354-4] kāyena vācāya uda cetasā pāpakammaṃ pavaḍḍhento tattha tattha punappunaṃ, so pāpakammo dummedho jānaṃ dukkaṭam attano daliddo iṇam ādāya bhuñjamāno[^354-5] vihaññati. Tato anucaranti naṃ saṅkappā mānasā dukkhā gāme vā yadi vāraññe yassa vippaṭisārajā, so pāpakammo dummedho jānaṃ dukkaṭam attano yonim aññataraṃ gantvā niraye vā pi bajjhati, etaṃ hi bandhanaṃ dukkhaṃ[^354-6] yamhā dhīro pamuccati[^354-7]. Dhammaladdhehi bhogehi dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādayaṃ ubhayattha kaṭaggāho[^354-8] saddhassa gharam esino diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya[^354-9] samparāyasukhāya ca: evam etaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ cāgo puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati. Tath'eva ariyavinaye[^354-10] saddhā yassa patiṭṭhitā hirimano ca ottāpi[^354-11] paññavā sīlasaṃvuto, eso kho ariyavinaye[^354-10] ‘sukhajīvī'; ti[^354-12] vuccati nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ laddhā upekhaṃ adhitiṭṭhati. Pañca nīvaraṇe hitvā niccaṃ āraddhaviriyo jhānāni upasampajja ekodi nipako sato: evaṃ[^354-13] ñatvā yathābhūtaṃ sabbasaññojanakkhaye[^354-14] sabbaso anupādāya sammā cittaṃ vimuccati. Tassa[^354-15] sammāvimuttassa ñāṇaṃ ce[^354-16] hoti tādino ‘akuppā me vimuttī'; ti bhavasaññojanakkhaye: etaṃ kho paramaṃ ñāṇaṃ, etaṃ sukham anuttaraṃ, asokaṃ virajaṃ khemaṃ, etaṃ āṇaṇyam[^354-17] uttaman ti.


<!-- PTS Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 355]*

XLVI.

1. Evaṃ[^355-1] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Mahācundo Cetīsu viharati Sahajātiyaṃ[^355-2]. Tatra kho āyasmā Mahācundo bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^355-3] ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahācundassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahācundo etad avoca:--

2. Idha āvuso dhammayogā bhikkhū jhāyī bhikkhū apasādenti[^355-4] ‘ime pana <<jhāyino[^355-5] 'mhā jhāyino 'mhā[^355-6]>> ti jhāyanti[^355-7] pajjhāyanti[^355-8], kiṃ h'ime[^355-9] jhāyanti, kint'ime[^355-10] jhāyanti, kathaṃ h'ime[^355-11] jhāyantī'; ti? Tattha dhammayogā ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, jhāyī ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipannā honti bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^355-12] janassa[^355-12] atthāya hitāya sukhāya[^355-13] devamanussānaṃ.

3. Idha panāvuso jhāyī bhikkhū dhammayoge bhikkhū apasādenti[^355-14] ‘ime pana <<dhammayog'; amhā dhammayog'; amhā>> ti uddhatā unnaḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā[^355-15] muṭṭhassatī[^355-16] asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā pākaṭindriyā, kiṃ h'ime[^355-17] dhammayogā, kint'ime[^355-18] dhammayogā[^355-18], kathaṃ h'ime[^355-11] dhammayogā'; ti? Tattha jhāyī ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, dhammayogā ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipannā honti bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^355-12] janassa[^355-12] atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ.

4. Idha panāvuso dhammayogā bhikkhū dhammayogānaṃ yeva[^355-19] bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, no jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti. Tattha dhammayogā ca


<!-- PTS Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 356]*

bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, jhāyī ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipannā honti bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^356-1] janassa[^356-1] atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ.

5. Idha panāvuso jhāyī bhikkhū jhāyīnaṃ yeva bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, no dhammayogānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ[^356-2] vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti. Tattha jhāyī ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, dhammayogā ca bhikkhū na ppasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipannā honti bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^356-1] janassa[^356-1] atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ. Tasmā ti hāvuso evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

6. Dhammayogā samānā jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissāmā ti. Evaṃ hi vo āvuso sikkhitabbaṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Acchariyā h'; ete[^356-3] āvuso puggalā dullabhā lokasmiṃ, ye amataṃ dhātuṃ kāyena phusitvā[^356-4] viharanti. Tasmā ti hāvuso evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

7. Jhāyī samānā dhammayogānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissāmā ti. Evaṃ hi vo {āvuso} sikkhitabbaṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Acchariyā h'; ete āvuso puggalā dullabhā lokasmiṃ, ye gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ paññāya ativijjha passantī ti. XLVII.

1. Atha kho Moliyasīvako[^356-5] paribbājako yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^356-6] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Moliyasīvako paribbājako Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sandiṭṭhiko dhammo sandiṭṭhiko dhammo ti bhante vuccati. Kittāvatā nu[^356-7] kho bhante sandiṭṭhiko dhammo hoti[^356-8] akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^356-9] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti?


<!-- PTS Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 357]*

2. Tena hi Sīvaka tañ ñev'; ettha[^357-1] paṭipucchissāmi. Yathā te khameyya, tathā naṃ vyākareyyāsi. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ lobhaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ lobho'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ lobhaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ lobho'; ti pajānāsī ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.' Yaṃ kho tvaṃ Sīvaka santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ lobhaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ lobho'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ lobhaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ lobho'; ti pajānāsi: evaṃ[^357-2] kho Sīvaka sandiṭṭhiko[^357-3] dhammo[^357-4] hoti . . .[^357-5] Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dosaṃ . . . pe[^357-6] . . . santaṃ[^357-7] vā ajjhattaṃ mohaṃ[^357-8], santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ lobhadhammaṃ, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dosadhammaṃ, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mohadhammaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ mohadhammo'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mohadhammaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ mohadhammo'; ti pajānāsī ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; Yaṃ kho tvaṃ Sīvaka santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mohadhammaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ mohadhammo'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mohadhammaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ mohadhammo'; ti pajānāsi: evaṃ[^357-9] kho Sīvaka sandiṭṭhiko dhammo hoti akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^357-10] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī ti. ‘Abhikkantaṃ[^357-11] . . . pe[^357-12] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante Bhagavā dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. XLVIII.

1. Atha kho aññataro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇiyaṃ[^357-13] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca 'sandiṭṭhiko dhammo sandiṭṭhiko dhammo ti bho Gotama vuccati. Kittāvatā nu kho bho Gotama


<!-- PTS Page 358 -->
<a id="page-358"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 358]*

sandiṭṭhiko dhammo hoti akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^358-1] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti?

2. Tena hi brāhmaṇa tañ ñev'; ettha paṭipucchissāmi. Yathā te khameyya, tathā naṃ vyākareyyāsi. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā[^358-2] ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ rāgo'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ rāgo'; ti pajānāsī ti? ‘Evaṃ bho.' Yaṃ kho tvaṃ brāhmaṇa santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ rāgo'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā[^358-2] ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ rāgo'; ti pajānāsi: evaṃ[^358-3] kho brāhmaṇa sandiṭṭhiko dhammo hoti[^358-4] . . . Taṃ kiṃ maññasi brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dosaṃ, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mohaṃ, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāyasandosaṃ, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vacīsandosaṃ[^358-5], santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manosandosaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ manosandoso'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manosandosaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ manosandoso'; ti pajānāsī ti? ‘Evaṃ bho'. Yaṃ kho tvaṃ brāhmaṇa santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manosandosaṃ ‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ manosandoso'; ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manosandosaṃ ‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ manosandoso'; ti pajānāsi[^358-6]: evaṃ[^358-7] kho brāhmaṇa sandiṭṭhiko dhammo hoti akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^358-1] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī ti. ‘Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama, abhikkantaṃ[^358-8] bho Gotama . . .[^358-5] upāsakaṃ[^358-9] maṃ[^358-9] bhavaṃ[^358-9] Gotamo[^358-9] dhāretu ajjat-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. XLIX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tena kho pana[^358-10] samayena āyasmā ca Khemo āyasmā ca Sumano Sāvatthiyaṃ viha-


<!-- PTS Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 359]*

ranti Andhavanasmiṃ. Atha kho āyasmā ca Khemo āyasmā ca Sumano yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu[^359-1], upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinno[^359-2] kho āyasmā Khemo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: ‘yo so bhante bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho parikkhīṇabhavasaññojano samma-d-aññā vimutto, tassa na[^359-3] evaṃ hoti <<atthi me seyyo>> ti vā <<atthi me sadiso>> ti vā <<atthi me hīno>> ti vā'; ti. Idam avoca āyasmā Khemo. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho āyasmā Khemo ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti uṭṭhāyāsanā[^359-4] Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi. Atha kho āyasmā Sumano acirapakkante āyasmante Kheme Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘yo so bhante bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho parikkhīṇabhavasaññojano samma-d-aññā vimutto, tassa na evaṃ hoti <<natthi me seyyo>> ti vā <<natthi me sadiso>> ti vā <<natthi me hīno>> ti vā'; ti. Idam avoca āyasmā Sumano. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho āyasmā Sumano ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā acirapakkante[^359-5] āyasmante ca[^359-6] Kheme āyasmante[^359-7] ca[^359-7] Sumane bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Evaṃ kho bhikkhave kulaputtā aññaṃ vyākaronti. Attho ca vutto attā ca anupanīto. Atha ca pana[^359-8] idh'; ekacce moghapurisā hasamānakā[^359-9] maññe[^359-10] aññaṃ vyākaronti, te pacchā vighātaṃ āpajjantī ti. Na ussesu na[^359-11] omesu[^359-12] samatte[^359-13] nopanīyare[^359-14] Khīṇā sañjāti[^359-15] vusitaṃ[^359-16] brahmacariyaṃ[^359-17] caranti Saññojanavippamuttā[^359-18] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 360 -->

##### *[PTS Page 360]*

L.

1. Indriyasaṃvare bhikkhave asati indriyasaṃvaravipannassa[^360-1] hatupanisaṃ[^360-2] hoti sīlaṃ, sīle asati sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipannassa hatupaniso hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge asati nibbidāvirāgavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

2. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā[^360-3] pi na pāripūriṃ[^360-4] gacchati, taco pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, pheggu pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave indriyasaṃvare asati indriyasaṃvaravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti[^360-5] . . . pe[^360-6] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

3. Indriyasaṃvare bhikkhave sati indriyasaṃvarasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ[^360-7] hoti sīlaṃ, sīle sati sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno[^360-8] hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampannassa upanisasampanno[^360-8] hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge sati nibbidāvirāgasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ[^360-8] hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

4. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho {sākhāpalāsasampanno}, tassa papaṭikā[^360-9] pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, pheggu pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave indriyasaṃvare sati indriyasaṃvarasampannassa[^360-10] upanisasampannaṃ[^360-8] hoti[^360-11] . . . pe[^360-12] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan[^360-13] ti.

LI.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Sāriputtena saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^361-1] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Kittāvatā nu kho āvuso Sāriputta bhikkhu assutañ[^361-2] c'eva dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutā c'assa dhammā na sammosaṃ gacchanti, ye c'assa dhammā pubbe cetasā[^361-3] samphuṭṭhapubbā, te ca samudācaranti, aviññātañ ca vijānātī ti? ‘Āyasmā kho Ānando bahussuto[^361-4], paṭibhātu āyasmantaṃ yeva Ānandan'; ti. Tena h'; āvuso Sāriputta[^361-5] suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ āvuso'; ti kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmato Ānandassa paccassosi. Āyasmā Ānando etad avoca:--

3. Idhāvuso Sāriputta bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vedallaṃ; yathāsutaṃ[^361-6] yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena paresaṃ deseti; yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena paresaṃ vāceti; yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena sajjhāyaṃ karoti; yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā anuvitakketi anuvicāreti[^361-7] manasānupekkhati; yasmiṃ āvāse therā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā[^361-8] āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tasmiṃ āvāse vassaṃ upeti[^361-9], te kālena kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati[^361-9] paripañhati[^361-9] ‘idaṃ[^361-10] bhante kathaṃ, imassa kvattho' ti? Tassa te[^361-11] āyasmanto[^361-12] avivaṭañ c'eva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ ca uttānīkaronti[^361-13], anekavihitesu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinodenti. Ettāvatā[^361-14] kho


<!-- PTS Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 362]*

āvuso Sāriputta bhikkhu assutañ[^362-1] c'eva dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutā c'assa dhammā na sammosaṃ gacchanti, ye c'assa dhammā pubbe[^362-2] cetasā[^362-3] samphuṭṭhapubbā, te ca samudācaranti, aviññātañ ca vijānātī ti.

4. Acchariyaṃ āvuso abbhutaṃ āvuso, yāva subhāsitañ[^362-4] c'; idaṃ[^362-4] āyasmatā Ānandena. Imehi ca mayaṃ[^362-5] chahi dhammehi samannāgataṃ[^362-6] āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ dhārema[^362-7].

5. Āyasmā[^362-8] hi[^362-9] Ānando[^362-10] dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vedallaṃ; āyasmā Ānando yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena paresaṃ deseti; āyasmā Ānando yathāsutaṃ pathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena paresaṃ vāceti; āyasmā Ānando yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthārena sajjhāyaṃ karoti; āyasmā Ānando yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā anuvitakketi anuvicāreti[^362-11] manasānupekkhati; āyasmā Ānando yasmiṃ āvāse therā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tasmiṃ āvāse vassaṃ upeti, te āyasmā Ānando kālena kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati ‘idaṃ bhante kathaṃ, imassa kvattho'; ti? Te āyasmato Ānandassa avivaṭañ c'eva {vivaranti}, anuttānīkatañ ca uttānīkaronti, anekavihitesu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinodentī ti. LII.

1. Atha kho Jāṇussoṇi[^362-12] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ[^362-13] kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^362-14] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Jāṇussoṇi[^362-12] brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 363]*

2. Khattiyā bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā[^363-1] kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Khattiyā kho brāhmaṇa bhogādhippāyā paññūpavicārā balādhiṭṭhānā[^363-2] paṭhavībhinivesā[^363-3] issariyapariyosānā'; ti. Brāhmaṇā pana bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Brāhmaṇā kho brāhmaṇa bhogādhippāyā paññūpavicārā mantādhiṭṭhānā yaññābhinivesā brahmalokapariyosānā'; ti. Gahapatikā pana bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Gahapatikā kho brāhmaṇa bhogādhippāyā paññūpavicārā sippādhiṭṭhānā kammantābhinivesā niṭṭhitakammantapariyosānā'; ti. Itthī pana bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Itthī kho brāhmaṇa purisādhippāyā alaṅkārūpavicārā puttādhiṭṭhānā asapatibhinivesā[^363-4] issariyapariyosānā'; ti. Corā pana bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Corā kho brāhmaṇa ādānādhippāyā gahaṇūpavicārā[^363-5] satthādhiṭṭhānā andhakārābhinivesā adassanapariyosānā'; ti. Samaṇā pana bho Gotama kim adhippāyā kiṃ upavicārā kiṃ adhiṭṭhānā kiṃ abhinivesā kiṃ pariyosānā ti? ‘Samaṇā kho brāhmaṇa khantisoraccādhippāyā paññūpavicārā sīlādhiṭṭhānā ākiñcaññābhinivesā[^363-6] nibbānapariyosānā'; ti.

3. Acchariyaṃ bho Gotama abbhutaṃ[^363-7] bho[^363-7] Gotama[^363-7], khattiyānam pi bhavaṃ Gotamo jānāti adhippāyañ ca upavicārañ ca adhiṭṭhānañ ca abhinivesañ ca pariyosānañ ca, brāhmaṇānam pi bhavaṃ Gotamo jānāti . . . pe[^363-8] . . . gahapatikānam[^363-9] pi bhavaṃ Gotamo jānāti . . . itthīnam pi bhavaṃ Gotamo jānāti . . . corānam pi bhavaṃ Gotamo

<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 364]*

jānāti . . . samaṇānam pi bhavaṃ Gotamo jānāti adhippāyañ ca upavicārañ ca adhiṭṭhānañ ca abhinivesañ ca pariyosānañ ca. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . . pe[^364-1] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. LIII.

1. Atha kho aññataro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^364-2] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Atthi nu kho bho Gotama eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato, yo ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko ti? ‘Atthi kho[^364-3] brāhmaṇa eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato, yo ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko'; ti. Katamo pana bho Gotama eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato, yo ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko ti?

3. ‘Appamādo kho brāhmaṇa eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato[^364-4] ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa yāni kānici jaṅgamānaṃ[^364-5] pāṇānaṃ[^364-6] padajātāni[^364-6], sabbāni[^364-3] tāni[^364-3] hatthipade samodhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ tesaṃ aggam akkhāyati[^364-7], evam eva kho brāhmaṇa appamādo eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato[^364-4] ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gopānasiyo, sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭaninnā kūṭasamosaraṇā, kūṭaṃ tāsaṃ aggam akkhāyati, evam eva kho
