<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 365]*

brāhmaṇa . . . pe[^365-1] . . . Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa babbajalāyako[^365-2] babbajaṃ[^365-3] lāyitvā agge gahetvā odhunāti nidhunāti[^365-4] nicchedeti[^365-5], evam eva kho brāhmaṇa[^365-6] . . . pe[^365-7] . . . Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa ambapiṇḍiyā[^365-8] vaṇṭacchinnāya[^365-9] yāni kānici ambāni vaṇṭūpanibandhanāni[^365-10], sabbāni[^365-11] tāni tadanvayāni bhavanti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa . . . pe[^365-12] . . . Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa ye keci khuddarājāno[^365-13], sabbe te rañño cakkavattissa anuyuttā[^365-14] bhavanti, rājā tesaṃ cakkavatti aggam akkhāyati, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa . . . pe[^365-12] . . . Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa yā kāci tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā, sabbā tā candassa[^365-15] pabhāya kalaṃ[^365-16] nāgghanti[^365-17] soḷasiṃ, candappabhā[^365-18] tāsaṃ aggam akkhāyati, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa appamādo eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato[^365-19] ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko[^365-20]. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato[^365-19] ubho atthe samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati: diṭṭhadhammikañ c'eva atthaṃ yo ca attho samparāyiko'; ti. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama, abhikkantaṃ[^365-21] bho Gotama . . . pe[^365-22] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti.


<!-- PTS Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 366]*

LIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Dhammiko jātibhūmiyaṃ āvāsiko hoti sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattasu āvāsesu. Tatra sudaṃ āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati[^366-1] roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā[^366-2] rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti, na saṇṭhanti[^366-3], riñcanti āvāsaṃ. Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘mayaṃ kho bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paccupaṭṭhitā[^366-4] cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena, atha kho pana āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ; ko nu kho hetu, ko paccayo yena āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsan'; ti? Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘ayaṃ kho āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati[^366-5] roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā[^366-2] rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ; yan nūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ pabbājeyyāmā'; ti. Atha kho jātibhūmikā upāsakā yena āyasmā Dhammiko ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘pakkamatu bhante āyasmā Dhammiko imamhā āvāsā, alan te idha vāsenā'; ti.

2. Atha kho āyasmā Dhammiko tamhā āvāsā aññaṃ[^366-6] āvāsaṃ[^366-6] agamāsi. Tatra[^366-7] pi sudaṃ āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati[^366-8] roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñ-


<!-- PTS Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 367]*

canti āvāsaṃ. Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘mayaṃ kho bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paccupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena, atha ca pana āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ; ko nu kho hetu, ko paccayo yena āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsan'; ti? Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘ayaṃ kho āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ; yan nūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ pabbājeyyāmā'; ti. Atha kho jātibhūmikā upāsakā yena āyasmā Dhammiko ten' upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘pakkamatu bhante āyasmā Dhammiko imamhā pi āvāsā, alan te idha vāsenā'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Dhammiko tamhā pi āvāsā aññaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi. Tatra pi sudaṃ āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati paribhāsati[^367-1] vihiṃsati[^367-2] vitudati roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā[^367-3] paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ. Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘mayaṃ kho bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paccupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena[^367-4], atha ca pana āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsaṃ; ko nu kho hetu, ko paccayo yena āgantukā bhikkhū pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti, riñcanti āvāsan'; ti? Atha kho jātibhūmikānaṃ upāsakānaṃ etad ahosi ‘ayaṃ[^367-5] kho āyasmā Dhammiko āgantuke bhikkhū akkosati . . . pe . . . roseti vācāya, te ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā Dhammikena akkosiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihesiyamānā vitudiyamānā rosiyamānā vācāya pakkamanti na saṇṭhanti,


<!-- PTS Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 368]*

riñcanti āvāsaṃ; yan nūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ pabbājeyyāma sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehī' ti. Atha kho jātibhūmikā upāsakā yena āyasmā Dhammiko ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘pakkamatu bhante āyasmā Dhammiko sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehī'; ti.

4. Atha kho āyasmato Dhammikassa etad ahosi ‘pabbājito kho 'mhi jātibhūmikehi upāsakehi sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehi, kahan nu kho dāni gacchāmī'; ti? Atha kho āyasmato Dhammikassa etad ahosi ‘yan nūnāhaṃ yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkammeyyan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Dhammiko pattacīvaram ādāya yena Rājagahaṃ tena pakkāmi, anupubbena yena Rājagahaṃ Gijjhakūṭo[^368-1] pabbato[^368-2], yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Dhammikaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘handa kuto nu tvaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika āgacchasī'; ti? ‘Pabbājito ahaṃ bhante jātibhūmikehi upāsakehi sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehī'; ti. Alaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika, kin te iminā? Yan taṃ tato tato pabbājenti, so tvaṃ tato tato pabbājito mam eva santike āgacchasi.

5. Bhūtapubbaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika sāmuddikā vāṇijā tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ gahetvā nāvāya samuddaṃ ajjhogāhanti[^368-3]. Te[^368-4] atīradakkhiṇiyā[^368-5] nāvāya tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ muñcanti. So gacchat'eva[^368-6] puratthimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ, gacchati anudisaṃ. Sace so samantā tīraṃ passati, tathā gatako[^368-7] 'va[^368-8] hoti. Sace pana so samantā tīraṃ na passati, tam eva nāvaṃ paccāgacchati. Evam eva kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika, yan taṃ tato tato pabbājenti, so tvaṃ tato tato pabbājito mam'eva santike[^368-9] āgacchasi.


<!-- PTS Page 369 -->
<a id="page-369"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 369]*

6. Bhūtapubbaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika rañño Koravyassa Suppatiṭṭho[^369-1] nāma nigrodharājā ahosi pañcasākho sītacchāyo manoramo[^369-2]. Suppatiṭṭhassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika nigrodharājassa dvādasa yojanāni abhiniveso ahosi, pañca yojanāni mūlasantānakānaṃ[^369-3]. Suppatiṭṭhassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika nigrodharājassa tāva[^369-4] mahantāni phalāni ahesuṃ. Seyyathā pi nāma āḷhakathālikā[^369-5], evam assa sādūni phalāni ahesuṃ, seyyathā pi nāma khuddaṃ madhuṃ[^369-6] anīlakaṃ[^369-7]. Suppatiṭṭhassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika nigrodharājassa ekaṃ khandhaṃ rājā paribhuñjati saddhiṃ itthāgārena, ekaṃ khandhaṃ balakāyo paribhuñjati, ekaṃ khandhaṃ negamajānapadā[^369-8] paribhuñjanti, ekaṃ khandhaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā paribhuñjanti, ekaṃ khandhaṃ migapakkhiyo[^369-9] paribhuñjanti. Suppatiṭṭhassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika nigrodharājassa na koci phalāni rakkhati[^369-10]. Na ca sudam[^369-11] aññamaññassa[^369-12] phalāni hiṃsanti. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika aññataro puriso Suppatiṭṭhassa nigrodharājassa yāvadatthaṃ phalāni bhakkhitvā[^369-13] sākhaṃ bhañjitvā[^369-14] pakkāmi. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Suppatiṭṭhe[^369-15] nigrodharāje[^369-16] adhivatthāya devatāya etad ahosi ‘acchariyaṃ vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho, yāva[^369-17] pāpo[^369-18] manusso yatra hi nāma Suppatiṭṭhassa nigrodharājassa yāvadatthaṃ phalāni bhakkhitvā sākhaṃ bhañjitvā[^369-14] pakkamissati, yan nūna Suppatiṭṭho nigrodharājā āyatiṃ phalaṃ na dadeyyā'; ti. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Suppatiṭṭho nigrodharājā āyatiṃ phalaṃ nādāsi[^369-19]. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika


<!-- PTS Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 370]*

rājā Koravyo yena Sakko devānam indo ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Sakkaṃ devānam indaṃ etad avoca ‘yagghe mārisa jāneyyāsi, Suppatiṭṭho nigrodharājā phalaṃ na detī'; ti? Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sakko devānam indo tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi[^370-1], yathā bhusā[^370-2] vātavuṭṭhi[^370-3] āgantvā Suppatiṭṭhaṃ nigrodharājaṃ pātesi[^370-4] ummūlam akāsi[^370-5]. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Suppatiṭṭhe nigrodharāje adhivatthā devatā dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sakko devānam indo yena Suppatiṭṭhe nigrodharāje adhivatthā devatā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Suppatiṭṭhe nigrodharāje adhivatthaṃ devataṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu tvaṃ devate dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā[^370-6] ekamantaṃ ṭhitā'; ti? ‘Tathā hi pana me mārisa bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pātesi[^370-7] ummūlam akāsī'; ti. ‘Api nu tvaṃ devate rukkhadhamme ṭhitāya[^370-8] bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pātesi[^370-7] ummūlam akāsī'; ti? ‘Kathaṃ pana mārisa rukkho[^370-9] rukkhadhamme ṭhito hotī'; ti? ‘Idh'eva[^370-10] devate rukkhassa mūlaṃ mūlatthikā haranti, tacaṃ tacatthikā haranti, pattaṃ pattatthikā haranti, pupphaṃ pupphatthikā haranti, phalaṃ phalatthika haranti, na ca tena[^370-11] devatāya[^370-12] anattamanatā vā anabhinandi[^370-13] vā karaṇīyā: evaṃ kho devate rukkho rukkhadhamme ṭhito hotī'; ti. ‘Aṭṭhitāy' eva kho me mārisa rukkhadhamme[^370-14] bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pātesi[^370-7] ummūlam akāsī'; ti. ‘Sace kho tvaṃ devate rukkhadhamme[^370-14] tiṭṭheyyāsi, siyā pi[^370-15] te bhavanaṃ yathāpure'; ti. Thassām'[^370-16] ahaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 371]*

mārisa rukkhadhamme, hotu me bhavanaṃ yathāpure' ti. ‘Atha kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sakko devānam indo tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi[^371-1], yathā bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā Suppatiṭṭham nigrodharājaṃ ussāpesi, sacchavīni[^371-2] mūlāni ahesuṃ. Evam eva kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika api nu taṃ[^371-3] samaṇadhamme[^371-4] ṭhitaṃ jātibhūmikā upāsakā pabbājeyyuṃ[^371-5] sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehī'; ti? ‘Kathaṃ pana[^371-6] bhante samaṇo samaṇadhamme[^371-7] ṭhito hotī'; ti? ‘Idha brāhmaṇa Dhammika samaṇo akkosantaṃ na paccakkosati, rosantaṃ na paṭirosati, bhaṇḍantaṃ na paṭibhaṇḍati: evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa Dhammika samaṇo samaṇadhamme ṭhito hotī'; ti. ‘Aṭṭhitaṃ[^371-8] yeva kho maṃ bhante samaṇadhamme jātibhūmikā upāsakā pabbājesuṃ sabbaso jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsehī'; ti.

7. Bhūtapubbaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sunetto nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Sunettassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika satthuno anekāni sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ. Sunetto satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desesi. Ye kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sunettassa satthuno brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni[^371-9] na pasādesuṃ[^371-10], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^371-11]. Ye kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika Sunettassa satthuno brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni pasādesuṃ, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^371-11]. Bhūtapubbaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika Mugapakkho[^371-12] nāma satthā ahosi . . . pe[^371-13] . . . Aranemi[^371-14] nāma satthā ahosi . . . Kuddālako[^371-15] nāma satthā ahosi . . . Hatthipālo nāma satthā ahosi . . .[^371-16]


<!-- PTS Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 372]*

Jotipālo nāma satthā ahosi titthakaro kāmesu vītarāgo. Jotipālassa kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika satthuno anekāni sāvakasatāni ahesuṃ. Jotipālo satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desesi. Ye kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika Jotipālassa satthuno brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni na[^372-1] pasādesuṃ[^372-1], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^372-2]. Ye kho pana brāhmaṇa Dhammika Jotipālassa satthuno brahmalokasahavyatāya dhammaṃ desentassa cittāni pasādesuṃ, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjiṃsu[^372-2]. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi brāhmaṇa Dhammika, yo ime cha satthāre[^372-3] titthakare kāmesu vītarāge anekasataparivāre sasāvakasaṅghe[^372-4] paduṭṭhacitto akkoseyya paribhāseyya, bahuṃ so apuññaṃ pasaveyyā[^372-5] ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante'.

8. Yo kho[^372-6] brāhmaṇa Dhammika ime cha satthāre[^372-3] titthakare kāmesu vītarāge anekasataparivāre sasāvakasaṅghe[^372-4] paduṭṭhacitto[^372-7] akkoseyya paribhāseyya, bahuṃ[^372-8] so apuññaṃ pasaveyya. Yo ekaṃ diṭṭhisampannaṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacitto[^372-7] akkosati paribhāsati, ayaṃ tato bahutaraṃ apuññaṃ pasavati[^372-9]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Nāhaṃ brāhmaṇa Dhammika ito bahiddhā evarūpiṃ[^372-10] khantiṃ[^372-11] vadāmi yathā 'maṃ sabrahmacārīsu. Tasmā ti ha brāhmaṇa Dhammika evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

9. Na no[^372-12] sabrahmacārīsu cittāni paduṭṭhāni bhavissantī ti. Evaṃ hi[^372-13] te brāhmaṇa Dhammika sikkhitabban ti.


<!-- PTS Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 373]*

Sunetto Mugapakkho[^373-1] ca Aranemi[^373-2] ca brāhmaṇo Kuddālako[^373-3] ahu satthā Hatthipālo ca māṇavo[^373-4] Jotipālo ca Govindo ahu sattapurohito[^373-5]. Ahiṃsakā[^373-6] atītaṃse cha satthāro yasassino nirāmagandhā karuṇe[^373-7] vimuttā[^373-8] kāmasaññojanātigā[^373-9] kāmarāgaṃ virājetvā brahmalokūpagā ahu[^373-10]. Ahesuṃ sāvakā tesaṃ anekāni satāni pi nirāmagandhā karuṇe[^373-11] vimuttā[^373-8] kāmasaññojanātigā[^373-9] kāmarāgaṃ virājetvā brahmalokūpagā ahu[^373-12]. Ye te isī[^373-13] bāhirake vītarāge samāhite paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo yo naro paribhāsati, bahuñ ca so pasavati apuññaṃ tādiso naro. Yo c'; ekaṃ diṭṭhisampannaṃ bhikkhuṃ[^373-14] buddhassa[^373-14] sāvakaṃ[^373-14] paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo[^373-15] yo naro paribhāsati, ayaṃ[^373-16] tato[^373-17] bahutaraṃ apuññaṃ pasave naro. Na[^373-18] sādhurūpaṃ āsīde diṭṭhiṭṭhānappahāyinaṃ[^373-19] sattamo puggalo eso ariyasaṅghassa vuccati. Avītarāgo kāmesu yassa pañc'; indriyā mudū[^373-20]: saddhā sati viriyañ ca[^373-21] samatho ca vipassanā[^373-22], tādisaṃ bhikkhuṃ āsajja pubb'eva upahaññati, attānaṃ upahantvāna[^373-23] pacchā aññaṃ vihiṃsati. Yo ca rakkhati attānaṃ, rakkhito[^373-24] tassa[^373-25] bāhiro; tasmā rakkheyya attānaṃ[^373-26], akkhato[^373-27] paṇḍito sadā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 374]*

Dhammikavaggo pañcamo[^374-1]. Tass'; uddānaṃ: Nāgamigasālā[^374-2] iṇaṃ Cunda[^374-3] dve 'va[^374-4] sandiṭṭhikaṃ[^374-5] Khema indriya[^374-6] Ānanda[^374-7] khattiya[^374-6] appamādena[^374-8] Dhammiko ti[^374-9]. DUTIYA-PAṆṆĀSAKO. LV.

1. Evam[^374-10] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Soṇo Rājagahe viharati Sītavanasmiṃ. Atha kho āyasmato Soṇassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi ‘ye kho keci Bhagavato sāvakā āraddhaviriyā viharanti, ahaṃ tesaṃ aññataro, atha ca pana me na[^374-11] anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati, saṃvijjanti[^374-12] kho pana me kule bhogā, sakkā bhogā[^374-13] ca bhuñjituṃ puññāni ca kātuṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā[^374-14] bhoge ca bhuñjeyyaṃ puññāni ca kareyyan'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmato Soṇassa cetasā cetoparivitakkam aññāya, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^374-15] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva kho Gijjhakūṭe pabbate antarahito Sītavane āyasmato Soṇassa sammukhe[^374-16] pāturahosi. Nisīdi Bhagavā paññatte āsane, āyasmā pi kho Soṇo Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Soṇaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca


<!-- PTS Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 375]*

‘nanu te Soṇa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi: ye kho keci Bhagavato sāvakā āraddhaviriyā viharanti, ahaṃ tesaṃ[^375-1] aññataro, atha ca pana me na anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati, saṃvijjanti[^375-2] kho pana me kule bhogā, sakkā bhogā[^375-3] ca bhuñjituṃ puññāni ca kātuṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā[^375-4] bhoge ca bhuñjeyyaṃ puññāni ca kareyyan'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; ‘Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Soṇa, kusalo tvaṃ pubbe āgārikabhūto[^375-5] vīṇāya tantissare'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.' ‘Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Soṇa, yadā[^375-6] te vīṇāya tantiyo accāyatā honti, api nu te vīṇā tasmiṃ samaye saravatī[^375-7] vā hoti kammaññā[^375-8] vā'; ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.'; ‘Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Sona, yadā te vīṇāya tantiyo atisithilā honti, api nu te vīṇā tasmiṃ samaye saravatī[^375-9] vā hoti kammaññā[^375-8] vā' ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.'; ‘Yadā pana te Soṇa vīṇāya tantiyo na accāyatā honti na[^375-10] atisithilā[^375-10] same[^375-11] guṇe patiṭṭhitā, api nu te vīṇā tasmiṃ samaye saravatī[^375-12] vā hoti kammaññā[^375-13] vā'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante.'; ‘Evam eva kho Soṇa accāraddhaviriyaṃ[^375-14] uddhaccāya saṃvattati, atilīnaviriyaṃ[^375-15] kosajjāya saṃvattati. Tasmā ti ha tvaṃ Soṇa viriyasamataṃ[^375-16] adhiṭṭhaha[^375-17] indriyānañ ca samataṃ paṭivijjha[^375-18] tattha ca nimittaṃ gaṇhāhī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Soṇo Bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Soṇaṃ iminā ovādena ovaditvā, seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva kho Sītavane antarahito Gijjhakūṭe pabbate pāturahosi.


<!-- PTS Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 376]*

2. Atha kho āyasmā Soṇo aparena samayena viriyasamataṃ[^376-1] adhiṭṭhahi[^376-2] indriyānañ[^376-3] ca samataṃ paṭivijjhi tattha ca nimittaṃ aggahesi. Atha kho āyasmā Soṇo eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto na cirass' eva yass'; atthāya kulaputtā samma-d-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi, ‘khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyā'; ti abbhaññāsi. Aññataro ca panāyasmā[^376-4] Soṇo arahataṃ ahosi. Atha kho āyasmato Soṇassa arahattappattassa[^376-5] etad ahosi ‘yan nūnāhaṃ yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkameyyaṃ, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato santike aññaṃ vyākareyyan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Soṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Soṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:

3. Yo so bhante bhikkhu arahaṃ[^376-6] khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho parikkhīṇabhavasaññojano[^376-7] sammadaññā vimutto, so cha ṭṭhānāni adhimutto hoti: nekkhammādhimutto[^376-8] hoti, pavivekādhimutto hoti, avyāpajjhādhimutto hoti, taṇhakkhayādhimutto hoti, upādānakkhayādhimutto[^376-9] hoti, asammohādhimutto hoti.

4. Siyā kho pana bhante idh'; ekaccassa āyasmato evam assa ‘kevalaṃ saddhāmattakaṃ nūna ayam[^376-10] āyasmā nissāya nekkhammādhimutto'; ti[^376-11]. Na kho pan'etaṃ[^376-12] bhante evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Khīṇāsavo bhante bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyo[^376-13] karaṇīyaṃ[^376-14] attano asamanupassanto katassa vā paticayaṃ[^376-15] khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā nekkhammādhimutto[^376-16] hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā nekkhammādhimutto[^376-16] hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā nekkhammādhimutto[^376-16] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 377]*

5. Siyā kho pana bhante idh'; ekaccassa āyasmato evam assa ‘lābhasakkārasilokaṃ nūna ayam āyasmā nikāmayamāno[^377-1] pavivekādhimutto'; ti[^377-2]. Na kho pan'etaṃ bhante evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Khīṇāsavo bhante bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyo[^377-3] karaṇīyaṃ[^377-4] attano asamanupassanto katassa vā paticayaṃ[^377-5] khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā pavivekādhimutto hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā pavivekādhimutto hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā pavivekādhimutto hoti. 6-9. Siyā kho pana bhante idh'; ekaccassa āyasmato evam assa ‘sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ nūna ayam āyasmā sārato paccāgacchanto avyāpajjhādhimutto'; ti[^377-2]. Na kho pan'etaṃ bhante evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Khīṇāsavo bhante bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyo[^377-3] karaṇīyaṃ[^377-4] attano asamanupassanto katassa vā paticayaṃ[^377-5] khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā avyāpajjhādhimutto hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā[^377-6] avyāpajjhādhimutto hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā avyāpajjhādhimutto hoti . . .[^377-7] khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā taṇhakkhayādhimutto hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā taṇhakkhayādhimutto hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā taṇhakkhayādhimutto hoti . . . khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā upādānakkhayādhimutto hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā upādānakkhayādhimutto hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā upādānakkhayādhimutto hoti . . . khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā asammohādhimutto hoti, khayā dosassa vītadosattā asammohādhimutto hoti, khayā mohassa vītamohattā asammohādhimutto hoti.

10. Evaṃ sammāvimuttacittassa bhante bhikkhuno bhusā[^377-8] ce pi cakkhuviññeyyā[^377-9] rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ[^377-10] āgacchanti, nev'assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam[^377-11] ev'assa cittaṃ hoti, ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ[^377-12], vayañ c'; assānu-


<!-- PTS Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 378]*

passati. Bhusā ce pi sotaviññeyyā saddā . . . pe[^378-1] . . . ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā . . . manoviññeyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nev'assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev'assa cittaṃ hoti, ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ, vayañ c'; assānupassati.

11. Seyyathā pi bhante selo pabbato acchiddo asusiro[^378-2] ekaghano[^378-3], atha puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, neva naṃ[^378-1] saṃkampeyya[^378-4] na sampakampeyya[^378-5] na sampavedheyya, atha pacchimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi . . . pe[^378-6] . . . atha uttarāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā[^378-7] vātavuṭṭhi[^378-7] . . . atha dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, neva[^378-8] naṃ[^378-1] saṃkampeyya[^378-9] na sampakampeyya[^378-10] na sampavedheyya: evam eva kho bhante evaṃ sammāvimuttacittassa bhikkhuno bhusā ce pi cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti[^378-11], nev'assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev'assa cittaṃ hoti, ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ, vayañ c'; assānupassati, bhusā ce pi sotaviññeyyā saddā . . . pe[^378-1] . . . ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā . . . manoviññeyyā dhammā manassa[^378-12] āpāthaṃ. āgacchanti[^378-13], nev'assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev'assa cittaṃ hoti, ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ, vayañ c'; assānupassatī ti. Nekkhammam[^378-14] adhimuttassa pavivekañ ca cetaso avyāpajjhādhimuttassa upādānakkhayassa ca taṇhakkhayādhimuttassa asammohañ ca cetaso disvā āyatanuppādaṃ sammā cittaṃ[^378-15] vimuccati. Tassa sammāvimuttassa santacittassa bhikkhuno katassa paticayo natthi karaṇīyaṃ na vijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 379]*

Selo yathā ekaghano vātena na samīrati, evaṃ rūpā rasā[^379-1] saddā gandhā phassā ca kevalā iṭṭhā dhammā aniṭṭhā ca na ppavedhenti tādino, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ vippamuttaṃ[^379-2], vayañ c'; assānupassatī ti. LVI.

1. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Phagguno[^379-3] ābādhiko[^379-4] hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘āyasmā bhante Phagguno ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno, sādhu bhante Bhagavā yen'; āyasmā Phagguno ten'; upasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā'; ti. Adhivāsesi Bhagavā tuṇhībhāvena. Atha kho Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ[^379-5] paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yen'; āyasmā[^379-6] Phagguno ten'; upasaṅkami. Addasā kho āyasmā Phagguno Bhagavantaṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvā[^379-7] mañcake samañco[^379-8] pi[^379-8]. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Phaggunaṃ etad avoca ‘alaṃ Phagguna, mā tvaṃ mañcake samañco[^379-8] pi[^379-8]. Sant'imāni āsanāni pure[^379-9] paññattāni, tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī'; ti. Nisīdi Bhagavā paññatte āsane. Nisajja kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Phaggunaṃ etad avoca ‘kacci te Phagguna khamanīyaṃ, kacci yāpanīyaṃ, kacci dukkhā[^379-10] vedanā paṭikkamanti no abhikkamanti, paṭikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no abhikkamo' ti? ‘Na me bhante khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti, abhikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no paṭikkamo[^379-11]. Seyyathā pi bhante balavā puriso tiṇhena sikharena muddhānaṃ[^379-12] abhimattheyya[^379-13], evam eva kho me[^379-14] bhante adhimattā vātā mud-


<!-- PTS Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 380]*

dhānaṃ[^380-1] hananti[^380-2]; na me bhante khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti, abhikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no paṭikkamo[^380-3]. Seyyathā pi bhante balavā puriso daḷhena varattakkhaṇḍena[^380-4] sīse[^380-5] sīsaveṭhanaṃ[^380-6] dadeyya, evam eva kho me[^380-7] bhante[^380-5] adhimattā sīse sīsavedanā; na me bhante khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti, abhikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no paṭikkamo[^380-3]. Seyyathā pi bhante dakkho goghātako vā goghātakantevāsī vā tiṇhena[^380-8] govikantanena[^380-9] kucchiṃ[^380-10] parikanteyya, evam eva kho me[^380-5] bhante adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ[^380-10] parikantanti[^380-11]; na me bhante khamanīyaṃ, na[^380-12] yāpanīyaṃ[^380-12], bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti, abhikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no paṭikkamo[^380-3]. Seyyathā pi bhante dve balavanto[^380-13] purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahetvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpeyyuṃ[^380-14] paritāpeyyuṃ[^380-15], evam eva kho bhante adhimatto[^380-16] kāyasmiṃ[^380-17] ḍāho; na me bhante khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkhamanti no paṭikkamanti, abhikkamosānaṃ paññāyati no paṭikkamo'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Phaggunaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassetvā samādapetvā samuttejetvā sampahaṃsetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi.

2. Atha kho āyasmā Phagguno acirapakkantassa Bhagavato kālam akāsi. Tamhi c'assa samaye maraṇakāle indriyāni vippasīdiṃsu. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena


<!-- PTS Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 381]*

Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘āyasmā bhante Phagguno acirapakkantassa Bhagavato kālam akāsi; tamhi c'assa samaye maraṇakāle indriyāni vippasīdiṃsū'; ti. Kiṃ h'; Ānanda[^381-1] Phaggunassa bhikkhuno indriyāni na[^381-2] vippasīdissanti? Phaggunassa Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ[^381-3] ahosi. Tassa taṃ dhammadesanaṃ sutvā pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ[^381-4]. Cha yime Ānanda ānisaṃsā kālena dhammasavane kālena atthupaparikkhāya[^381-5]. Katame cha?

3. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ[^381-6] hoti. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle labhati Tathāgataṃ[^381-7] dassanāya. Tassa[^381-2] Tathāgato dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti[^381-8]. Tassa taṃ dhammadesanaṃ sutvā pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda paṭhamo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane.

4. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ[^381-9] hoti. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle na h'eva[^381-10] kho labhati Tathāgataṃ dassanāya, api ca kho Tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya. Tassa[^381-2] Tathāgatasāvako dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Tassa taṃ[^381-11] dhammadesanaṃ sutvā pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda dutiyo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane.

5. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi oram-


<!-- PTS Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 382]*

bhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hoti. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle na h'eva[^382-1] kho labhati Tathāgataṃ dassanāya, na[^382-2] pi Tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya, api ca kho yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā anuvitakketi anuvicāreti[^382-3] manasānupekkhati[^382-4]. Tassa yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā anuvitakkayato anuvicārayato manasānupekkhato pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda tatiyo ānisaṃso kālena atthupaparikkhāya[^382-5].

6. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hoti, anuttare ca kho upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ avimuttaṃ[^382-6] hoti. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle labhati Tathāgataṃ dassanāya. Tassa[^382-7] Tathāgato dhammaṃ deseti[^382-8] ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^382-9] . . . brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Tassa taṃ dhammadesanaṃ sutvā anuttare upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda catuttho ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane.

7. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hoti, anuttare ca[^382-10] kho[^382-10] upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ avimuttaṃ[^382-11] hoti[^382-12]. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle na h'eva[^382-13] kho labhati Tathāgataṃ dassanāya, api ca kho Tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya. Tassa Tathāgatasāvako dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^382-14] . . . parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Tassa taṃ dhammadesanaṃ sutvā anuttare upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda pañcamo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane.

8. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda bhikkhuno pañcahi orambhāgiyehi saṃyojanehi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ[^382-15] hoti, anut-


<!-- PTS Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 383]*

tare ca kho upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hoti. So tamhi samaye maraṇakāle na h'eva[^383-1] kho labhati Tathāgataṃ dassanāya, na[^383-2] pi Tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya, api ca kho yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā[^383-3] anuvitakketi anuvicāreti[^383-4] manasānupekkhati. Tassa yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ cetasā[^383-5] anuvitakkayato anuvicārayato manasānupekkhato anuttare upadhisaṃkhaye cittaṃ vimuccati. Ayaṃ Ānanda chaṭṭho ānisaṃso kālena atthupaparikkhāya[^383-6]. Ime kho Ānanda cha ānisaṃsā kālena dhammasavane kālena atthupaparikkhāyā[^383-7] ti. LVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Pūraṇena bhante Kassapena chaḷābhijātiyo paññattā: kaṇhābhijāti[^383-8] paññattā, nīlābhijāti paññattā, lohitābhijāti paññattā, haliddābhijāti paññattā, sukkābhijāti paññattā, paramasukkābhijāti paññattā. Tatr'; idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena kaṇhābhijāti paññattā: orabbhikā sūkarikā[^383-9] sākuṇikā māgavikā[^383-10] luddā macchaghātakā corā[^383-11] coraghātakā bandhanāgārikā ye vā pan'; aññe[^383-12] pi keci kurūrakammantā. Tatr'; idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena nīlābhijāti paññattā: bhikkhū[^383-11] kaṇḍakavuttikā[^383-13] ye vā pan'; aññe[^383-12] pi keci[^383-14] kammavādā kiriyavādā[^383-15]. Tatr'; idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena lohitābhijāti paññattā: nigaṇṭhā ekasā-


<!-- PTS Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 384]*

ṭakā[^384-1]. Tatr'; idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena haliddābhijāti paññattā: gihī odātavasanā acelakasāvakā. Tatr' idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena sukkābhijāti[^384-2] paññattā: ājīvakā ājīvakiniyo[^384-3]. Tatr'; idaṃ bhante Pūraṇena Kassapena paramasukkābhijāti paññattā: Nando Vaccho, Kiso[^384-4] Saṅkicco[^384-5], Makkhali[^384-6] Gosālo. Pūraṇena bhante Kassapena imā[^384-7] chaḷābhijātiyo paññattā ti.

3. Kiṃ[^384-8] pan'; Ānanda[^384-9] Pūraṇassa Kassapassa sabbo loko etad abbhanujānāti imā chaḷābhijātiyo paññāpetun ti? ‘No h'etaṃ bhante.' Seyyaṭhā pi Ānanda puriso daliddo assako[^384-10] anāḷiko[^384-11], tassa akāmakassa[^384-12] bilaṃ olaggeyyuṃ[^384-13] ‘idaṃ te ambho purisa maṃsañ ca[^384-7] khāditabbaṃ mūlañ ca anuppadātabban'; ti, evam eva kho Ānanda Pūraṇena Kassapena appaṭiññāya etesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ imā chaḷābhijātiyo paññattā yathā taṃ bālena avyattena akhettaññunā akusalena. Ahaṃ kho pan'; Ānanda chaḷābhijātiyo paññāpemi, taṃ suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:-- Katamā[^384-14] c'Ānanda chaḷābhijātiyo?

4. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco kaṇhābhijātiyo samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, idha[^384-15] pan'; Ānanda ekacco kaṇhābhijātiyo samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, idha[^384-15] pan' Ānanda ekacco kaṇhābhijātiyo[^384-16] samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati[^384-17], idha[^384-15] pan'; Ānanda ekacco


<!-- PTS Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 385]*

sukkābhijātiyo samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, idha pan'; Ānanda ekacco sukkābhijātiyo samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, idha pan'; Ānanda ekacco sukkābhijātiyo samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati?

5. Idh[^385-1]'; Ānanda ekacco nīce kule paccājāto hoti caṇḍālakule vā nesādakule vā veṇakule vā rathakārakule vā pukkusakule vā dalidde appannapānabhojane kasiravuttike[^385-2], yattha kasirena ghāsacchādo labbhati, so ca hoti dubbaṇṇo duddasiko okoṭimako bahvābādho[^385-3] kāṇo vā kuṇi vā khañjo vā pakkhahato[^385-4] vā, na[^385-5] lābhī annassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilepanassa seyyāvasathapadīpeyyassa. So kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya duccaritaṃ carati, manasā duccaritaṃ carati. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duccaritaṃ caritvā, manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^385-6]. Evaṃ kho Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo[^385-7] samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo[^385-7] samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati?

6. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco nīce kule paccājāto hoti caṇḍālakule vā . . . pe[^385-8] . . . seyyāvasathapadīpeyyassa. So kāyena sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati. So kāyena sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^385-6]. Evaṃ kho Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo[^385-7] samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo[^385-8] samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati?

7. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco nīce kule paccājāto hoti caṇḍālakule vā . . . pe[^385-9] . . . so ca[^385-10] hoti dubbaṇṇo duddasiko


<!-- PTS Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 386]*

okoṭimako. So kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^386-1] pabbajati. So evaṃ pabbajito samāno pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe catūsu[^386-2] satipaṭṭhānesu supaṭṭhitacitto[^386-3] satta bojjhaṅge yathābhūtaṃ bhāvetvā akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati. Evaṃ kho Ānanda kaṇhābhijātiyo[^386-4] samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati?

8. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco ucce kule paccājāto hoti khattiyamahāsālakule vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakule vā gahapatimahāsālakule vā aḍḍhe[^386-5] mahaddhane mahābhoge pahūtajātarūparajate[^386-6] pahūtavittūpakaraṇe[^386-7] pahūtadhanadhaññe[^386-7]. So ca hoti abhirūpo dassanīyo pāsādiko paramāya vaṇṇapokkharatāya samannāgato, lābhī annassa pānassa vatthassa[^386-8] yānassa mālāgandhavilepanassa seyyāvasathapadīpeyyassa. So kāyena duccaritaṃ carati, vācāya duccaritaṃ carati, manasā duccaritaṃ carati. So kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duccaritaṃ caritvā, manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^386-9]. Evaṃ kho Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati?

9. Idh'; Ānanda ekacco ucce kule paccājāto hoti khattiyamahāsālakule vā . . . pe[^386-10] . . . seyyāvasathapadīpeyyassa. So kāyena sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati. So kāyena sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjati[^386-9]. Evaṃ kho Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno sukkaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati. Kathañ c'Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati?


<!-- PTS Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 387]*

10. Idh'[^387-1] Ānanda ekacco[^387-2] ucce kule paccājāto hoti khattiyamahāsālakule vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakule vā gahapatimahāsālakule vā aḍḍhe[^387-3] mahaddhane mahābhoge pahūtajātarūparajate[^387-4] pahūtavittūpakaraṇe[^387-5] pahūtadhanadhaññe[^387-5]. So ca hoti abhirūpo dassanīyo pāsādiko paramāya vaṇṇapokkharatāya samannāgato, lābhī annassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilepanassa seyyāvasathapadīpeyyassa. So kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^387-6] pabbajati. So evaṃ pabbajito samāno pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe catūsu satipaṭṭhānesu supaṭṭhitacitto[^387-7] satta bojjhaṅge yathābhūtaṃ bhāvetvā akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati. Evaṃ kho Ānanda sukkābhijātiyo samāno akaṇhaṃ asukkaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati. Imā kho Ānanda chaḷābhijātiyo ti. LVIII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti[^387-8] pāhuneyyo[^387-9] dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno ye āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā, te saṃvarena pahīnā honti, ye āsavā paṭisevanā pahātabbā, te paṭisevanāya[^387-10] pahīnā honti, ye āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā, te adhivāsanāya pahīnā honti, ye āsavā parivajjanā pahātabbā, te parivajjanāya pahīnā honti, ye āsavā vinodanā pahātabbā, te vinodanāya pahīnā honti, ye āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā, te bhāvanāya pahīnā honti. Katame ca[^387-11] bhikkhave āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā, ye saṃvarena pahīnā honti?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso cakkhundriyasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave cakkhundriyasaṃvara-asaṃvutassa viharato uppajjeyyuṃ[^387-12]


<!-- PTS Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 388]*

āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, cakkhundriyasaṃvarasaṃvutassa viharato evaṃsa[^388-1] te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Paṭisaṅkhā yoniso sotindriya- . . . ghānindriya- . . . jivhindriya- . . . kāyindriya- . . . manindriyasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave manindriyasaṃvara-asaṃvutassa viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, manindriyasaṃvarasaṃvutassa viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Yaṃ[^388-2] hi 'ssa bhikkhave saṃvaraasaṃvutassa viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, saṃvarasaṃvutassa viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā, ye[^388-3] saṃvarena pahīnā honti. Katame ca bhikkhave āsavā paṭisevanā pahātabbā, ye paṭisevanāya pahīnā honti?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso cīvaraṃ paṭisevati yāva-d-eva sītassa paṭighātāya uṇhassa paṭighātāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassānaṃ[^388-4] paṭighātāya yāva-d-eva hirikopīnapaṭicchādanatthaṃ[^388-5]. Paṭisaṅkhā yoniso piṇḍapātaṃ paṭisevati[^388-6] neva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya yāva-d-eva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya ‘iti purāṇañ ca vedanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi navañ ca vedanaṃ na uppādessāmi yātrā[^388-7] ca me bhavissati anavajjatā ca phāsuvihāro cā 'ti. Paṭisaṅkhā yoniso senāsanaṃ paṭisevati yāva-d-eva sītassa paṭighātāya uṇhassa paṭighātāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassānaṃ[^388-4] paṭighātāya yāva-d-eva utuparissayavinodanaṃ[^388-8] paṭisallānārāmatthaṃ[^388-9]. Paṭisaṅkhā yoniso gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ paṭisevati yāva-d-eva uppannānaṃ veyyābādhikānaṃ[^388-10] vedanānaṃ paṭighātāya avyāpajjhaparamatāya[^388-11].


<!-- PTS Page 389 -->
<a id="page-389"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 389]*

Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave appaṭisevato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, paṭisevato[^389-1] evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā paṭisevanā pahātabbā, ye paṭisevanāya pahīnā honti. Katame ca bhikkhave āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā, ye adhivāsanāya pahīnā honti?

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso khamo hoti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassānaṃ[^389-2] duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppannānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vedanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātiko[^389-3] hoti. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave anadhivāsayato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, adhivāsayato[^389-4] evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā, ye adhivāsanāya pahīnā honti. Katame ca bhikkhave āsavā parivajjanā pahātabbā, ye parivajjanāya pahīnā honti?

6. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso caṇḍaṃ[^389-5] hatthiṃ parivajjeti, caṇḍaṃ[^389-6] assaṃ parivajjeti, caṇḍaṃ[^389-6] goṇaṃ parivajjeti, caṇḍaṃ[^389-6] kukkuraṃ parivajjeti, ahiṃ khāṇuṃ[^389-7] kaṇṭakaṭṭhānaṃ[^389-8] sobbhaṃ papātaṃ[^389-9] candanikaṃ[^389-10] oligallaṃ[^389-11], yathārūpe anāsane nisinnaṃ yathārūpe agocare carantaṃ yathārūpe pāpake mitte bhajantaṃ viññū sabrahmacārī[^389-12] pāpakesu ṭhānesu okappeyyuṃ[^389-13], so tañ c'eva[^389-14] anāsanaṃ tañ ca agocaraṃ te ca pāpake mitte paṭisaṅkhā yoniso parivajjeti. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave aparivajjayato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, parivajjayato[^389-15] evaṃsa[^389-16] te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti.


<!-- PTS Page 390 -->
<a id="page-390"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 390]*

Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā parivajjanā pahātabbā, ye parivajjanāya pahīnā honti. Katame ca bhikkhave āsavā vinodanā pahātabbā, ye vinodanāya pahīnā honti?

7. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso uppannaṃ kāmavitakkaṃ nādhivāseti pajahati vinodeti vyantikaroti[^390-1] anabhāvaṃ gameti, paṭisaṅkhā yoniso uppannaṃ vyāpādavitakkaṃ . . . pe[^390-2] . . . uppannaṃ vihiṃsāvitakkaṃ . . . pe[^390-3] . . . uppannuppanne pāpake akusale dhamme nādhivāseti pajahati vinodeti vyantikaroti[^390-4] anabhāvaṃ gameti. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave avinodayato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, vinodayato[^390-5] evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā vinodanā pahātabbā, ye vinodanāya pahīnā honti. Katame ca bhikkhave āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā, ye bhāvanāya pahīnā honti?

8. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yoniso satisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ, paṭisaṅkhā yoniso dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti[^390-6], viriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti, pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti, passaddhisambojjhaṅghaṃ bhāveti, samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ[^390-7] bhāveti[^390-7], upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ[^390-8] bhāveti[^390-8] vivekanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ[^390-9]. Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave abhāvayato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, bhāvayato[^390-10] evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā, ye bhāvanāya pahīnā honti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo[^390-11] dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 391 -->
<a id="page-391"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 391]*

LIX.

1. Evam[^391-1] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Nādike[^391-2] viharati Giñjakāvasathe. Atha kho dārukammiko gahapati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho dārukammikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Api nu te gahapati kule dānaṃ dīyatī ti? ‘Dīyati me bhante kule dānaṃ tañ ca kho, ye te bhikkhū āraññakā[^391-3] piṇḍapātikā paṃsukulikā arahanto[^391-4] vā arahattamaggaṃ[^391-5] vā samāpannā, tathārūpesu bhante bhikkhūsu dānaṃ dīyatī'; ti.

3. Dujjānaṃ kho etaṃ gahapati tayā gihinā kāmabhoginā puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantena Kāsikacandanaṃ[^391-6] paccanubhontena mālāgandhavilepanaṃ dhārayantena jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantena[^391-7] ‘ime vā arahanto ime vā arahattamaggaṃ[^391-5] samāpannā[^391-8]'; ti. Āraññako[^391-9] ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato unnaḷo[^391-10] capalo mukharo vikiṇṇavāco muṭṭhassati[^391-11] asampajāno asamāhito vibbhantacitto pākaṭindriyo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Āraññako[^391-12] ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato anunnaḷo[^391-13] acapalo amukharo avikiṇṇavāco upaṭṭhitasati sampajāno samāhito ekaggacitto saṃvutindriyo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Gāmantavihārī ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato . . . pe[^391-14] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Gāmantavihārī ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato . . . pe[^391-15] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Piṇḍapātiko ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato . . . pe[^391-15] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Piṇḍapātiko ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato


<!-- PTS Page 392 -->
<a id="page-392"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 392]*

. . . pe[^392-1] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Nemantaniko[^392-2] ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato . . . pe[^392-1] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Nemantaniko[^392-2] ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato . . . pe[^392-1] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Paṃsukuliko ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato . . . pe[^392-1] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Paṃsukuliko ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato . . . pe[^392-3] . . . evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Gahapaticīvaradharo ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti uddhato unnaḷo[^392-4] capalo mukharo vikiṇṇavāco muṭṭhassati asampajāno asamāhito vibbhantacitto pākaṭindriyo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena gārayho. Gahapaticīvaradharo ce pi gahapati bhikkhu hoti anuddhato anunnaḷo[^392-5] acapalo amukharo avikiṇṇavāco upaṭṭhitasati sampajāno samāhito ekaggacitto saṃvutindriyo, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena pāsaṃso. Iṅgha tvaṃ gahapati saṅghe dānaṃ[^392-6] dehi. Saṅghe te dānaṃ dadato cittaṃ pasīdissati, so tvaṃ pasannacitto kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjissatī[^392-7] ti ‘Esāhaṃ bhante ajja-t-agge saṅghe dānaṃ[^392-8] dassāmī'; ti. LX. Evam[^392-9] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati Isipatane Migadāye. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā therā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantā maṇḍalamāḷe sannisinnā sannipatitā abhidhammakathaṃ kathenti. Tatra sudaṃ āyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ kathentānaṃ antarantarā kathaṃ opāteti. Atha kho āyasmā Mahākoṭṭhito[^392-10] āyasmantaṃ Cittaṃ Hatthisāriputtaṃ etad avoca ‘mā[^392-11] āyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ kathentānaṃ antarantarā


<!-- PTS Page 393 -->
<a id="page-393"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 393]*

kathaṃ opātesi[^393-1], kathāpariyosānaṃ[^393-2] āyasmā Citto[^393-3] āgametū'; ti. Evaṃ vutte āyasmato Cittassa Hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ Mahākoṭṭhitaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘mā[^393-4] āyasmā Koṭṭhito[^393-5] āyasmantaṃ Cittaṃ Hatthisāriputtaṃ apasādesi[^393-6]. Paṇḍito āyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto pahoti c'; āyasmā[^393-7] Citto Hatthisāriputto therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ kathetun'; ti. Dujjānaṃ[^393-8] kho etaṃ[^393-9] āvuso parassa cetopariyāyaṃ ajānantehi.

2. Idhāvuso ekacco puggalo tāva-d-eva soratasorato hoti nivātanivāto hoti upasantupasanto hoti, yāva Satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati aññataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ. Yato ca kho so vapakassat'eva Satthārā, vapakassati garuṭṭhāniyehi sabrahmacārīhi, so saṃsaṭṭho viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi[^393-10] raññā rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi[^393-11] titthiyasāvakehi[^393-12], tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissaṭṭhassa pākaṭassa bhassam anuyuttassa viharato rāgo cittaṃ anuddhaṃseti, so rāgānuddhaṃsena[^393-13] cittena sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattati[^393-14]. Seyyathā pi āvuso goṇo kiṭṭhādo dāmena[^393-15] vā baddho vaje[^393-16] vā oruddho, yo nu kho āvuso evaṃ vadeyya <<na dānāyaṃ goṇo kiṭṭhādo puna-d-eva kiṭṭhaṃ otarissatī>> ti, sammā nu kho so[^393-17] āvuso vadamāno vadeyyā ti[^393-18]? ‘No h'; idaṃ āvuso, ṭhānaṃ h'etaṃ[^393-19] āvuso vijjati, yaṃ so[^393-20] goṇo kiṭṭhādo dāmaṃ[^393-21] vā chetvā vajaṃ vā bhinditvā[^393-22] puna-d-eva kiṭṭhaṃ otareyyā'; ti. Evam eva kho āvuso idh'; ekacco pug-

Yato ca kho so vapakassat'eva Satthārā, vapakassati garuṭṭhāniyehi sabrahmacārīhi, so saṃsaṭṭho viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi[^394-1] raññā[^394-2] rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi, tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissaṭṭhassa pākaṭassa bhassam anuyuttassa viharato rāgo cittaṃ anuddhaṃseti, so rāgānuddhaṃsena cittena sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattati[^394-3].

Idha panāvuso ekacco puggalo sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ cetosamādhiṃ upasampajja viharati, so ‘lābhī 'mhi animittassa cetosamādhissā'; ti saṃsaṭṭho viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi[^397-13] raññā[^397-14] rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi, tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissaṭṭhassa[^397-15] pākaṭassa bhassam[^397-16] anuyuttassa viharato rāgo cittaṃ anuddhaṃseti, so rāgānuddhaṃsena cittena sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattati[^397-12]. Seyyathā pi āvuso rājā vā rājamahāmatto vā caturaṅginiyā senāya addhānamaggapaṭipanno aññatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍe ekarattiṃ[^397-17] vāsaṃ upagaccheyya, tattha[^397-18] hatthisaddena assasaddena rathasaddena pattisaddena bheripaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavaninnādasaddena[^397-19] cīriḷikāsaddo[^397-20] antaradhāpeyya[^397-21], yo nu kho āvuso evaṃ vadeyya <<na[^397-22] dāni[^397-23] amusmiṃ vanasaṇḍe puna-d-eva cīriḷikāsaddo[^397-24] pātubhavissatī>> ti, sammā nu kho

Evam eva kho āvuso idh'; ekacco puggalo sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ cetosamādhiṃ upasampajja viharati, so ‘lābhī 'mhi animittassa cetosamādhissā'; ti saṃsaṭṭho viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi[^398-2] raññā[^398-3] rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi, tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissaṭṭhassa[^398-4] pākaṭassa bhassam[^398-5] anuyuttassa viharato rāgo cittaṃ anuddhaṃseti, so rāgānuddhaṃsena cittena sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattatī[^398-6] ti.

8. Atha kho āyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto[^398-7] aparena samayena sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattati[^398-6]. Atha kho Cittassa Hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā bhikkhū yen'; āyasmā Mahākoṭṭhito ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mohākoṭṭhitaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘kiṃ nu kho āyasmatā Mahākoṭṭhitena Citto Hatthisāriputto cetasā ceto paricca vidito: imāsañ ca imāsañ ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ Citto Hatthisāriputto lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī[^398-6] ti, udāhu devatā etam atthaṃ ārocesuṃ: Citto bhante Hatthisāriputto imāsañ ca imāsañ ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī'[^398-7] ti? ‘Cetaso ceto paricca vidito me āvuso Citto Hatthisāriputto: imāsañ ca imāsañ ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī[^398-6] ti, devatā pi me etam atthaṃ ārocesuṃ: Citto bhante Hatthisāriputto imāsañ ca imāsañ ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī[^398-8]'; ti. Atha kho Cittassa Hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekaman-


<!-- PTS Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 399]*

taṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘Citto bhante Hatthisāriputto imāsañ ca imāsañ ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvatto[^399-1]'; ti. ‘Na bhikkhave Citto ciraṃ sarissati[^399-2] nekkhammassā[^399-3]'; ti.

9. Atha kho Citto Hatthisāriputto na cirass'eva kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji[^399-4]. Atha kho āyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto eko[^399-5] vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto na cirass'eva yass'; atthāya kulaputtā samma-d-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi[^399-6], ‘khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyā'; ti abbhaññāsi, aññataro ca panāyasmā Citto Hatthisāriputto arahataṃ ahosī ti. LXI.

1. Evam[^399-7] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati Isipatane Migadāye. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulānaṃ therānaṃ[^399-8] bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantānaṃ maṇḍalamāle[^399-9] sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘vuttam idaṃ āvuso Bhagavatā pārāyane[^399-10] Metteyyapañhe: Yo[^399-11] ubh'; ante[^399-12] viditvāna[^399-13] majjhe mantā na limpati taṃ brūmi mahāpuriso ti[^399-7], so 'dha[^399-14] sibbanim[^399-15] accagā ti. Katamo nu kho āvuso eko anto, katamo dutiyo anto, kiṃ majjhe, kā sibbanī[^399-16]'; ti?

2. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘phasso kho āvuso eko anto, phassasamudayo dutiyo


<!-- PTS Page 400 -->
<a id="page-400"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 400]*

anto, phassanirodho majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī[^400-1]; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā[^400-2]. Ettāvatā[^400-3] kho āvuso bhikkhu abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariññeyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto[^400-4] pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto[^400-4] diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro[^400-5] hotī[^400-6]'; ti[^400-7].

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘atītaṃ kho āvuso eko anto, anāgataṃ dutiyo anto, paccuppannaṃ majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī[^400-8]; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā. Ettāvatā kho āvuso bhikkhu abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariññeyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto[^400-9] pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto[^400-9] diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro[^400-10] hotī'; ti[^400-7].

4. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘sukhā āvuso vedanā eko anto, dukkhā vedanā dutiyo anto, adukkhamasukhā vedanā majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī[^400-11]; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā. Ettāvatā[^400-12] kho āvuso bhikkhu abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānāti . . . pe[^400-13] . . . diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hotī'; ti.

5. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘nāmaṃ kho āvuso eko anto, rūpaṃ dutiyo anto, viññāṇaṃ majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī[^400-14]; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati . . . pe[^400-13] . . . diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hotī'; ti[^400-7].

6. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘cha kho[^400-15] āvuso ajjhattikāni āyatanāni eko anto, cha bāhirāni āyatanāni dutiyo anto, viññāṇaṃ majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā. Ettāvatā kho āvuso bhikkhu abhiñ-


<!-- PTS Page 401 -->
<a id="page-401"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 401]*

ñeyyaṃ abhijānāti . . . pe[^401-1] . . . diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hotī'; ti.

7. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘sakkāyo kho āvuso eko anto, sakkāyasamudayo dutiyo anto, sakkāyanirodho majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā. Ettāvatā kho āvuso bhikkhu abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariññeyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto[^401-2] pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto[^401-2] diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hotī'; ti.

8. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu there bhikkhū etad avoca ‘vyākataṃ kho āvuso amhehi sabbeh'eva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ[^401-3]. Āyāmāvuso yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamissāma, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato etam atthaṃ ārocessāma. Yathā no Bhagavā vyākarissati, tathā naṃ dhāressāmā'; ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho therā[^401-4] bhikkhū tassa bhikkhuno paccassosuṃ. Atha kho therā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho therā bhikkhū yāvatako ahosi sabbeh'eva saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesuṃ. ‘Kassa nu kho bhante subhāsitan'; ti? ‘Sabbesaṃ vo[^401-5] bhikkhave subhāsitaṃ pariyāyena, api ca yaṃ mayā sandhāya bhāsitaṃ pārāyane[^401-6] Metteyyapañhe: Yo ubh'; ante[^401-7] viditvāna[^401-8] majjhe mantā na limpati[^401-9] taṃ brūmi mahāpuriso ti[^401-10], so 'dha[^401-11] sibbanim[^401-12] accagā ti taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho therā[^401-13] bhikkhū Bhagavato paccasosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca ‘phasso kho bhikkhave eko


<!-- PTS Page 402 -->
<a id="page-402"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 402]*

anto, phassasamudayo dutiyo anto, phassanirodho majjhe, taṇhā sibbanī[^402-1]; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tass'eva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā. Ettāvatā kho bhikkhave bhikkhu abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariññeyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto[^402-2] pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto[^402-2] diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hotī'; ti. LXII.

1. Evam[^402-3] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosalesu cārikaṃ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ yena Daṇḍakappakaṃ nāma Kosalānaṃ nigamo tad avasari. Atha kho Bhagavā maggā okkamma[^402-4] aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle paññatte āsane nisīdi, te[^402-5] ca[^402-3] bhikkhū Daṇḍakappakaṃ pavisiṃsu āvasathaṃ pariyesituṃ. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando sambahulehi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yena Aciravatī nadī ten'; upasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Aciravatiyā[^402-6] nadiyā[^402-6] gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamāno[^402-7].

2. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yenāyasmā Ānando ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho āvuso Ānanda sabbaṃ cetaso[^402-8] samannāharitvā nu kho Devadatto Bhagavatā vyākato <<āpāyiko Devadatto nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho>> ti, udāhu kenaci devapariyāyenā'; ti? ‘Evaṃ kho pan'etaṃ āvuso Bhagavatā vyākatan'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘idhāhaṃ[^402-9] bhante sambahulehi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yena Aciravatī nadī ten'; upasaṅkamiṃ gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Aciravatiyā[^402-10] nadiyā[^402-10] gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi[^402-11] gattāni pubbāpaya-


<!-- PTS Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 403]*

māno[^403-1]. Atha kho bhante aññataro bhikkhu yenāhaṃ ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ etad avoca: Kin nu kho āvuso Ānanda sabbaṃ cetaso[^403-2] samannāharitvā nu kho Devadatto Bhagavatā vyākato <<āpāyiko Devadatto nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho>> ti, udāhu kenaci devapariyāyenā ti? Evaṃ vutte ahaṃ bhante taṃ bhikkhuṃ etad avoca[^403-3]: Evaṃ kho pan'etaṃ āvuso Bhagavatā vyākatan' ti. ‘So[^403-4] vā[^403-5] kho[^403-6] Ānanda bhikkhu navo[^403-7] bhavissati acirapabbajito, thero vā[^403-7] pana bālo avyatto. Kathaṃ hi nāma yaṃ mayā ekaṃsena vyākataṃ tattha dvejjhaṃ[^403-8] āpajjissati? Nāhaṃ Ānanda aññaṃ ekapuggalam pi[^403-9] samanupassāmi, yo[^403-10] evaṃ mayā sabbaṃ cetaso[^403-2] samannāharitvā vyākato, yathayidaṃ Devadatto. Yāvakīvañ cāhaṃ Ānanda Devadattassa vālaggakoṭinittuddanamattam[^403-11] pi sukkaṃ[^403-12] dhammaṃ addasaṃ, neva tāvāhaṃ[^403-13] Devadattaṃ vyākāsiṃ[^403-14] <<āpāyiko Devadatto nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho>> ti. Yato ca kho ahaṃ Ānanda Devadattassa vālaggakoṭinittuddanamattam pi sukkaṃ dhammaṃ na addasaṃ, athāhaṃ[^403-15] Devadattaṃ vyākāsiṃ[^403-14] <<āpāyiko Devadatto nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho>> ti. Seyyathā pi Ānanda gūthakūpo[^403-16] sādhikaporiso[^403-17] pūro gūthassa samatittiko, tatra puriso sasīsako nimuggo assa. Tassa koci-d-eva puriso uppajjeyya[^403-18] atthakāmo hitakāmo yogakkhemakāmo tamhā gūthakūpā uddharitukāmo, so taṃ gūthakūpaṃ samantānuparigacchanto[^403-19] na[^403-20] passeyya tassa purisassa vālaggakoṭinittuddanamattam pi gūthena amakkhitaṃ, yattha


<!-- PTS Page 404 -->
<a id="page-404"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 404]*

taṃ[^404-1] gahetvā[^404-2] uddhareyya[^404-3]: evam eva kho ahaṃ Ānanda yato[^404-4] Devadattassa vālaggakoṭinittuddanamattam pi sukkaṃ dhammaṃ na addasaṃ, athāhaṃ[^404-5] Devadattaṃ vyākāsiṃ <<āpāyiko Devadatto nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho>> ti. Sace tumhe Ānanda suṇeyyātha Tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇāni vibhajantassā[^404-6]'; ti? ‘Etassa Bhagavā kālo, etassa Sugata kālo, yaṃ Bhagavā purisindriyañāṇāni vibhajeyya, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī'; ti. ‘Tenah'; Ānanda suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī' ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

3. Idhāhaṃ Ānanda ekaccaṃ puggalaṃ evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa vijjamānā kusalā pi dhammā akusalā pi dhammā'; ti. Tam enaṃ aparena samayena evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa kusalā dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā, atthi ca khvāssa[^404-7] kusalamūlaṃ asamucchinnaṃ[^404-8], tamhā tassa[^404-9] kusalā kusalaṃ pātubhavissati. Evam ayaṃ[^404-10] puggalo āyatiṃ aparihānadhammo bhavissatī'; ti. Seyyathā pi Ānanda bījāni akhaṇḍāṇi apūtīni avātātapahatāni[^404-11] sāradāni[^404-12] sukhasayitāni[^404-13] sukhette suparikammakatāya bhūmiyā[^404-14] nikkhittāni; jāneyyāsi tvaṃ Ānanda ‘imāni bījāni vuddhiṃ[^404-15] virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissantī'; ti? ‘Evaṃ bhante'; . Evam eva kho ahaṃ Ānanda idh'; ekaccaṃ puggalaṃ evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa vijjamānā kusalā[^404-16] pi

Evam ayaṃ puggalo[^405-5] āyatiṃ aparihānadhammo bhavissatī' ti. Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa purisapuggalo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti. 4. Idha panāhaṃ Ānanda ekaccaṃ puggalaṃ evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa vijjamānā kusalā pi dhammā akusalā pi dhammā'; ti. Tam enaṃ aparena samayena evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa akusalā dhammā antarahitā, kusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā, atthi ca kvāssa[^405-2] akusalamūlaṃ asamucchinnaṃ, tamhā tassa[^405-3] akusalā[^405-4] akusalaṃ[^405-6] pātubhavissati. Evam ayaṃ puggalo

āyatiṃ parihānadhammo[^406-1] bhavissatī'; ti[^406-2]. Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa purisapuggalo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti. ‘sakkā nu kho bhante imesaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ apare[^407-1] pi tayo puggalā sappaṭibhāgā paññāpetun'; ti? ‘Sakk' Ānandā'; ti. Bhagavā[^407-2] avoca:--

6. Idhāhaṃ Ānanda ekaccaṃ puggalaṃ evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa vijjamānā kusalā pi dhammā akusalā pi dhammā'; ti. Evam ayaṃ puggalo āyatiṃ parihānadhammo bhavissatī'; ti. Seyyathā[^407-4] pi[^407-4] Ānanda aṅgārāni[^407-5] ādittāni sampajjalitāni sajotibhūtāni[^407-6] puthusilāya nikkhittāni; jāneyyāsi tvaṃ Ānanda ‘na yimāni aṅgārāni[^407-7] vuddhiṃ[^407-8] virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissantī[^407-9]'; ti. ‘Evam[^407-10] bhante[^407-10]'. Tam enaṃ aparena samayena evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa kusalā[^407-14] dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā, atthi ca khvāssa[^407-3] kusalamūlaṃ asamucchinnaṃ, tam pi[^407-15] sabbena sabbaṃ samugghātaṃ gacchati. Evam ayaṃ puggalo āyatiṃ parihānadhammo bhavissatī' ti. Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa purisapuggalo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti[^407-10]. Evam pi kho[^407-15] Ānanda

Evam ayaṃ puggalo āyatiṃ aparihānadhammo bhavissatī'; ti. Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa purisapuggalo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti. paricca viditaṃ hoti. Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti.

8. Idha panāhaṃ Ānanda ekaccaṃ puggalaṃ evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘imassa kho puggalassa vijjamānā kusalā pi dhammā akusalā pi dhammā'; ti. Tam enaṃ aparena samayena evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāmi ‘natthi imassa puggalassa vālaggakoṭinittuddanamatto[^409-1] pi akusalo dhammo, samannāgato[^409-2] 'yaṃ[^409-2] puggalo ekantasukkehi anavajjehi dhammehi, diṭṭh'eva dhamme parinibbāyissatī'; ti.
