Evam pi kho Ānanda Tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādo cetasā ceto paricca vidito hoti. Tatr'; Ānanda ye te purimā tayo puggalā, tesaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ eko aparihānadhammo, eko[^409-5] parihānadhammo[^409-5], eko āpāyiko nerayiko. Tatr'; Ānanda ye 'me pacchimā tayo puggalā, imesaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ eko aparihānadhammo[^409-6], eko parihānadhammo[^409-6], eko[^409-5] parinibbānadhammo[^409-7] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 410 -->
<a id="page-410"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 410]*

LXIII.

1. Nibbedhikapariyāyaṃ vo bhikkhave dhammapariyāyaṃ desessāmi, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katamo ca so[^410-1] bhikkhave nibbedhikapariyāyo dhammapariyāyo[^410-2]? Kāmā bhikkhave veditabbā, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, kāmānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, kāmānaṃ vipāko veditabbo, kāmanirodho[^410-3] veditabbo, kāmanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^410-2] veditabbā. Vedanā bhikkhave veditabbā, vedanānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, vedanānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, vedanānaṃ vipāko veditabbo, vedanānirodho veditabbo, vedanānirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā. Saññā bhikkhave veditabbā, saññānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, saññānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, saññānaṃ vipāko veditabbo, saññānirodho veditabbo, saññānirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā. Āsavā bhikkhave veditabbā, āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, āsavānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, āsavānaṃ vipāko veditabbo, āsavanirodho veditabbo, āsavanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^410-4] veditabbā. Kammaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ, kammānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, kammānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, kammānaṃ vipāko veditabbo, kammanirodho veditabbo, kammanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā. Dukkhaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ, dukkhassa nidānasambhavo veditabbo, dukkhassa vemattatā veditabbā, dukkhassa vipāko veditabbo, dukkhanirodho[^410-5] veditabbo, dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^410-6] veditabbā[^410-7].

3. Kāmā bhikkhave veditabbā, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavo veditabbo, kāmānaṃ vemattatā veditabbā, kāmānaṃ vi-


<!-- PTS Page 411 -->
<a id="page-411"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 411]*

pāko veditabbo, kāmanirodho[^411-1] veditabbo, kāmanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^411-1] veditabbā ti[^411-2]: iti[^411-3] kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Pañc'ime bhikkhave kāmaguṇā: cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā; sotaviññeyyā saddā . . .[^411-4] ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā. Api ca kho[^411-5] bhikkhave n'; ete[^411-6] kāmā[^411-7], kāmaguṇā nām'; ete[^411-8] ariyassa vinaye vuccanti[^411-9]. Saṅkapparāgo purisassa kāmo. N'; ete[^411-10] kāmā yāni citrāni loke. Saṅkapparāgo purisassa kāmo. Tiṭṭhanti citrāni tath'eva loke. Ath'; ettha dhīrā vinayanti chandan ti.

4. Katamo ca bhikkhave kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavo[^411-11]? Phasso bhikkhave kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavo. Katamā ca bhikkhave kāmānaṃ vemattatā? Añño bhikkhave kāmo rūpesu[^411-12], añño kāmo saddesu, añño kāmo gandhesu, añño kāmo rasesu, añño kāmo phoṭṭhabbesu. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave kāmānaṃ vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave kāmānaṃ vipāko? Yaṃ kho bhikkhave kāmayamāno[^411-13] tajjaṃ tajjaṃ attabhāvaṃ[^411-14] abhinibbatteti[^411-15] puññabhāgiyaṃ vā apuññabhāgiyaṃ[^411-16] vā[^411-16]. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave kāmānaṃ vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave kāmanirodho? Phassanirodho[^411-17] bhikkhave kāmanirodho. {Ayam} eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo kāmanirodhagāminipaṭipadā, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammā-


<!-- PTS Page 412 -->
<a id="page-412"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 412]*

kammanto sammā-ājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi. Yato ca[^412-1] kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ kāme pajānāti, evaṃ kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kāmānaṃ vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kāmānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kāmanirodhaṃ[^412-2] pajānāti, evaṃ kāmanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^412-2] pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti kāmanirodhaṃ[^412-2]. Kāmā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^412-3] . . . kāmanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^412-2] veditabbā ti[^412-1]: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

5. Vedanā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^412-3] . . . vedanānirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^412-4] veditabbā ti: iti kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tisso imā bhikkhave vedanā: sukhā vedanā, dukkhā vedanā, adukkhamasukhā vedanā.

6. Katamo ca bhikkhave vedanānaṃ[^412-5] nidānasambhavo? Phasso bhikkhave vedanānaṃ nidānasambhavo. Katamā ca bhikkhave vedanānaṃ vemattatā? Atthi bhikkhave sāmisā sukhā vedanā, atthi nirāmisā sukhā vedanā; atthi sāmisā dukkhā vedanā, atthi nirāmisā dukkhā vedanā; atthi sāmisā adukkhamasukhā vedanā, atthi nirāmisā adukkhamasukhā vedanā. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave vedanānaṃ vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave vedanānaṃ vipāko? Yaṃ kho bhikkhave vediyamāno[^412-6] tajjaṃ tajjaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinibbatteti puññabhāgiyaṃ vā apuññabhāgiyaṃ vā. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave vedanānaṃ vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave vedanānirodho? Phassanirodho[^412-7] bhikkhave[^412-8] vedanānirodho[^412-8]. Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo vedanānirodhagāminipaṭipadā, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^412-3] . . . sammāsamādhi. Yato ca[^412-9] kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ vedanā[^412-10] pajānāti, evaṃ vedanānaṃ nidānasambhavam pajānāti, evaṃ veda-


<!-- PTS Page 413 -->
<a id="page-413"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 413]*

nānaṃ vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ vedanānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ vedanānirodhaṃ[^413-1] pajānāti, evaṃ vedanānirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^413-1] pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti vedanānirodhaṃ[^413-2]. Vedanā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^413-3] . . . vedanānirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^413-1] veditabbā ti: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

7. Saññā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^413-3] . . . saññānirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ[^413-4] c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Cha yimā bhikkhave saññā: rūpasaññā, saddasaññā, gandhasaññā, rasasaññā, phoṭṭhabbasaññā, dhammasaññā.

8. Katamo ca bhikkhave saññānaṃ nidānasambhavo? Phasso bhikkhave saññānaṃ[^413-5] nidānasambhavo. Katamā ca bhikkhave saññānaṃ vemattatā? Aññā bhikkhave saññā[^413-6] rūpesu[^413-6], aññā saññā saddesu, aññā saññā gandhesu, aññā saññā rasesu, aññā saññā phoṭṭhabbesu, aññā saññā dhammesu. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saññānaṃ vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave saññānaṃ vipāko? Vohāravepakkāhaṃ[^413-7] bhikkhave saññā[^413-8] vadāmi; yathā yathā naṃ sañjānāti[^413-9], tathā tathā voharati ‘evaṃ saññī[^413-10] ahosin[^413-11]'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saññānaṃ vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave saññānirodho[^413-1]? Phassanirodho[^413-12] bhikkhave saññānirodho. Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo saññānirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^413-1], seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^413-3] . . . sammāsamādhi. Yato ca[^413-13] kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ saññā[^413-14] pajānāti, evaṃ saññānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ saññānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 414 -->
<a id="page-414"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 414]*

vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ saññānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ saññānirodhaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ saññānirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti saññānirodhaṃ[^414-1]. Saññā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^414-2] . . . saññānirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

9. Āsavā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^414-2] . . . āsavanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tayo 'me[^414-3] bhikkhave āsavā: kāmāsavo, bhavāsavo, avijjāsavo.

10. Katamo ca bhikkhave āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavo? Avijjā[^414-4] bhikkhave āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavo. Katamā ca bhikkhave āsavānaṃ vemattatā? Atthi bhikkhave āsavā nirayagamaniyā, atthi āsavā tiracchānayonigamaniyā, atthi āsavā pittivisayagamaniyā[^414-5], atthi āsavā manussalokagamaniyā, atthi āsavā devalokagamaniyā. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave āsavānaṃ vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave āsavānaṃ vipāko? Yaṃ kho bhikkhave avijjāgato tajjaṃ tajjaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinibbatteti puññabhāgiyaṃ vā apuññabhāgiyaṃ vā. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave āsavānaṃ vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave āsavanirodho? Avijjānirodho[^414-6] bhikkhave āsavanirodho. Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo āsavanirodhagāminipaṭipadā, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^414-2] . . . sammāsamādhi. Yato ca kho[^414-7] bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ āsave pajānāti, evaṃ āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ āsavānaṃ vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ āsavānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ āsavanirodhaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ āsavanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti āsavanirodhaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 415 -->
<a id="page-415"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 415]*

Āsavā bhikkhave veditabbā . . . pe[^415-1] . . . āsavanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

11. Kammaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ . . . pe[^415-2] . . . kammanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Cetanāhaṃ bhikkhave kammaṃ vadāmi; cetayitvā kammaṃ karoti kāyena vācāya manasā.

12. Katamo ca bhikkhave kammānaṃ nidānasambhavo? Phasso bhikkhave kammānaṃ nidānasambhavo. Katamo ca bhikkhave kammānaṃ vemattatā? Atthi bhikkhave kammaṃ nirayavedaniyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ tiracchānayonivedaniyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ pittivisayavedaniyaṃ[^415-3], atthi kammaṃ manussalokavedaniyaṃ, atthi[^415-4] kammaṃ devalokavedaniyaṃ. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave kammānaṃ vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave kammānaṃ vipāko? Tividhāhaṃ[^415-5] bhikkhave kammānaṃ vipākaṃ vadāmi: diṭṭh'eva dhamme upapajje[^415-6] vā apare[^415-7] vā pariyāye. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave kammānaṃ vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave kammanirodho? Phassanirodho[^415-8] bhikkhave kammanirodho. {Ayam} eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo kammanirodhagāminipaṭipadā, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^415-1] . . . sammāsamādhi. Yato ca[^415-9] kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ kammaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kammānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kammānaṃ vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kammānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ kammanirodhaṃ[^415-10] pajānāti, evaṃ kammanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti kammanirodhaṃ. Kammaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ . . . pe[^415-1] . . . kamma-


<!-- PTS Page 416 -->
<a id="page-416"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 416]*

nirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

13. Dukkhaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ, dukkhassa {nidānasambhavo} veditabbo, dukkhassa vemattatā veditabbā, dukkhassa vipāko veditabbo, dukkhanirodho veditabbo, dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti kho pan'etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Jāti pi dukkhā, jarā pi dukkhā, vyādhi pi dukkhā, maraṇam pi dukkhaṃ, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā[^416-1] pi dukkhā, yam p'; icchaṃ na labhati tam pi dukkhaṃ, saṃkhittena pañcupādānakkhandhā dukkhā.

14. Katamo ca bhikkhave dukkhassa nidānasambhavo? Taṇhā bhikkhave dukkhassa nidānasambhavo. Katamā ca bhikkhave dukkhassa vemattatā? Atthi bhikkhave dukkhaṃ adhimattaṃ, atthi parittaṃ, atthi dandhavirāgi[^416-2], atthi khippavirāgi[^416-2]. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhassa vemattatā. Katamo ca bhikkhave dukkhassa vipāko? Idha bhikkhave ekacco yena dukkhena[^416-3] abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto socati kilamati paridevati urattāḷī[^416-4] kandati sammohaṃ āpajjati, yena vā pana dukkhena abhibhūto[^416-5] pariyādinnacitto bahiddhā pariyeṭṭhiṃ āpajjati ‘ko[^416-6] ekapadaṃ dvipadaṃ[^416-7] jānāti[^416-8] imassa dukkhassa nirodhāyā[^416-9]' ti? Sammohavepakkaṃ vāhaṃ bhikkhave dukkhaṃ vadāmi pariyeṭṭhivepakkaṃ vā. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhassa vipāko. Katamo ca bhikkhave dukkhanirodho? Taṇhānirodho[^416-10] bhikkhave dukkhanirodho[^416-11]. Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^416-12] . . . sammāsamādhi. Yato ca[^416-13] kho bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ dukkhaṃ pajānāti,


<!-- PTS Page 417 -->
<a id="page-417"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 417]*

evaṃ dukkhassa nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ dukkhassa vemattataṃ pajānāti, evaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ dukkhanirodhaṃ pajānāti, evaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ pajānāti, so imaṃ nibbedhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti dukkhanirodhaṃ. Dukkhaṃ bhikkhave veditabbaṃ, dukkhassa nidānasambhavo veditabbo, dukkhassa vemattatā veditabbā, dukkhassa vipāko veditabbo, dukkhanirodho[^417-1] veditabbo, dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā veditabbā ti: iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ. Ayaṃ kho so bhikkhave[^417-2] nibbedhikapariyāyo dhammapariyāyo ti. LXIV.

1. Cha yimāni[^417-3] bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalāni, yehi balehi samannāgato Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati[^417-4] brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti. Katamāni cha?

2. Idha bhikkhave Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ[^417-5] ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato jhānavimokkha-


<!-- PTS Page 418 -->
<a id="page-418"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 418]*

samādhisamāpattīnaṃ[^418-1] saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato . . . pe[^418-2] . . . idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ[^418-3] dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^418-4] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, yam[^418-5] pi bhikkhave Tathāgato anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ[^418-6] dve pi jātiyo . . . pe . . . iti sākāram sauddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena . . . pe[^418-7] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānāti, yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena . . . pe[^418-7] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^418-8] . . . abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati, yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^418-9] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.


<!-- PTS Page 419 -->
<a id="page-419"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 419]*

Imāni kho bhikkhave cha Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalāni, yehi balehi samannāgato Tathāgato[^419-1] āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

8. Tatra ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^419-2] upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā[^419-1] tesaṃ Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^419-3] pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

9. Tatra ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ[^419-4] ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā tesaṃ Tathāgato atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

10. Tatra ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ jhānavimokkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ[^419-5] saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa jhānavimokkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ[^419-5] saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā tesaṃ Tathāgato jhānavimokkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ[^419-6] saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

11. Tatra[^419-7] ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ pubbenivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa pubbenivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ[^419-8] viditaṃ, tathā tathā


<!-- PTS Page 420 -->
<a id="page-420"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 420]*

tesaṃ Tathāgato pubbenivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

12. Tatra ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ[^420-1] yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^420-2] upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ[^420-1] yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā tesaṃ Tathāgato sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ[^420-1] yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^420-2] pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

13. Tatra ce bhikkhave pare Tathāgataṃ āsavānaṃ khayā[^420-3] . . . yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^420-2] upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathā yathā bhikkhave Tathāgatassa āsavānaṃ khayā[^420-3] . . . yathābhūtuṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā tesaṃ Tathāgato āsavānaṃ khayā[^420-4] . . . yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇena[^420-2] pañhaṃ puṭṭho vyākaroti.

14. Tatra[^420-5] bhikkhave yam idaṃ[^420-6] ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Yam p'; idaṃ[^420-7] atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Yam p'; idaṃ[^420-8] jhānavimokkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Yam p'; idaṃ[^420-8] pubbenivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Yam p'; idaṃ sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Yam p'; idaṃ āsavānaṃ khayā[^420-9] . . . yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tam pi samāhitassa vadāmi, no asamāhitassa. Iti[^420-10] kho bhikkhave samādhi maggo, asamādhi kummaggo ti. Mahāvaggo chaṭṭho[^420-11].


<!-- PTS Page 421 -->
<a id="page-421"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 421]*

Tass'; uddānaṃ[^421-1]: Soṇo Phagguṇo[^421-2] chaḷābhijātiyo āsavā dārukammahatthimajjhaṃ[^421-3] Passa[^421-4]-Citta[^421-5] pārāyanaṃ[^421-6] udakaṃ[^421-7] nibbedhika-sīhanādena[^421-8] vaggo[^421-9] ti[^421-10]. LXV.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

2. Assaddhiyaṃ[^421-11], ahirikaṃ, anottappaṃ, kosajjaṃ, muṭṭhasaccaṃ, duppaññataṃ[^421-12]. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

4. Assaddhiyaṃ[^421-11], ahirikaṃ, anottappaṃ, kosajjaṃ, muṭṭhasaccaṃ, duppaññataṃ[^421-12]. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātun ti. LXVI.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

2. Thīnaṃ, middhaṃ, uddhaccaṃ, kukkuccaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ[^421-11], pamādaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 422 -->
<a id="page-422"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 422]*

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ[^422-1]. Katame cha?

4. Thīnaṃ, middhaṃ, uddhaccaṃ, kukkuccaṃ, assaddhiyaṃ[^422-2], pamādaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātun ti. LXVII.

1. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu pāpamitto pāpasahāyo pāpasampavaṅko[^422-3] pāpamitte[^422-4] sevamāno bhajamāno payirupāsamāno tesañ ca diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpajjamāno abhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūressatī[^422-5] ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Abhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūretvā[^422-6] sekhaṃ[^422-7] dhammaṃ paripūressatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sekhaṃ[^422-7] dhammaṃ aparipūretvā sīlāni paripūressatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sīlāni aparipūretvā kāmarāgaṃ vā rūparāgaṃ vā arūparāgaṃ vā pajahissatī ti n'etaṃ thānaṃ vijjati.

2. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko[^422-3] kalyāṇamitte[^422-8] sevamāno bhajamāno payirupāsamāno tesañ ca diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpajjamāno abhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūressatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Abhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūretvā sekhaṃ[^422-7] dhammaṃ paripūressatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sekhaṃ[^422-7] dhammaṃ paripūretvā sīlāni paripūressatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sīlāni paripūretvā kāmarāgaṃ vā rūparāgaṃ vā arūparāgaṃ vā pajahissatī ti ṭhānaṃ etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXVIII.

1. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu saṃgaṇikārāmo saṃgaṇikārato[^422-9] saṃgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyutto gaṇārāmo gaṇarato gaṇārāmataṃ anuyutto eko paviveke[^422-10] abhiramissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Eko paviveke[^422-10] anabhiramanto


<!-- PTS Page 423 -->
<a id="page-423"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 423]*

cittassa nimittaṃ gahessatī[^423-1] ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Cittassa nimittaṃ agaṇhanto sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūressatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sammādiṭṭhiṃ aparipūretvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūressatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sammāsamādhiṃ aparipūretvā saṃyojanāni pajahissatī[^423-2] ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Saṃyojanāni appahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati.

2. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu na saṃgaṇikārāmo na saṃgaṇikārato[^423-3] na saṃgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyutto na gaṇārāmo na gaṇarato na gaṇārāmataṃ anuyutto eko paviveke[^423-4] abhiramissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Eko paviveke[^423-4] abhiramanto cittassa nimittaṃ gahessatī[^423-5] ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Cittassa nimittaṃ gaṇhanto sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūressatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūretvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūressatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sammāsamādhiṃ paripūretvā saṃyojanāni pajahissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Saṃyojanāni pahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXIX.

1. Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho sā devatā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘cha yime[^423-6] bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, sovacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā. Ime kho bhante cha dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī' ti. Idam avoca sā devatā. Samanuñño Satthā ahosi. Atha kho sā devatā ‘samanuñño me Satthā'; ti Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi.


<!-- PTS Page 424 -->
<a id="page-424"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 424]*

2. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi ‘imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave sā devatā maṃ etad avoca: Cha yime[^424-1] bhante dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame cha? Satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, sovacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā. Ime kho bhante cha dhammā bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave sā devatā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyī'; ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā etad avoca:-- Imassa kho ahaṃ bhante Bhagavatā[^424-2] saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi. Idha bhante bhikkhu attanā[^424-3] ca[^424-3] satthugāravo hoti satthugāravatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na satthugāravā, te ca satthugāravatāya samādapeti, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū satthugāravā, tesañ ca[^424-4] vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Attanā ca[^424-5] dhammagāravo hoti . . . pe[^424-6] . . . saṅghagāravo hoti . . . sikkhāgāravo hoti . . . suvaco hoti . . .[^424-7] kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇamittatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā[^424-8], te ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapeti, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā, tesañ ca vaññaṃ bhaṇati[^424-9] bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Imassa kho ahaṃ[^424-10] bhante Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmī[^424-11] ti. ‘Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta, sādhu kho tvaṃ Sāriputta imassa mayā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāsi[^424-12]. Idha Sāriputta bhikkhu attanā ca satthugāravo


<!-- PTS Page 425 -->
<a id="page-425"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 425]*

hoti satthugāravatāya ca[^425-1] vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na[^425-2] satthugāravā, te[^425-3] ca satthugāravatāya samādapeti, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū satthugāravā, tesañ ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati[^425-4] bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Attanā ca dhammagāravo hoti . . . pe[^425-5] . . . saṅghagāravo hoti . . . sikkhāgāravo hoti . . . suvaco hoti . . .[^425-6] kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇamittatāya ca[^425-1] vaṇṇavādī, ye c'; aññe bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā[^425-7], te ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapeti, ye[^425-8] c'; aññe[^425-8] bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā, tesañ ca[^425-1] vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālena. Imassa kho Sāriputta mayā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena attho daṭṭhabbo'; ti. LXX.

1. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu na santena samādhinā na paṇītena na paṭippassaddhiladdhena[^425-9] na ekodibhāvādhigatena anekavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ paccanubhavissati[^425-10]: eko pi hutvā bahudhā bhavissati, bahudhā pi hutvā eko bhavissati . . . pe[^425-11] . . . yāva brahmalokā pi kāyena 'va saṃvattissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; dibbāya sotadhātuyā visuddhāya atikkantamānusikāya[^425-12] ubho sadde suṇissati dibbe ca mānuse[^425-13] ca ye dūre santike cā ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānissati: sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittan ti pajānissati . . . pe[^425-11] . . . avimuttaṃ[^425-14] vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittan ti pajānissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^425-15]; anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarissati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^425-16] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 426 -->
<a id="page-426"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 426]*

anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^426-1] satte passissati . . . pe[^426-2] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati; āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^426-3] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati.

2. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu santena samādhinā paṇītena paṭippassaddhiladdhena ekodibhāvādhigatena anekavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ paccanubhavissati . . . pe[^426-4] . . . yāva brahmalokā pi kāyena 'va {saṃvattissatī} ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati; dibbāya sotadhātuyā visuddhāya atikkantamānusikāya[^426-5] ubho sadde suṇissati[^426-6] dibbe ca mānuse[^426-7] ca ye dūre santike cā ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati; parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānissati: sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittan ti pajānissati . . . pe[^426-8] . . . avimuttaṃ[^426-9] vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittan ti pajānissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati[^426-10]; anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarissati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^426-11] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati; dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^426-1] satte passissati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^426-12] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ . . . pe[^426-11] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXXI.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo tatra tatr'eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatane. Katamehi chahi?


<!-- PTS Page 427 -->
<a id="page-427"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 427]*

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘ime hānabhāgiyā dhammā' ti yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, ‘ime ṭhitibhāgiyā[^427-1] dhammā' ti yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, ‘ime visesabhāgiyā dhammā' ti yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti, ‘ime nibbedhabhāgiyā dhammā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti asakkaccakārī ca hoti asappāyakārī ca. Imehi kho[^427-2] bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo tatra tatr'eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatane[^427-3].

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo tatra tatr'eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatane. Katamehi chahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘ime hānabhāgiyā dhammā' ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ime ṭhitibhāgiyā[^427-1] dhammā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ime visesabhāgiyā dhammā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ime nibbedhabhāgiyā dhammā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti sakkaccakārī ca hoti sappāyakārī ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu tatra tatr'eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatane ti. LXXII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu[^427-4] abhabbo samādhismiṃ[^427-5] balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na samādhissa samāpattikusalo hoti, na samādhissa ṭhitikusalo hoti, na samādhissa[^427-6] vuṭṭhānakusalo hoti asakkaccakārī ca hoti asātaccakārī ca asappāyakārī ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo samādhismiṃ[^427-7] balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo samādhismiṃ[^427-7] balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ. Katamehi chahi?


<!-- PTS Page 428 -->
<a id="page-428"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 428]*

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu samādhissa samāpattikusalo hoti, samādhissa ṭhitikusalo hoti, samādhissa[^428-1] vuṭṭhānakusalo hoti sakkaccakārī ca hoti sātaccakārī ca sappāyakārī ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo samādhismiṃ balataṃ pāpuṇitun ti. LXXIII.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ. Katame cha?

2. Kāmacchandaṃ, vyāpādaṃ, thīnamiddhaṃ, uddhaccakukkuccaṃ, vicikicchaṃ, kāmesu kho pan'assa ādīnavo na yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭho[^428-3] hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ.

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ. Katame cha?

4. Kāmacchandaṃ, vyāpādaṃ, thīnamiddhaṃ, uddhaccakukkuccaṃ, vicikicchaṃ, kāmesu kho pan'assa[^428-4] ādīnavo yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭho hoti. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharitun ti. LXXIV.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ. Katame cha?

2. Kāmavitakkaṃ, vyāpādavitakkaṃ, vihiṃsāvitakkaṃ, kāmasaññaṃ, vyāpādasaññaṃ, vihiṃsāsaññaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ.

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^428-2] jhānaṃ[^428-2] upasampajja viharituṃ. Katame cha?

4. Kāmavitakkaṃ, vyāpādavitakkaṃ, vihiṃsāvitakkaṃ, kāmasaññaṃ, vyāpādasaññaṃ, vihiṃsāsaññaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 429 -->
<a id="page-429"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 429]*

Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo paṭhamaṃ[^429-1] jhānaṃ[^429-1] upasampajja viharitun ti. Devatāvaggo sattamo[^429-2]. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^429-3]: Anāgāmi arahaṃ ca[^429-4] mittā[^429-5] devatā[^429-6] so[^429-4] vata[^429-4] Sakkhi[^429-7] balataṃ[^429-7] jhānaṃ ca[^429-4] apare duve pi[^429-4] cā[^429-4] ti. LXXV.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ sa-upāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā. Katamehi chahi?

2. Kāmavitakkena, vyāpādavitakkena, vihiṃsāvitakkena, kāmasaññāya, vyāpādasaññāya, vihiṃsāsaññāya. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ sa-upāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā.

4. Nekkhammavitakkena[^429-8], avyāpādavitakkena, avihiṃsāvitakkena, nekkhammasaññāya[^429-9], avyāpādasaññāya, avihiṃsāsaññāya. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 430 -->
<a id="page-430"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 430]*

LXXVI.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

2. Mānaṃ, omānaṃ, atimānaṃ, adhimānaṃ[^430-1], thambhaṃ[^430-2], atinipātaṃ[^430-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

4. Mānaṃ, omānaṃ, atimānaṃ, adhimānaṃ[^430-1], thambhaṃ[^430-2], atinipātaṃ[^430-4]. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātun ti. LXXVII.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo uttarimanussadhammā[^430-5] alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

2. Muṭṭhasaccaṃ, asampajaññaṃ, indriyesu aguttadvārataṃ, bhojane amattaññutaṃ, kuhanaṃ, lapanaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo uttarimanussadhammā[^430-5] alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Cha bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo uttarimanussadhammā[^430-5] alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

4. Muṭṭhasaccaṃ, asampajaññaṃ, indriyesu aguttadvārataṃ, bhojane amattaññutaṃ, kuhanaṃ, lapanaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo uttarimanussadhammā[^430-5] alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ sacchikātun ti.


<!-- PTS Page 431 -->
<a id="page-431"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 431]*

LXXVIII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme sukhasomanassabahulo viharati, yoni c'assa āraddhā[^431-1] hoti āsavānaṃ khayāya[^431-2]. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammārāmo hoti, bhāvanārāmo hoti, pahānārāmo[^431-3] hoti[^431-3], pavivekārāmo hoti, avyāpajjhārāmo hoti, nippapañcārāmo[^431-4] hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu diṭṭh'eva dhamme sukhasomanassabahulo viharati, yoni c'assa āraddhā[^431-1] hoti āsavānaṃ khayāyā ti. LXXIX.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātikātuṃ[^431-5]. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na āyakusalo hoti, na apāyakusalo hoti, na upāyakusalo hoti, anadhigatānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ adhigamāya na[^431-6] chandaṃ janeti, adhigate kusale dhamme na sārakkhati[^431-7], sātaccakiriyāya[^431-8] na sampādeti. Imehi kho[^431-6] bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātikātuṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātikātuṃ. Katamehi chahi? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu āyakusalo ca hoti, apāyakusalo ca hoti[^431-9], upāyakusalo ca[^431-10] hoti[^431-9], anadhigatānaṃ kusalānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 432 -->
<a id="page-432"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 432]*

dhammānaṃ adhigamāya chandaṃ janeti, adhigate kusale dhamme sārakkhati[^432-1], sātaccakiriyāya[^432-2] sampādeti. Imehi kho[^432-3] bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ adhigataṃ[^432-4] vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātikātun ti. LXXX.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'eva mahantattaṃ[^432-5] vepullattaṃ[^432-6] pāpuṇāti dhammesu. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ālokabahulo ca hoti, yogabahulo ca hoti[^432-7], vedabahulo ca[^432-8] hoti, asantuṭṭhibahulo[^432-9] ca, anikkhittadhuro ca kusalesu dhammesu, uttariñ[^432-10] ca patāreti[^432-11]. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu na cirass'eva mahantattaṃ[^432-12] vepullattaṃ[^432-6] pāpuṇāti dhammesū ti. LXXXI.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi chahi?

2. Pāṇātipātī[^432-13] hoti, adinnādāyī hoti, kāmesu micchācārī hoti, musāvādī hoti, pāpiccho ca, micchādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi chahi?

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, appiccho[^432-14] ca, sammādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti.


<!-- PTS Page 433 -->
<a id="page-433"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 433]*

LXXXII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi chahi?

2. Musāvādī[^433-1] hoti, pisuṇāvāco[^433-2] hoti, pharusāvāco[^433-3] hoti, samphappalāpī[^433-4] hoti, luddho[^433-5] ca, pagabbho[^433-6] ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi chahi?

4. Musāvādā[^433-7] paṭivirato hoti, pisuṇāvācāya[^433-8] paṭivirato hoti, pharusāvācāya[^433-9] paṭivirato hoti, samphappalāpā[^433-10] paṭivirato hoti, aluddho[^433-11] ca, appagabbho[^433-12] ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. LXXXIII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo aggaṃ[^433-13] dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu assaddho hoti, ahiriko hoti, anottāpī hoti, kusīto[^433-14] hoti[^433-14], duppañño hoti, kāye ca jīvite ca sāpekho[^433-15] hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo aggaṃ[^433-16] dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo aggaṃ[^433-16] dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katamehi chahi?


<!-- PTS Page 434 -->
<a id="page-434"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 434]*

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho hoti, hirīmā hoti, ottāpī hoti, āraddhaviriyo hoti, paññavā hoti, kāye ca jīvite ca anapekho hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo aggaṃ[^434-1] dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātun ti. LXXXIV.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, hāni yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu, no vuddhi. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu mahiccho hoti vighātavā asantuṭṭho itaritaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena[^434-2], assaddho hoti, dussīlo hoti, kusīto hoti, muṭṭhassati hoti, duppañño hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, hāni yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu, no vuddhi.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu, no parihāni. Katamehi chahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na mahiccho hoti na[^434-3] vighātavā[^434-3] santuṭṭho itaritaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena[^434-2], saddho hoti, sīlavā hoti, āraddhaviriyo hoti, satimā hoti, paññavā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu, no parihānī ti. Arahattavaggo[^434-4] aṭṭhamo[^434-5]. Tass'; uddānaṃ[^434-6]:


<!-- PTS Page 435 -->
<a id="page-435"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 435]*

Dukkhaṃ arahattaṃ[^435-1] uttariñ[^435-1] ca sukhaṃ adhigamena ca Mahantattaṃ[^435-2] dvayaniraye[^435-3] aggadhammañ ca rattiyo[^435-4] ti. LXXXV.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ niggahetabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ na niggaṇhāti; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ paggahetabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ na paggaṇhāti; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ sampahaṃsitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ na sampahaṃsati; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ ajjhupekkhitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittam na ajjhupekkhati; hīnādhimuttiko ca hoti; sakkāyābhirato ca[^435-5]. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu abhabbo anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katamehi chahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ niggahetabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ niggaṇhāti; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ paggahetabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ paggaṇhāti; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ sampahaṃsitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ sampahaṃsati; yasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ ajjhupekkhitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ ajjhupekkhati; paṇītādhimuttiko ca hoti; nibbānābhirato ca[^435-5]. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhabbo anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātun ti. LXXXVI.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato[^435-6] suṇanto pi saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ[^435-7]. Katamehi chahi?


<!-- PTS Page 436 -->
<a id="page-436"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 436]*

2. Kammāvaraṇatāya samannāgato hoti, kilesāvaraṇatāya[^436-1] samannāgato hoti, vipākāvaraṇatāya samannāgato hoti, assaddho ca hoti, acchandiko[^436-2] ca, duppañño ca[^436-3]. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato[^436-4] suṇanto pi[^436-5] saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato[^436-6] suṇanto pi[^436-7] saddhammaṃ bhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ. Katamehi chahi?

4. Na kammāvaraṇatāya samannāgato hoti, na kilesāvaraṇatāya samannāgato hoti, na vipākāvaraṇatāya samannāgato hoti, saddho ca hoti, chandiko[^436-8] ca, paññavā ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato[^436-4] suṇanto pi[^436-9] saddhammaṃ bhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattan ti. LXXXVII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto pi saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Mātā jīvitā voropitā hoti, pitā jīvitā voropito[^436-10] hoti, arahā[^436-11] jīvitā voropito[^436-12] hoti, Tathāgatassa duṭṭhena cittena lohitaṃ uppāditaṃ hoti, saṅgho bhinno hoti, duppañño hoti jaḷo eḷamūgo. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato pi saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ.

4. Na[^437-1] mātā jīvitā voropitā hoti, na pitā jīvitā voropito[^437-2] hoti, na arahā[^437-3] jīvitā voropito[^437-4] hoti, na Tathāgatassa duṭṭhena cittena lohitaṃ uppāditaṃ hoti, na saṅgho bhinno hoti, paññavā hoti ajaḷo aneḷamūgo. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto pi[^437-5] saddhammaṃ bhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattan ti. LXXXVIII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto pi saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ[^437-6] okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Tathāgatappavedite[^437-7] dhammavinaye desiyamāne na sussūsati, na sotaṃ odahati, na aññācittaṃ[^437-8] upaṭṭhapeti[^437-9], anatthaṃ gaṇhāti, atthaṃ riñcati, ananulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi[^437-6] dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto pi saddhammaṃ abhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ.

3. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto pi[^437-10] saddhammaṃ bhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu sammattaṃ. Katamehi chahi?

4. Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye desiyamāne sussūsati, sotaṃ odahati, aññācittaṃ upaṭṭhapeti[^437-11], atthaṃ gaṇhāti, anatthaṃ riñcati, anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato suṇanto[^437-12] pi saddhammaṃ bhabbo niyāmaṃ okkamituṃ kusalesu dhammesu samattan ti.

LXXXIX.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha[^438-1]?

2. Sakkāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ[^438-1] rāgaṃ[^438-1], apāyagamaniyaṃ dosaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ mohaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ.

3. Cha[^438-2] bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame cha?

4. Sakkāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ rāgaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ dosaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ mohaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya bhabbo diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātun ti. XC.

1. Cha yime bhikkhave dhammā diṭṭhisampannassa puggalassa pahīnā. Katame cha?

2. Sakkāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāso, apāyagamaniyo rāgo, apāyagamaniyo doso, apāyagamaniyo moho. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhammā diṭṭhisampannassa puggalassa pahīnā ti. XCI.

1. Cha[^438-3] bhikkhave dhamme abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo uppādetuṃ. Katame cha?

2. Sakkāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ rāgaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ dosaṃ, apāyagamaniyaṃ mohaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo uppādetun ti. XCII.

1. Cha yimāni[^438-4] bhikkhave abhabbaṭṭhānāni. Katamāni cha?


<!-- PTS Page 439 -->
<a id="page-439"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 439]*

2. Abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo Satthari agāravo viharituṃ appatisso[^439-1], abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo dhamme agāravo viharituṃ appatisso, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo saṅghe agāravo viharituṃ appatisso, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo sikkhāya agāravo viharituṃ appatisso, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo anāgamaniyaṃ[^439-2] vatthuṃ paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo aṭṭhamaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattetuṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave abhabbaṭṭhānānī ti. XCIII.

1. Cha yimāni bhikkhave abhabbaṭṭhānāni. Katamāni cha?

2. Abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo kañci[^439-3] saṅkhāraṃ niccato upagantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo kañci[^439-3] saṅkhāraṃ sukhato upagantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo kañci[^439-3] dhammaṃ attato upagantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo anantariyakammaṃ[^439-4] kātuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo kotūhalamaṅgalena[^439-5] suddhiṃ paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo ito bahiddhā dakkhiṇeyyaṃ gavesituṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī ti. XCIV.

1. Cha yimāni bhikkhave abhabbaṭṭhānāni. Katamāni cha?

2. Abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo mātaraṃ jīvitā voropetuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo pitaraṃ jīvitā voropetuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo arahantaṃ jīvitā voropetuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo Tathāgatassa duṭṭhena cittena lohitaṃ uppādetuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo saṅghaṃ bhindituṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo aññaṃ satthāraṃ uddisituṃ[^439-6]. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī ti.

<a id="page-440"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 440]*

XCV.

1. Cha yimāni[^440-1] bhikkhave abhabbaṭṭhānāni. Katamāni cha[^440-2]?

2. Abhabbo[^440-3] diṭṭhisampanno puggalo sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ[^440-4] paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo paraṃkataṃ[^440-5] sukhadukkhaṃ paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo sayaṃkatañ ca paraṃkatañ[^440-5] ca sukhadukkhaṃ[^440-6] paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo asayaṃkāraṃ[^440-7] adhiccasamuppannaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo aparaṃkāraṃ adhiccasamuppannaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ paccāgantuṃ, abhabbo diṭṭhisampanno puggalo asayaṃkārañ ca aparaṃkārañ ca adhiccasamuppannaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ paccāgantuṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā[^440-8] hi 'ssa bhikkhave diṭṭhisampannassa puggalassa[^440-9] hetu[^440-10] ca[^440-11] sudiṭṭho[^440-12], hetusamuppannā[^440-13] ca dhammā. Imāni kho bhikkhave cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī ti. Sītivaggo[^440-14] navamo[^440-15]. Tass[^440-16]'; uddānaṃ: Sīti[^440-9] bhabbo[^440-17] āvaraṇatā[^440-18] sussūsā[^440-19] pavuccati[^440-20] Pahātabbaṃ pahīnā me uppādetabbaṃ[^440-21] Satthari Kañci saṅkhāraṃ[^440-22] mātari[^440-22] sayaṃkatena[^440-23] vaggo[^440-24] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 441 -->
<a id="page-441"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 441]*

XCVI.

1. Channaṃ bhikkhave pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmim. Katamesaṃ channaṃ?

2. Tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmiṃ, Tathāgatappaveditassa dhammavinayassa desetā[^441-1] puggalo dullabho lokasmiṃ, ariyāyatane paccājāti[^441-2] dullabhā lokasmiṃ, indriyānaṃ avekallatā dullabhā lokasmiṃ, ajaḷatā aneḷamūgatā dullabhā lokasmiṃ, kusaladhammacchando[^441-3] dullabho lokasmiṃ. Imesam kho bhikkhave channaṃ pātubhāvo dullabho lokasmin ti. XCVII.

1. Cha yime bhikkhave ānisaṃsā sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya. Katame cha?

2. Saddhammaniyato[^441-4] hoti, aparihānadhammo hoti, pariyantakatassa dukkhaṃ na[^441-5] hoti, asādhāraṇena[^441-6] ñāṇena[^441-6] samannāgato hoti, hetu c'assa[^441-7] sudiṭṭho[^441-8], hetusamuppannā[^441-9] ca dhammā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha ānisaṃsā sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāyā ti. XCVIII.

1. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu kañci[^441-10] saṅkhāraṃ niccato samanupassanto[^441-11] anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato bhavissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Anulomikāya khantiyā asamannāgato[^441-12] sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamissatī[^441-13] ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sammattaniyāmaṃ anokkamamāno sotāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ[^441-14] vā[^441-14] arahattaṃ[^441-15] vā sacchikarissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 442 -->
<a id="page-442"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 442]*

2. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu sabbasaṅkhāraṃ[^442-1] aniccato samanupassanto anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato bhavissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Anulomikāya khantiyā[^442-2] samannāgato sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamamāno sotāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ[^442-3] vā sacchikarissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. XCIX. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu kañci[^442-4] saṅkhāraṃ sukhato samanupassanto . . . pe[^442-5] . . . sabbasaṅkhāraṃ[^442-6] dukkhato samanupassanto . . . pe[^442-7] . . . C. . . .8 kañci[^442-9] dhammaṃ attato samanupassanto . . . pe[^442-10] . . . sabbadhammaṃ[^442-11] anattato samanupassanto . . . pe[^442-7] . . . CI.

1. So[^442-12] vata[^442-12] bhikkhave[^442-12] bhikkhu[^442-12] nibbānaṃ dukkhato samanupassanto anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato bhavissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Anulomikāya khantiyā asamannāgato[^442-13] sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamissatī ti n'etaṃ[^442-14] ṭhānaṃ vijjati. Sammattaniyāmaṃ anokkamamāno sotāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ[^442-3] vā sacchikarissatī ti n'etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati.

2. So vata bhikkhave bhikkhu nibbānaṃ sukhato samanupassanto anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato bhavissatī


<!-- PTS Page 443 -->
<a id="page-443"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 443]*

ti ṭhānam[^443-1] etaṃ vijjati. Anulomikāya khantiyā samannāgato sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Sammattaniyāmaṃ okkamamāno sotāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ[^443-2] vā[^443-2] arahattaṃ[^443-3] vā sacchikarissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. CII.

1. Cha[^443-4] bhikkhave ānisaṃse sampassamānena[^443-5] alam eva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāresu anodhiṃ karitvā aniccasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetuṃ[^443-6]. Katame cha?

2. Sabbasaṅkhārā ca me anavaṭṭhitato[^443-7] khāyissanti[^443-8], sabbaloke ca me mano nābhiramissati[^443-9], sabbalokā ca me mano vuṭṭhahissati[^443-10], nibbānapoṇañ[^443-11] ca me mānasaṃ[^443-12] bhavissati, saṃyojanā ca me pahānaṃ gacchanti, paramena ca sāmaññena samannāgato bhavissāmī[^443-13] ti. Ime kho bhikkhave cha ānisaṃse sampassamānena alam eva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāresu anodhiṃ karitvā aniccasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetun[^443-14] ti. CIII.

1. Cha bhikkhave ānisaṃse sampassamānena alam eva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāresu anodhiṃ karitvā dukkhasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetuṃ[^443-6]. Katame cha?

2. Sabbasaṅkhāresu ca me nibbānasaññā[^443-15] paccupaṭṭhitā bhavissati[^443-16] seyyathā pi ukkhittāsike vadhake, sabbalokā ca me mano vuṭṭhahissati, nibbāne ca santadassāvī[^443-17] bhavissāmi, anusayā[^443-18] ca me samugghātaṃ[^443-19] gacchanti, kiccakārī ca bhavissāmi, Satthā ca me pariciṇṇo bhavissati mettāvatāyā[^443-20] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 444 -->
<a id="page-444"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 444]*

Ime kho bhikkhave cha ānisaṃse sampassamānena alam eva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāresu anodhiṃ karitvā dukkhasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetun[^444-1] ti. CIV.

1. Cha bhikkhave ānisaṃse sampassamānena alam eva bhikkhunā sabbadhammesu anodhiṃ karitvā anattasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetuṃ[^444-2]. Katame cha?

2. Sabbaloke ca atammayo[^444-3] bhavissāmi, ahaṃkārā[^444-4] ca me uparujjhissanti, mamaṃkārā[^444-5] ca me uparujjhissanti, asādhāraṇena[^444-6] ca ñāṇena[^444-7] samannāgato bhavissāmi, hetu[^444-8] ca me sudiṭṭho bhavissati, hetusamuppannā ca dhammā. Ime kho bhikkhave cha ānisaṃse sampassamānena alam eva bhikkhunā sabbadhammesu anodhiṃ karitvā anattasaññaṃ upaṭṭhāpetun[^444-9] ti. CV.

1. Tayo[^444-10] 'me bhikkhave bhavā pahātabbā[^444-11], tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitabbaṃ. Katame tayo bhavā pahātabbā[^444-11]?

2. Kāmabhavo, rūpabhavo, arūpabhavo. Ime tayo bhavā pahātabbā[^444-11]. Katamāsu tīsu sikkhitabbam?

3. Adhisīlasikkhāya, adhicittasikkhāya, adhipaññāsikkhāya. Imāsu tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitabbaṃ.

4. Yato kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno ime tayo bhavā pahīnā honti, imāsu ca[^444-12] tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitasikkho hoti: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu acchejji[^444-13] taṇhaṃ, vivattayi[^444-14] saṃyojanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antam akāsi dukkhassā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 445 -->
<a id="page-445"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 445]*

CVI.

1. Tisso imā bhikkhave taṇhā pahātabbā tayo ca mānā. Katamā tisso taṇhā pahātabbā?

2. Kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā. Imā tisso taṇhā pahātabbā. Katame tayo mānā pahātabbā[^445-1]?

3. Māno, omāno, atimāno. Ime tayo mānā pahātabbā[^445-2].

4. Yato kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno imā tisso taṇhā pahīnā honti ime ca tayo mānā: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu acchejji[^445-3] taṇhaṃ, vivattayi[^445-4] saṃyojanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antam akāsi dukkhassā ti. Ānisaṃsavaggo[^445-5] dasamo[^445-6]. Tass'[^445-7] uddānaṃ: Pātubhāvo ānisaṃso[^445-8] aniccaṃ[^445-9] dukkham[^445-10] anattato Nibbānaṃ[^445-11] tayo[^445-12] anodhi[^445-13] saṃvuttā[^445-13] bhavataṇhāya[^445-14] te[^445-15] dasā[^445-15] ti[^445-16]. [PAṆṆĀSAKĀSAṄGAHITO-VAGGO.] CVII.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Rāgo, doso, moho. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?


<!-- PTS Page 446 -->
<a id="page-446"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 446]*

3. Rāgassa pahānāya asubhā bhāvetabbā, dosassa pahānāya mettā bhāvetabbā, mohassa pahānāya paññā bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CVIII.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Kāyaduccaritaṃ, vacīduccaritaṃ, manoduccaritaṃ. Ime kho[^446-1] bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Kāyaduccaritassa pahānāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ, vacīduccaritassa pahānāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ, manoduccaritassa pahānāya manosucaritaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ. Imesaṃ kho[^446-1] bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CIX.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Kāmavitakko, vyāpādavitakko, vihiṃsāvitakko. Ime kho[^446-1] bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Kāmavitakkassa pahānāya nekkhammavitakko[^446-2] bhāvetabbo, vyāpādavitakkassa pahānāya avyāpādavitakko bhāvetabbo, vihiṃsāvitakkassa pahānāya avihiṃsāvitakko bhāvetabbo. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime[^446-3] tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^446-3]. CX.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Kāmasaññā, vyāpādasaññā, vihiṃsāsaññā.


<!-- PTS Page 447 -->
<a id="page-447"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 447]*

Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya[^447-1] tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Kāmasaññāya[^447-2] pahānāya nekkhammasaññā[^447-3] bhāvetabbā, vyāpādasaññāya[^447-2] pahānāya avyāpādasaññā bhāvetabbā, vihiṃsāsaññāya[^447-2] pahānāya avihiṃsāsaññā bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXI.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Kāmadhātu, vyāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo[^447-4] dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Kāmadhātuyā pahānāya nekkhammadhātu[^447-3] bhāvetabbā, vyāpādadhātuyā pahānāya avyāpādadhātu bhāvetabbā, vihiṃsādhātuyā pahāṇāya avihiṃsādhātu bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXII.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Assādadiṭṭhi, attānudiṭṭhi, micchādiṭṭhi. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Assādadiṭṭhiyā pahānāya aniccasaññā bhāvetabbā, attānudiṭṭhiyā pahānāya anattasaññā bhāvetabbā, micchādiṭṭhiyā pahānāya sammādiṭṭhi bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 448 -->
<a id="page-448"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 448]*

CXIII.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Arati, vihiṃsā[^448-1], adhammacariyā. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya[^448-2] tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Aratiyā pahānāya muditā bhāvetabbā, vihiṃsāya[^448-3] pahānāya avihiṃsā bhāvetabbā, adhammacariyāya pahānāya dhammacariyā[^448-4] bhāvetabbā[^448-4]. Imesam kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXIV.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Asantuṭṭhitā, asampajaññaṃ[^448-5], mahicchatā. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya[^448-6] tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Asantuṭṭhitāya pahānāya santuṭṭhitā bhāvetabbā, asampajaññassa pahānāya sampajaññaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ, mahicchatāya pahānāya appicchatā bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXV.

1. Tayo 'me[^448-7] bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Dovacassatā, pāpamittatā, cetaso vikkhepo[^448-8]. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

##### *[PTS Page 449]*

3. Dovacassatāya pahānāya sovacassatā bhāvetabbā, pāpamittatāya pahānāya kalyāṇamittatā bhāvetabbā, cetaso vikkhepassa pahānāya ānāpāṇassati[^449-1] bhāvetabbā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXVI.

1. Tayo 'me bhikkhave dhammā. Katame tayo?

2. Uddhaccaṃ, asaṃvaro, pamādo. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhammā. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame tayo?

3. Uddhaccassa pahānāya samatho[^449-2] bhāvetabbo, asaṃvarassa pahānāya saṃvaro bhāvetabbo, pamādassa pahānāya appamādo bhāvetabbo. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya ime tayo dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. Tikavaggo[^449-3] ekādasamo[^449-4]. Tass[^449-5]'; uddānaṃ: Rāga-duccarita-vitakka[^449-6]-saññā-dhātū ti vuccati[^449-7]. Assāda[^449-8]-arati-tuṭṭhi-dovaca-uddhaccena vaggo ti. CXVII.

1. Cha bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo kāye kāyānupassī viharituṃ. Katame cha?

2. Kammārāmataṃ, bhassārāmataṃ, niddārāmataṃ, saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ, indriyesu aguttadvārataṃ, bhojane amattaññutaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme appahāya abhabbo kāye kāyānupassī viharituṃ.

2. Kammārāmataṃ, bhassārāmataṃ, niddārāmataṃ, saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ, indriyesu aguttadvārataṃ, bhojane amattaññutaṃ . . . Ime kho bhikkhave cha dhamme pahāya[^450-10] bhabbo[^450-10] ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu dhammānupassī viharitun ti. CXIX.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato Tapusso gahapati Tathāgate niṭṭhaṃ gato amataddaso amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyati. Katamehi chahi?

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato Bhalliko gahapati . . .[^451-1] Sudatto gahapati Anāthapiṇḍiko . . . Citto gahapati Macchikāsaṇḍiko[^451-2] . . . Hatthako Āḷavako[^451-3] . . . Tavakaṇṇiko[^451-7] gahapati . . . Pūraṇo gahapati . . . Isidatto gahapati . . . Sandhāno[^451-8] gahapati . . . Vijayo[^451-9] gahapati . . . Vajjiyamahito[^451-10] gahapati . . . Meṇḍako[^451-11] gahapati . . . Vāseṭṭho[^451-12] upāsako, Ariṭṭho upāsako, Sāraggo[^451-13] upāsako Tathāgate niṭṭhaṃ gato amataddaso amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyati. Katamehi chahi?

2. Buddhe aveccappasādena, dhamme aveccappasādena, saṅghe aveccappasādena, ariyena sīlena, ariyena ñāṇena, ariyāya vimuttiyā. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato Sāraggo[^451-14] upāsako Tathāgate niṭṭhaṃ gato amataddaso amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyatī ti. CXXI.

1. Rāgassa[^451-15] bhikkhave abhiññāya cha dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame cha?


<!-- PTS Page 452 -->
<a id="page-452"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 452]*

2. Dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime cha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXXII.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya cha dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame cha?

2. Buddhānussati, dhammānussati[^452-1], saṅghānussati, sīlānussati[^452-2], cāgānussati, devatānussati. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime cha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXXIII.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya cha dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame cha?

2. Aniccasasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā, pahānasaññā[^452-1], virāgasaññā, nirodhasaññā. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime cha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. CXXIV.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave[^452-3] pariññāya . . . pe[^452-4] . . . parikkhayāya . . .[^452-5] pahānāya khayāya vayāya virāgāya nirodhāya cāgāya paṭinissaggāya cha[^452-6] dhammā bhāvetabbā . . .[^452-7]

2. Dosassa . . .[^452-5] mohassa kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa palāsassa[^452-8] issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa[^452-9] thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa abhiññāya . . .[^452-5] pariññāya parikkhayāya pahānāya[^452-1] khayāya[^452-1] vayāya virāgāya nirodhāya cāgāya paṭinissaggāya . . . pe[^452-4] . . . ime[^452-10] cha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā. Attamanā te bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinandun ti. Chakkanipāto[^452-11] samatto[^452-11].
